### `src/ccis_sim/decision/ahp.py`

*283 lines. Analytic Hierarchy Process (AHP) multi-criteria weighting engine (DEC-001).*

```python
"""Analytic Hierarchy Process (AHP) multi-criteria weighting engine (DEC-001).

Implements Saaty's eigenvalue method, consistency checking (CR <= 0.10),
and candidate scoring across microgrid sizing alternatives.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Sequence
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

# Saaty Random Index (RI) table for matrix sizes n = 1..10
SAATY_RI: dict[int, float] = {
    1: 0.0,
    2: 0.0,
    3: 0.58,
    4: 0.90,
    5: 1.12,
    6: 1.24,
    7: 1.32,
    8: 1.41,
    9: 1.45,
    10: 1.49,
}

CRITERIA_NAMES: list[str] = [
    "Reliability",
    "Cost",
    "Self-sufficiency",
    "Curtailment",
]


def ahp_weights(
    matrix: np.ndarray | Sequence[Sequence[float]],
) -> tuple[np.ndarray, float, float, float]:
    """Calculate priority weight vector and consistency ratio via principal eigenvector method.

    Parameters
    ----------
    matrix : np.ndarray | Sequence[Sequence[float]]
        Positive reciprocal pairwise comparison matrix of size (n, n).

    Returns
    -------
    tuple[np.ndarray, float, float, float]
        (weights, lambda_max, CI, CR):
        - weights: 1D numpy array of normalized priority weights summing to 1.0.
        - lambda_max: Principal eigenvalue (scalar float).
        - CI: Consistency Index = (lambda_max - n) / (n - 1).
        - CR: Consistency Ratio = CI / RI[n].

    Raises
    ------
    ValueError
        If matrix is not square, not positive, or if CR > 0.10.
    """
    A = np.asarray(matrix, dtype=np.float64)
    if A.ndim != 2 or A.shape[0] != A.shape[1]:
        raise ValueError(f"Pairwise comparison matrix must be square (n, n), got shape {A.shape}")

    n = A.shape[0]
    if n == 0:
        raise ValueError("Matrix cannot be empty.")
    if np.any(A <= 0.0):
        raise ValueError("All entries in pairwise comparison matrix must be strictly positive.")

    if n == 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64), 1.0, 0.0, 0.0

    # Compute eigenvalues and right eigenvectors
    eigvals, eigvecs = np.linalg.eig(A)

    # Find principal eigenvalue (largest real component)
    max_idx = int(np.argmax(np.real(eigvals)))
    lambda_max = float(np.real(eigvals[max_idx]))

    # Extract corresponding principal eigenvector
    principal_vec = np.real(eigvecs[:, max_idx])

    # Ensure all components have positive sign
    if np.all(principal_vec < 0.0):
        principal_vec = -principal_vec
    elif np.any(principal_vec < 0.0):
        # In case of small numerical rounding near zero
        principal_vec = np.abs(principal_vec)

    total_sum = float(np.sum(principal_vec))
    if total_sum <= 0.0:
        raise ValueError("Principal eigenvector elements sum to non-positive value.")

    weights = principal_vec / total_sum

    # Consistency Index (CI)
    ci = (lambda_max - n) / (n - 1)
    if abs(ci) < 1e-12:
        ci = 0.0

    # Consistency Ratio (CR)
    ri = SAATY_RI.get(n, 1.49)
    cr = (ci / ri) if ri > 0.0 else 0.0

    # Saaty consistency threshold check
    if cr > 0.10:
        raise ValueError(
            f"AHP Consistency Ratio {cr:.4f} exceeds allowable threshold (CR <= 0.10). "
            f"Matrix is inconsistent (lambda_max={lambda_max:.4f}, CI={ci:.4f})."
        )

    return weights, lambda_max, ci, cr


def get_base_pairwise_matrix() -> np.ndarray:
    """Construct the baseline 4x4 pairwise comparison matrix per task specification [D-38].

    Criteria order:
    1. Reliability (Tier 1+2 outage coverage in typhoon regime)
    2. Cost Index
    3. Self-sufficiency
    4. Curtailment (lower is better)

    Pairwise judgments:
    - Reliability vs Cost = 3
    - Reliability vs Self-sufficiency = 5
    - Reliability vs Curtailment = 7
    - Cost vs Self-sufficiency = 2
    - Cost vs Curtailment = 3
    - Self-sufficiency vs Curtailment = 2
    """
    matrix = np.array(
        [
            [1.0, 3.0, 5.0, 7.0],
            [1.0 / 3.0, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0],
            [1.0 / 5.0, 1.0 / 2.0, 1.0, 2.0],
            [1.0 / 7.0, 1.0 / 3.0, 1.0 / 2.0, 1.0],
        ],
        dtype=np.float64,
    )
    return matrix


def get_ahp_weight_sets() -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:
    """Return dictionary of the 3 canonical AHP weight profiles evaluated in DEC-001/002."""
    base_matrix = get_base_pairwise_matrix()
    base_w, _, _, _ = ahp_weights(base_matrix)

    equal_w = np.array([0.25, 0.25, 0.25, 0.25], dtype=np.float64)
    reliability_heavy_w = np.array([0.60, 0.20, 0.10, 0.10], dtype=np.float64)

    return {
        "base": base_w,
        "equal": equal_w,
        "reliability_heavy": reliability_heavy_w,
    }


def compute_cost_index(
    solar_kwp: float | np.ndarray,
    battery_kwh: float | np.ndarray,
    r: float = 0.6,
) -> float | np.ndarray:
    """Calculate relative system cost index per [D-38].

    Cost = solar_kwp * 1.0 + battery_kwh * r
    where r is the relative unit capacity cost assumption of storage vs solar PV.

    Parameters
    ----------
    solar_kwp : float | np.ndarray
        Installed solar capacity in kWp.
    battery_kwh : float | np.ndarray
        Installed battery capacity in kWh.
    r : float, optional
        Relative unit cost factor (default: 0.6, sensitivity {0.3, 1.0}).
    """
    return solar_kwp * 1.0 + battery_kwh * r


def score_candidates(
    candidates_df: pd.DataFrame,
    weights: np.ndarray | Sequence[float],
    r: float = 0.6,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Score and rank candidate sizings using min-max normalized multi-criteria synthesis.

    Criteria directions:
    - Reliability (fraction of outage hours T1+2 served): MAXIMIZE
    - Cost Index: MINIMIZE
    - Self-sufficiency: MAXIMIZE
    - Curtailment (kWh): MINIMIZE

    Parameters
    ----------
    candidates_df : pd.DataFrame
        DataFrame containing candidate performance metrics with columns:
        'solar_kwp', 'battery_kwh', 'typhoon_t1_2_served_pct', 'self_sufficiency_mean', 'curtailed_mean'.
    weights : np.ndarray | Sequence[float]
        4-element vector of criterion weights summing to 1.0.
    r : float, optional
        Relative cost parameter (default: 0.6).

    Returns
    -------
    pd.DataFrame
        Sorted DataFrame with normalized criterion scores, composite 'ahp_score', and 'ahp_rank'.
    """
    df = candidates_df.copy()
    w = np.asarray(weights, dtype=np.float64)
    if len(w) != 4:
        raise ValueError(f"Weights must have length 4, got {len(w)}")

    # 1. Compute Cost Index if not already present
    if "cost_index" not in df.columns:
        df["cost_index"] = compute_cost_index(df["solar_kwp"], df["battery_kwh"], r=r)

    # 2. Extract raw criteria
    # Reliability: outage hours served fraction (0.0 to 1.0)
    if "reliability" in df.columns:
        rel = df["reliability"].to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)
    elif "typhoon_t1_2_served_pct" in df.columns:
        rel = df["typhoon_t1_2_served_pct"].to_numpy(dtype=np.float64) / 100.0
    else:
        raise KeyError("Candidates DataFrame must include 'reliability' or 'typhoon_t1_2_served_pct'")

    cost = df["cost_index"].to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)

    if "self_sufficiency" in df.columns:
        ss = df["self_sufficiency"].to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)
    elif "self_sufficiency_mean" in df.columns:
        ss = df["self_sufficiency_mean"].to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        raise KeyError("Candidates DataFrame must include 'self_sufficiency' or 'self_sufficiency_mean'")

    if "curtailment" in df.columns:
        curt = df["curtailment"].to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)
    elif "curtailed_mean" in df.columns:
        curt = df["curtailed_mean"].to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        raise KeyError("Candidates DataFrame must include 'curtailment' or 'curtailed_mean'")

    def _norm_benefit(val: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        v_min, v_max = float(np.min(val)), float(np.max(val))
        if abs(v_max - v_min) < 1e-12:
            return np.ones_like(val)
        return (val - v_min) / (v_max - v_min)

    def _norm_cost(val: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        v_min, v_max = float(np.min(val)), float(np.max(val))
        if abs(v_max - v_min) < 1e-12:
            return np.ones_like(val)
        return (v_max - val) / (v_max - v_min)

    norm_rel = _norm_benefit(rel)
    norm_cost = _norm_cost(cost)
    norm_ss = _norm_benefit(ss)
    norm_curt = _norm_cost(curt)

    df["norm_reliability"] = norm_rel
    df["norm_cost"] = norm_cost
    df["norm_self_sufficiency"] = norm_ss
    df["norm_curtailment"] = norm_curt

    # Composite weighted score
    ahp_score = (
        w[0] * norm_rel
        + w[1] * norm_cost
        + w[2] * norm_ss
        + w[3] * norm_curt
    )
    df["ahp_score"] = ahp_score

    # Rank descending by score (with unserved T1+2 kWh as tie-breaker if available)
    sort_cols = ["ahp_score"]
    ascending_flags = [False]
    if "unserved_t1_2_mean" in df.columns:
        sort_cols.append("unserved_t1_2_mean")
        ascending_flags.append(True)

    df = df.sort_values(by=sort_cols, ascending=ascending_flags).reset_index(drop=True)
    df["ahp_rank"] = np.arange(1, len(df) + 1)

    return df
```

### `src/ccis_sim/decision/plots.py`

*368 lines. Publication-quality visualization and figure generation suite (DEC-003.1 / DEC-003.2).*

```python
"""Publication-quality visualization and figure generation suite (DEC-003.1 / DEC-003.2).

Generates matplotlib figures saved to results/figures/ (150 dpi) conforming to
task specifications and academic reporting standards:
1. 7-day dispatch stacked area chart during typhoon outage.
2. Battery State-of-Charge (SoC) duration curves.
3. Distribution of unserved Tier 1+2 energy across Monte Carlo seeds.
4. Pareto frontier trade-off scatter across demand cases.
5. Monthly building electricity demand profile (bottom-up vs synthetic bills).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Sequence

import matplotlib
matplotlib.use("Agg")  # Non-interactive headless backend
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from ccis_sim.core.models import HourlyRecord
from ccis_sim.demand.mock_bills import SyntheticBillGenerator
from ccis_sim.integration.engine import SimulationConfig, simulate


def ensure_figures_dir(output_dir: Path | str = "results/figures") -> Path:
    """Ensure destination directory for figures exists."""
    p = Path(output_dir).resolve()
    p.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return p


def plot_7day_dispatch(
    records: Sequence[HourlyRecord],
    battery_kwh: float,
    output_path: Path | str = "results/figures/fig1_dispatch_7day.png",
    start_hour: int | None = None,
) -> Path:
    """Figure 1: 7-day chronological dispatch stacked area chart during an outage window.

    Shows power flows meeting demand (Solar to load, Battery discharge, Grid import,
    and Unserved shortfall) with stored battery SoC on a secondary right axis.
    """
    path = Path(output_path).resolve()
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # Find a 7-day window (168h) containing an outage if start_hour not specified
    if start_hour is None:
        outage_hours = [i for i, r in enumerate(records) if not r.grid_available]
        if outage_hours:
            center_outage = outage_hours[len(outage_hours) // 2]
            start_hour = max(0, min(center_outage - 84, len(records) - 168))
        else:
            start_hour = 24 * 180  # Default to mid-year

    window = records[start_hour : start_hour + 168]
    hours = np.arange(len(window))

    solar_to_load = np.array([r.solar_to_demand_kwh for r in window])
    bat_discharge = np.array([r.battery_discharge_kwh for r in window])
    grid_import = np.array([r.grid_import_kwh for r in window])
    unserved = np.array([r.unserved_load_kwh for r in window])
    soc_kwh = np.array([r.battery_soc_kwh for r in window])
    grid_avail = np.array([r.grid_available for r in window])

    fig, ax1 = plt.subplots(figsize=(12, 5.5), dpi=150)

    # Stacked area of electrical supply dispatch
    ax1.stackplot(
        hours,
        solar_to_load,
        bat_discharge,
        grid_import,
        unserved,
        labels=["Solar to Demand", "Battery Discharge", "Grid Import", "Unserved Shortfall"],
        colors=["#f39c12", "#27ae60", "#2980b9", "#e74c3c"],
        alpha=0.85,
    )

    # Highlight outage periods with shaded vertical spans
    in_outage = False
    outage_start = 0
    for h, avail in enumerate(grid_avail):
        if not avail and not in_outage:
            in_outage = True
            outage_start = h
        elif avail and in_outage:
            in_outage = False
            ax1.axvspan(outage_start, h, color="grey", alpha=0.25, hatch="//", label="Grid Outage" if outage_start == 0 else "")
    if in_outage:
        ax1.axvspan(outage_start, len(grid_avail), color="grey", alpha=0.25, hatch="//")

    ax1.set_xlim(0, 168)
    ax1.set_xlabel("Time Elapsed (Hours over 7-Day Window)", fontsize=11, fontweight="bold")
    ax1.set_ylabel("Hourly Electrical Flow (kW or kWh/h)", fontsize=11, fontweight="bold")
    ax1.set_title("Figure 1: 7-Day Microgrid Dispatch Dynamics Across Outage Window", fontsize=12, fontweight="bold")
    ax1.grid(True, linestyle=":", alpha=0.6)

    # Secondary axis for Battery SoC
    ax2 = ax1.twinx()
    ax2.plot(hours, soc_kwh, color="#8e44ad", linewidth=2.0, linestyle="--", label="Battery Stored SoC (kWh)")
    if battery_kwh > 0:
        ax2.set_ylim(0, battery_kwh * 1.1)
        ax2.axhline(0.10 * battery_kwh, color="darkred", linestyle=":", linewidth=1.2, label="10% Protection Floor")
    ax2.set_ylabel("Battery State-of-Charge (kWh)", color="#8e44ad", fontsize=11, fontweight="bold")
    ax2.tick_params(axis="y", labelcolor="#8e44ad")

    # Combine legends
    lines1, labels1 = ax1.get_legend_handles_labels()
    lines2, labels2 = ax2.get_legend_handles_labels()
    # Remove duplicate labels
    by_label = dict(zip(labels1 + labels2, lines1 + lines2))
    ax1.legend(by_label.values(), by_label.keys(), loc="upper left", framealpha=0.9, fontsize=9)

    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(path, dpi=150)
    plt.close(fig)
    return path


def plot_soc_duration_curve(
    records_normal: Sequence[HourlyRecord],
    records_typhoon: Sequence[HourlyRecord],
    battery_kwh: float,
    output_path: Path | str = "results/figures/fig2_soc_duration_curve.png",
) -> Path:
    """Figure 2: Battery State-of-Charge duration curve (Normal vs Typhoon outage regimes)."""
    path = Path(output_path).resolve()
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    soc_normal = np.sort([r.battery_soc_kwh for r in records_normal])[::-1]
    soc_typhoon = np.sort([r.battery_soc_kwh for r in records_typhoon])[::-1]
    pct_year = np.linspace(0, 100, len(soc_normal))

    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8, 5), dpi=150)
    ax.plot(pct_year, soc_normal, label="Normal Reliability Regime", color="#2980b9", linewidth=2.0)
    ax.plot(pct_year, soc_typhoon, label="Typhoon Reliability Regime", color="#c0392b", linewidth=2.0, linestyle="--")

    if battery_kwh > 0:
        ax.axhline(battery_kwh, color="black", linestyle=":", label=f"Nominal Capacity ({battery_kwh:.0f} kWh)")
        ax.axhline(0.10 * battery_kwh, color="red", linestyle=":", label=f"Min SoC Floor ({0.10*battery_kwh:.0f} kWh)")
        ax.set_ylim(0, battery_kwh * 1.08)

    ax.set_xlim(0, 100)
    ax.set_xlabel("Percentage of Annual Hours Exceeded (%)", fontsize=11, fontweight="bold")
    ax.set_ylabel("Battery Usable State-of-Charge (kWh)", fontsize=11, fontweight="bold")
    ax.set_title("Figure 2: Annual Battery State-of-Charge Duration Curve", fontsize=12, fontweight="bold")
    ax.grid(True, linestyle=":", alpha=0.6)
    ax.legend(loc="upper right", framealpha=0.9, fontsize=9.5)

    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(path, dpi=150)
    plt.close(fig)
    return path


def plot_unserved_distribution(
    runs_df: pd.DataFrame,
    recommended_pair: tuple[float, float],
    output_path: Path | str = "results/figures/fig3_unserved_distribution.png",
) -> Path:
    """Figure 3: Unserved Tier 1+2 energy distributions across Monte Carlo seeds (Typhoon regime).

    Compares 3 representative sizings: (100 kWp, 50 kWh), Recommended Sizing, and (200 kWp, 200 kWh).
    """
    path = Path(output_path).resolve()
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    df_typh = runs_df[
        (runs_df["outage_regime"] == "typhoon")
        & (runs_df["demand_scale"] == 1.0)
    ].copy()
    df_typh["unserved_t1_2_kwh"] = df_typh["unserved_t1_kwh"] + df_typh["unserved_t2_kwh"]

    rec_sol, rec_bat = recommended_pair
    triplet = [
        (100.0, 50.0, "100 kWp / 50 kWh\n(moderate system)"),
        (rec_sol, rec_bat, f"{rec_sol:.0f} kWp / {rec_bat:.0f} kWh\n(recommended)"),
        (200.0, 200.0, "200 kWp / 200 kWh\n(max grid cap)"),
    ]

    data_to_plot = []
    labels = []
    for sol, bat, name in triplet:
        subset = df_typh[(df_typh["solar_kwp"] == sol) & (df_typh["battery_kwh"] == bat)]
        if not subset.empty:
            data_to_plot.append(subset["unserved_t1_2_kwh"].to_numpy())
            labels.append(name)
        else:
            data_to_plot.append(np.array([0.0]))
            labels.append(name)

    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(9.0, 5.2), dpi=150)
    box = ax.boxplot(
        data_to_plot,
        tick_labels=labels,
        patch_artist=True,
        showmeans=True,
        meanline=True,
    )
    ax.tick_params(axis="x", labelsize=9)
    colors = ["#3498db", "#2ecc71", "#9b59b6"]
    for patch, color in zip(box["boxes"], colors):
        patch.set_facecolor(color)
        patch.set_alpha(0.7)

    ax.set_ylabel("Unserved Tier 1 + 2 Energy (kWh / year)", fontsize=11, fontweight="bold")
    ax.set_title("Figure 3: Unserved Critical Energy Distribution across 50 Stochastic Seeds (Typhoon)", fontsize=11, fontweight="bold")
    ax.grid(True, linestyle=":", alpha=0.6)

    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(path, dpi=150)
    plt.close(fig)
    return path


def plot_pareto_scatter(
    candidates_df: pd.DataFrame,
    recommended_pair: tuple[float, float],
    output_path: Path | str = "results/figures/fig4_pareto_scatter.png",
) -> Path:
    """Figure 4: Pareto trade-off scatter (Cost Index vs Reliability) for both demand cases."""
    path = Path(output_path).resolve()
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    fig, (ax1, ax2) = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(14, 5.5), dpi=150, sharey=True)

    cases = [
        ("bottom_up", "Panel A: Bottom-Up Demand (Primary, Scale = 1.0)", ax1),
        ("bill_calibrated", "Panel B: Bill-Calibrated Demand (Sensitivity, Scale ≈ 2.10)", ax2),
    ]

    rec_sol, rec_bat = recommended_pair

    for case_key, title, ax in cases:
        sub = candidates_df[
            (candidates_df["demand_case"] == case_key)
            & (candidates_df["solar_kwp"] > 0)
        ].copy()

        # Non-Pareto vs Pareto points (cast to bool to handle mixed-type column)
        is_pareto_bool = sub["is_pareto"].astype(bool)
        non_pareto = sub[~is_pareto_bool]
        pareto = sub[is_pareto_bool].sort_values("cost_index")

        sc1 = ax.scatter(
            non_pareto["cost_index"],
            non_pareto["reliability"] * 100.0,
            c=non_pareto["self_sufficiency"] * 100.0,
            cmap="viridis",
            s=60,
            alpha=0.5,
            edgecolors="none",
            label="Dominated Sizing",
            vmin=30,
            vmax=90,
        )

        sc2 = ax.scatter(
            pareto["cost_index"],
            pareto["reliability"] * 100.0,
            c=pareto["self_sufficiency"] * 100.0,
            cmap="viridis",
            s=120,
            marker="o",
            edgecolors="black",
            linewidth=1.5,
            label="Pareto Optimal Frontier",
            vmin=30,
            vmax=90,
        )

        # Connect Pareto frontier with step / line
        if not pareto.empty:
            ax.plot(
                pareto["cost_index"],
                pareto["reliability"] * 100.0,
                color="black",
                linestyle="--",
                alpha=0.7,
                linewidth=1.2,
            )

        # Mark recommended point
        rec_pt = sub[(sub["solar_kwp"] == rec_sol) & (sub["battery_kwh"] == rec_bat)]
        if not rec_pt.empty:
            ax.scatter(
                rec_pt["cost_index"],
                rec_pt["reliability"] * 100.0,
                color="#e74c3c",
                s=280,
                marker="*",
                edgecolors="black",
                linewidth=1.5,
                zorder=5,
                label=f"Recommended ({rec_sol:.0f}kWp/{rec_bat:.0f}kWh)",
            )

        ax.set_xlabel("Relative Sizing Cost Index (PV + 0.6×Battery)", fontsize=10.5, fontweight="bold")
        if ax == ax1:
            ax.set_ylabel("Tier 1+2 Outage Hours Served (%) [Typhoon]", fontsize=10.5, fontweight="bold")
        ax.set_title(title, fontsize=11, fontweight="bold")
        ax.grid(True, linestyle=":", alpha=0.6)
        ax.legend(loc="lower right", framealpha=0.9, fontsize=8.5)

    cbar_ax = fig.add_axes([0.92, 0.15, 0.02, 0.7])
    cbar = fig.colorbar(sc2, cax=cbar_ax)
    cbar.set_label("Annual Self-Sufficiency (%)", fontsize=10.5, fontweight="bold")

    fig.suptitle("Figure 4: Multi-Objective Pareto Trade-Off: Cost vs Resilience vs Self-Sufficiency", fontsize=12, fontweight="bold")
    plt.subplots_adjust(right=0.90, wspace=0.10)
    plt.savefig(path, dpi=150)
    plt.close(fig)
    return path


def plot_monthly_demand_comparison(
    records: Sequence[HourlyRecord],
    bills_path: Path | str = "data/fixtures/synthetic-mock-bills-ccis.csv",
    output_path: Path | str = "results/figures/fig5_monthly_demand_comparison.png",
) -> Path:
    """Figure 5: Monthly electricity consumption: Bottom-Up model vs Synthetic Mock Utility Bills.

    Visualizes the ~2.1x demand gap between bottom-up academic schedule and continuous baseload.
    """
    path = Path(output_path).resolve()
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # 1. Monthly bottom-up simulation
    monthly_sim = np.zeros(12, dtype=np.float64)
    for r in records:
        m = int(r.timestamp[5:7]) - 1
        monthly_sim[m] += r.demand_gross_kwh

    # 2. Mock utility bills
    bills = SyntheticBillGenerator.load_from_csv(bills_path)
    monthly_bills = np.array([b.total_kwh for b in bills])

    month_names = ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun", "Jul", "Aug", "Sep", "Oct", "Nov", "Dec"]
    x = np.arange(12)
    width = 0.38

    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 5), dpi=150)
    b1 = ax.bar(x - width / 2, monthly_sim / 1e3, width, label="Bottom-Up Model (DEM-001/002, scale=1.0)", color="#3498db")
    b2 = ax.bar(x + width / 2, monthly_bills / 1e3, width, label="Synthetic Utility Bills (CORE-003, scale≈2.10)", color="#e67e22")

    tot_sim = np.sum(monthly_sim) / 1e3
    tot_bill = np.sum(monthly_bills) / 1e3
    ratio = tot_bill / tot_sim

    ax.set_xticks(x)
    ax.set_xticklabels(month_names, fontsize=10)
    ax.set_ylabel("Monthly Electricity Consumption (MWh)", fontsize=11, fontweight="bold")
    ax.set_title(
        f"Figure 5: Monthly Demand Profile: Bottom-Up vs Synthetic Bills\n"
        f"Annual: Bottom-Up = {tot_sim:.1f} MWh (EUI 75.7) vs Bills = {tot_bill:.1f} MWh (EUI 159.4) | Ratio = {ratio:.2f}×",
        fontsize=11,
        fontweight="bold",
    )
    ax.grid(True, axis="y", linestyle=":", alpha=0.6)
    ax.legend(loc="upper left", framealpha=0.9, fontsize=9.5)

    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(path, dpi=150)
    plt.close(fig)
    return path
```

### `src/ccis_sim/decision/sizing.py`

*392 lines. Pareto trade-off optimization, candidate evaluation, and sizing recommendation engine (DEC-002).*

```python
"""Pareto trade-off optimization, candidate evaluation, and sizing recommendation engine (DEC-002).

Evaluates candidate microgrid sizing configurations (solar in {50, 100, 150, 200} kWp,
battery in {0, 25, 50, 100, 150, 200} kWh) across stochastic outage regimes,
constructs non-dominated Pareto frontiers, and executes formal sizing rules.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import multiprocessing as mp
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Sequence

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from scipy import stats

from ccis_sim.decision.ahp import compute_cost_index, score_candidates
from ccis_sim.integration.engine import SimulationConfig, simulate
from ccis_sim.integration.scenarios import compute_bill_calibrated_scale
from ccis_sim.weather.loader import load_weather_fixture

CANDIDATE_SOLAR_SIZES: list[float] = [50.0, 100.0, 150.0, 200.0]
CANDIDATE_BATTERY_SIZES: list[float] = [0.0, 25.0, 50.0, 100.0, 150.0, 200.0]

_DEC_WEATHER_CACHE: dict[int, pd.DataFrame] = {}


def _init_dec_worker() -> None:
    """Initialize worker process by pre-loading and caching canonical weather fixtures once."""
    global _DEC_WEATHER_CACHE
    try:
        _DEC_WEATHER_CACHE[2016] = load_weather_fixture(2016)
        _DEC_WEATHER_CACHE[2019] = load_weather_fixture(2019)
    except Exception:
        _DEC_WEATHER_CACHE = {}


def _run_dec_simulation(config: SimulationConfig) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Execute a single simulation run optimized for batch decision evaluation."""
    global _DEC_WEATHER_CACHE
    if not _DEC_WEATHER_CACHE:
        _init_dec_worker()

    w_df = _DEC_WEATHER_CACHE.get(config.weather_year)
    res = simulate(config, weather_df=w_df, verify=False)
    m = res.metrics

    battery_cycles = (
        m["annual_battery_discharge_kwh"] / config.battery_kwh
        if config.battery_kwh > 0.0
        else 0.0
    )
    self_sufficiency = m["self_sufficiency_pct"] / 100.0

    return {
        "solar_kwp": float(config.solar_kwp),
        "battery_kwh": float(config.battery_kwh),
        "weather_year": int(config.weather_year),
        "outage_regime": str(config.outage_regime),
        "demand_scale": float(config.demand_scale),
        "temp_offset_c": float(config.temp_offset_c),
        "seed": int(config.seed),
        "grid_import_kwh": float(m["annual_grid_import_kwh"]),
        "solar_gen_kwh": float(m["annual_solar_generation_kwh"]),
        "curtailed_kwh": float(m["annual_solar_curtailed_kwh"]),
        "unserved_t1_kwh": float(m["unserved_tier1_kwh"]),
        "unserved_t2_kwh": float(m["unserved_tier2_kwh"]),
        "unserved_t3_kwh": float(m["unserved_tier3_kwh"]),
        "total_outage_hours": int(m["total_outage_hours"]),
        "outage_hours_t1_served": int(m["outage_hours_tier1_served"]),
        "outage_hours_t2_served": int(m["outage_hours_tier2_served"]),
        "battery_cycles": float(battery_cycles),
        "self_sufficiency": float(self_sufficiency),
    }


def get_candidate_sizing_pairs() -> list[tuple[float, float]]:
    """Return the list of 24 candidate sizing pairs (excluding 0/0 reference baseline per [D-38])."""
    pairs: list[tuple[float, float]] = []
    for sol in CANDIDATE_SOLAR_SIZES:
        for bat in CANDIDATE_BATTERY_SIZES:
            pairs.append((sol, bat))
    return pairs


def generate_dec_configs(
    bill_scale: float | None = None,
    n_seeds: int = 50,
    weather_year: int = 2019,
    sizing_pairs: Sequence[tuple[float, float]] | None = None,
) -> list[SimulationConfig]:
    """Generate configuration matrix for DEC-002 candidate evaluation.

    Includes all 24 solar/battery candidate combinations plus the 0/0 reference baseline.
    Evaluated across normal and typhoon regimes, both demand scales (1.0 and bill-calibrated),
    and N random seeds using Common Random Numbers (CRN).
    """
    if bill_scale is None:
        bill_scale = compute_bill_calibrated_scale()

    if sizing_pairs is None:
        # All 24 candidates + (0, 0) reference
        pairs = [(0.0, 0.0)] + get_candidate_sizing_pairs()
    else:
        pairs = list(sizing_pairs)

    configs: list[SimulationConfig] = []
    for scale in (1.0, float(bill_scale)):
        for regime in ("normal", "typhoon"):
            for seed in range(n_seeds):
                for sol, bat in pairs:
                    configs.append(
                        SimulationConfig(
                            solar_kwp=sol,
                            battery_kwh=bat,
                            weather_year=weather_year,
                            outage_regime=regime,
                            seed=seed,
                            demand_scale=scale,
                        )
                    )
    return configs


def run_candidate_simulations(
    configs: Sequence[SimulationConfig],
    n_workers: int | None = None,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Execute simulation batch across multiprocessing worker pool."""
    workers = n_workers or min(mp.cpu_count(), 8)
    with mp.Pool(processes=workers, initializer=_init_dec_worker) as pool:
        records = pool.map(_run_dec_simulation, configs)
    return pd.DataFrame(records)


def compute_cell_statistics(series: pd.Series) -> dict[str, float]:
    """Compute sample mean, standard deviation, 95% CI half-width, and percentiles."""
    vals = series.to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(vals)
    if n == 0:
        return {"mean": 0.0, "std": 0.0, "ci95_half": 0.0, "p5": 0.0, "p50": 0.0, "p95": 0.0}

    mean_val = float(np.mean(vals))
    std_val = float(np.std(vals, ddof=1)) if n > 1 else 0.0
    se = std_val / np.sqrt(n) if n > 0 else 0.0
    t_crit = float(stats.t.ppf(0.975, df=n - 1)) if n > 1 else 1.96
    ci95_half = t_crit * se

    p5 = float(np.percentile(vals, 5))
    p50 = float(np.percentile(vals, 50))
    p95 = float(np.percentile(vals, 95))

    return {
        "mean": mean_val,
        "std": std_val,
        "ci95_half": ci95_half,
        "p5": p5,
        "p50": p50,
        "p95": p95,
    }


def aggregate_candidates_for_decision(
    runs_df: pd.DataFrame,
    r: float = 0.6,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Aggregate simulation runs into candidate summary records for AHP and Pareto analysis.

    For each sizing and demand scale:
    - Extracts mean fraction of outage hours with Tier 1+2 served in typhoon regime (Reliability).
    - Extracts mean self-sufficiency and curtailment (normal regime).
    - Computes cost index = solar_kwp + battery_kwh * r.
    - Computes full sample statistics (mean, std, 95% CI, percentiles).
    """
    df = runs_df.copy()
    # Add combined unserved Tier 1+2 metric
    df["unserved_t1_2_kwh"] = df["unserved_t1_kwh"] + df["unserved_t2_kwh"]
    # Fraction of outage hours where T1+2 were served
    # In HourlyRecord/scenarios, outage_hours_t2_served represents hours where Tier 2 was served.
    # By priority structure, Tier 1 is always served whenever Tier 2 is served.
    df["t1_2_served_fraction"] = np.where(
        df["total_outage_hours"] > 0,
        df["outage_hours_t2_served"] / df["total_outage_hours"],
        1.0,
    )

    rows = []
    group_cols = ["solar_kwp", "battery_kwh", "weather_year", "demand_scale"]

    for (sol, bat, year, scale), group in df.groupby(group_cols):
        # Normal regime subset (baseline grid metrics)
        norm_group = group[group["outage_regime"] == "normal"]
        # Typhoon regime subset (extreme reliability metrics)
        typh_group = group[group["outage_regime"] == "typhoon"]

        typh_rel_stats = compute_cell_statistics(typh_group["t1_2_served_fraction"])
        typh_unserved_stats = compute_cell_statistics(typh_group["unserved_t1_2_kwh"])

        norm_rel_stats = compute_cell_statistics(norm_group["t1_2_served_fraction"])
        norm_unserved_stats = compute_cell_statistics(norm_group["unserved_t1_2_kwh"])

        ss_mean = float(norm_group["self_sufficiency"].mean())
        curt_mean = float(norm_group["curtailed_kwh"].mean())
        grid_mean = float(norm_group["grid_import_kwh"].mean())
        solar_mean = float(norm_group["solar_gen_kwh"].mean())
        cycles_mean = float(norm_group["battery_cycles"].mean())
        cost = compute_cost_index(sol, bat, r=r)

        rows.append(
            {
                "solar_kwp": sol,
                "battery_kwh": bat,
                "weather_year": year,
                "demand_scale": scale,
                "demand_case": "bottom_up" if abs(scale - 1.0) < 1e-3 else "bill_calibrated",
                "cost_index": cost,
                # Typhoon reliability (AHP Criterion 1)
                "reliability": typh_rel_stats["mean"],
                "reliability_std": typh_rel_stats["std"],
                "reliability_ci95_half": typh_rel_stats["ci95_half"],
                "reliability_p5": typh_rel_stats["p5"],
                "reliability_p50": typh_rel_stats["p50"],
                "reliability_p95": typh_rel_stats["p95"],
                # Typhoon unserved Tier 1+2 (tie-breaker & engineering KPI)
                "unserved_t1_2_mean": typh_unserved_stats["mean"],
                "unserved_t1_2_std": typh_unserved_stats["std"],
                "unserved_t1_2_ci95_half": typh_unserved_stats["ci95_half"],
                "unserved_t1_2_p5": typh_unserved_stats["p5"],
                "unserved_t1_2_p50": typh_unserved_stats["p50"],
                "unserved_t1_2_p95": typh_unserved_stats["p95"],
                # Normal reliability
                "normal_reliability_mean": norm_rel_stats["mean"],
                "normal_unserved_t1_2_mean": norm_unserved_stats["mean"],
                # Grid & Solar performance metrics (Normal regime)
                "self_sufficiency": ss_mean,
                "self_sufficiency_mean": ss_mean,
                "curtailment": curt_mean,
                "curtailed_mean": curt_mean,
                "grid_import_mean": grid_mean,
                "solar_gen_mean": solar_mean,
                "battery_cycles_mean": cycles_mean,
            }
        )

    res_df = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    return res_df


def identify_pareto_frontier(
    candidates_df: pd.DataFrame,
    r: float = 0.6,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Identify non-dominated Pareto optimal solutions over (Reliability ↑, Cost ↓, Self-sufficiency ↑).

    Per decision [D-38], candidate evaluations exclude solar = 0 (except for comparison).
    A candidate A dominates candidate B if:
      - Reliability(A) >= Reliability(B)
      - Cost(A) <= Cost(B)
      - Self_sufficiency(A) >= Self_sufficiency(B)
      with strict inequality in at least one metric.
    """
    df = candidates_df.copy()
    if "cost_index" not in df.columns:
        df["cost_index"] = compute_cost_index(df["solar_kwp"], df["battery_kwh"], r=r)

    # Exclude (0, 0) baseline from candidate trade-off competition if requested
    # But evaluate among all active solar candidates
    n = len(df)
    is_pareto = np.ones(n, dtype=bool)

    rel = df["reliability"].to_numpy()
    cost = df["cost_index"].to_numpy()
    ss = df["self_sufficiency"].to_numpy()

    tol = 1e-6
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(n):
            if i == j:
                continue
            # Does j dominate i?
            j_no_worse = (
                (rel[j] >= rel[i] - tol)
                and (cost[j] <= cost[i] + tol)
                and (ss[j] >= ss[i] - tol)
            )
            j_strictly_better = (
                (rel[j] > rel[i] + tol)
                or (cost[j] < cost[i] - tol)
                or (ss[j] > ss[i] + tol)
            )
            if j_no_worse and j_strictly_better:
                is_pareto[i] = False
                break

    df["is_pareto"] = is_pareto
    return df


def select_recommended_sizing(
    candidates_df: pd.DataFrame,
    r: float = 0.6,
    threshold_bottom_up: float = 0.90,
    threshold_bill_calibrated: float = 0.80,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Execute formal sizing recommendation rule locked in [D-38].

    Rule:
    "The cheapest non-dominated sizing whose mean Tier 1+2 outage-hours-served fraction
    is >= 0.90 in the typhoon regime under the bottom-up demand AND >= 0.80 under the
    bill-calibrated demand."

    If no single sizing simultaneously meets both, reports the best qualifying sizing
    under each demand case and the best composite trade-off.
    """
    df = candidates_df.copy()
    # Separate by demand case
    bu_df = df[df["demand_case"] == "bottom_up"].copy()
    bc_df = df[df["demand_case"] == "bill_calibrated"].copy()

    # Determine Pareto frontier for each demand case
    bu_pareto = identify_pareto_frontier(bu_df, r=r)
    bc_pareto = identify_pareto_frontier(bc_df, r=r)

    # Candidates meeting thresholds (excluding 0 solar)
    bu_qualifying = bu_pareto[
        (bu_pareto["solar_kwp"] > 0)
        & (bu_pareto["is_pareto"])
        & (bu_pareto["reliability"] >= threshold_bottom_up - 1e-4)
    ]
    bc_qualifying = bc_pareto[
        (bc_pareto["solar_kwp"] > 0)
        & (bc_pareto["is_pareto"])
        & (bc_pareto["reliability"] >= threshold_bill_calibrated - 1e-4)
    ]

    # Find common sizings meeting both criteria
    bu_pairs = set(zip(bu_qualifying["solar_kwp"], bu_qualifying["battery_kwh"]))
    bc_pairs = set(zip(bc_qualifying["solar_kwp"], bc_qualifying["battery_kwh"]))
    joint_pairs = bu_pairs.intersection(bc_pairs)

    joint_qualifying_rows = []
    for sol, bat in joint_pairs:
        cost = compute_cost_index(sol, bat, r=r)
        joint_qualifying_rows.append({"solar_kwp": sol, "battery_kwh": bat, "cost_index": cost})

    if joint_qualifying_rows:
        joint_df = pd.DataFrame(joint_qualifying_rows).sort_values("cost_index").reset_index(drop=True)
        recommended_solar = float(joint_df.iloc[0]["solar_kwp"])
        recommended_battery = float(joint_df.iloc[0]["battery_kwh"])
        is_robust = True
        status_note = "Qualifies simultaneously under both bottom-up (>=90%) and bill-calibrated (>=80%) demand."
    else:
        # Fallback: pick cheapest Pareto sizing from bill-calibrated that also has highest reliability
        is_robust = False
        if not bc_qualifying.empty:
            best_bc = bc_qualifying.sort_values("cost_index").iloc[0]
            recommended_solar = float(best_bc["solar_kwp"])
            recommended_battery = float(best_bc["battery_kwh"])
            status_note = "No sizing jointly qualified; selected cheapest bill-calibrated qualifying Pareto sizing."
        elif not bu_qualifying.empty:
            best_bu = bu_qualifying.sort_values("cost_index").iloc[0]
            recommended_solar = float(best_bu["solar_kwp"])
            recommended_battery = float(best_bu["battery_kwh"])
            status_note = "No sizing qualified under bill-calibrated demand; selected cheapest bottom-up qualifying sizing."
        else:
            # Pick highest reliability sizing
            max_bu = bu_pareto.sort_values("reliability", ascending=False).iloc[0]
            recommended_solar = float(max_bu["solar_kwp"])
            recommended_battery = float(max_bu["battery_kwh"])
            status_note = "No candidate reached threshold; selected maximum achievable reliability sizing."

    # Extract detailed metrics for recommended sizing under both demand cases
    rec_bu = bu_df[
        (bu_df["solar_kwp"] == recommended_solar) & (bu_df["battery_kwh"] == recommended_battery)
    ].iloc[0]
    rec_bc = bc_df[
        (bc_df["solar_kwp"] == recommended_solar) & (bc_df["battery_kwh"] == recommended_battery)
    ].iloc[0]

    return {
        "solar_kwp": recommended_solar,
        "battery_kwh": recommended_battery,
        "cost_index": compute_cost_index(recommended_solar, recommended_battery, r=r),
        "is_robust": is_robust,
        "status_note": status_note,
        "bottom_up_metrics": rec_bu.to_dict(),
        "bill_calibrated_metrics": rec_bc.to_dict(),
        "bu_qualifying_count": len(bu_qualifying),
        "bc_qualifying_count": len(bc_qualifying),
        "joint_qualifying_count": len(joint_qualifying_rows),
    }
```
