### `src/ccis_sim/demand/cooling.py`

*134 lines. Temperature and humidity sensitive AC cooling demand model.*

```python
"""Temperature and humidity sensitive AC cooling demand model.

Implements the Cooling Degree Day (CDD) sensible response, tropical latent humidity
penalty, inverter modulation bounds, and capacity caps conforming to:
- docs/prompts/DEM-002-PROMPT.md
- docs/context/phase2-interface-contract.md
- docs/decisions/decision-log.md [D-07], [D-10], [D-16], [D-28]
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from ccis_sim.core.interfaces import TieredDemand
from ccis_sim.core.models import Building
from ccis_sim.demand.equipment import EquipmentAudit


def compute_cooling_demand(
    weather_df: pd.DataFrame,
    occupancy_fraction: np.ndarray,
    audit: EquipmentAudit,
    building: Building,
    *,
    rh_low: float = 60.0,
    rh_high: float = 80.0,
) -> TieredDemand:
    r"""Compute hourly cooling electrical demand decomposed across priority tiers 1 to 3.

    Conforms strictly to docs/context/phase2-interface-contract.md:
    `compute_cooling_demand(weather_df: pd.DataFrame, occupancy_fraction: np.ndarray, audit: EquipmentAudit, building: Building) -> TieredDemand`

    Cooling model formulation ([ASSUMPTION / [D-28]]):
    1. Sensible Cooling Response ([D-07]):
       Sensible temperature delta above balance-point setpoint:
       $\Delta T_i = \max(0.0, T_{\text{amb}, i} - T_{\text{base}})$, where $T_{\text{base}} = \text{building.t\_base\_cooling\_c} = 24.0^\circ\text{C}$.
       Relative sensitivity: $s_T = \text{building.cooling\_sensitivity\_per\_c} = 0.04$ ($4.0\%/^\circ\text{C}$).
       At or below $T_{\text{base}}$, sensible cooling demand is identically zero.
    2. Tropical Latent Humidity Penalty ([D-07]):
       At tropical high humidity, coil moisture condensation increases enthalpy load:
       $M_{\text{latent}}(RH) = 1.0 + \text{building.latent\_cooling\_penalty} \times \text{clip}\left(\frac{RH - RH_{\text{low}}}{RH_{\text{high}} - RH_{\text{low}}}, 0.0, 1.0\right)$.
       Below $RH_{\text{low}} = 60.0\%$, $M_{\text{latent}} = 1.00$. Above $RH_{\text{high}} = 80.0\%$, $M_{\text{latent}} = 1.20$ (+20% penalty).
       Between 60% and 80%, penalty ramps linearly, avoiding unphysical step discontinuities.
    3. Operational Categories and Tier Allocation ([D-10], [D-16]):
       - Tier 2 Spot Cooling (IDF / telecommunications closets):
         Runs 24/7 uninterrupted ($hours = 24.0$) to dissipate continuous heat from PoE switches
         and routers. Independent of human occupancy ($f_{\text{occ}}$):
         $P_{\text{spot}, i} = \min\left(P_{\text{conn, spot}}, P_{\text{conn, spot}} \times s_T \Delta T_i \times M_{\text{latent}}(RH_i)\right)$.
       - Tier 3 Comfort Cooling (Classrooms, PC labs, faculty/admin offices):
         Operates only when rooms are occupied ($f_{\text{occ}} > 0$). When unoccupied ($f_{\text{occ}} = 0$),
         comfort AC units are turned off:
         $P_{\text{comfort}, i} = \min\left(f_{\text{occ}, i} \times P_{\text{conn, comfort}}, f_{\text{occ}, i} \times P_{\text{conn, comfort}} \times s_T \Delta T_i \times M_{\text{latent}}(RH_i)\right)$.
       - Tier 1 Life-Safety:
         Zero cooling equipment is assigned to Tier 1 in the building audit ($P_{\text{Tier 1}} = 0.0$).
    4. Capacity Cap and Modulation Floor:
       Cooling electrical demand is hard-capped at connected cooling capacity per tier.
       Inverter compressors modulate continuously; cycling at minimum speed over the 1-hour interval
       yields smooth energy integration proportional to required thermal load.

    Args:
        weather_df: DataFrame containing 't_amb' (°C) and 'rh' (%) time series.
        occupancy_fraction: 1D numpy array of length N containing float values in [0.0, 1.0].
        audit: EquipmentAudit inventory containing audited equipment items.
        building: Building envelope providing t_base_cooling_c, cooling_sensitivity_per_c,
            and latent_cooling_penalty.
        rh_low: Lower relative humidity threshold for latent penalty ramp (default 60.0%).
        rh_high: Upper relative humidity threshold for full latent penalty (default 80.0%).

    Returns:
        TieredDemand instance containing tier1_kwh, tier2_kwh, and tier3_kwh arrays of length N.
    """
    n = len(weather_df)
    occ = np.asarray(occupancy_fraction, dtype=np.float64)
    if len(occ) != n:
        raise ValueError(
            f"occupancy_fraction length ({len(occ)}) must match weather_df length ({n})."
        )
    if n == 0:
        raise ValueError("weather_df must not be empty.")

    if "t_amb" not in weather_df.columns or "rh" not in weather_df.columns:
        raise ValueError("weather_df must contain 't_amb' and 'rh' columns.")

    t_amb = weather_df["t_amb"].to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)
    rh = weather_df["rh"].to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)

    # 1. Delta-T above base temperature
    dt = np.maximum(0.0, t_amb - building.t_base_cooling_c)

    # 2. Continuous piecewise-linear latent humidity multiplier
    rh_norm = np.clip((rh - rh_low) / (rh_high - rh_low), 0.0, 1.0)
    m_latent = 1.0 + building.latent_cooling_penalty * rh_norm

    # 3. Disaggregate cooling equipment from audit by tier and operational schedule
    cooling_items = [it for it in audit.items if it.is_cooling]

    # Pre-accumulate connected capacity for spot cooling (24/7) vs comfort cooling per tier
    # Spot cooling: hours_per_day >= 24.0; Comfort cooling: hours_per_day < 24.0
    p_conn_spot = {1: 0.0, 2: 0.0, 3: 0.0}
    p_conn_comfort = {1: 0.0, 2: 0.0, 3: 0.0}

    for item in cooling_items:
        conn_kw = item.count * item.rated_kw
        if item.hours_per_day >= 24.0:
            p_conn_spot[item.tier] += conn_kw
        else:
            p_conn_comfort[item.tier] += conn_kw

    s_t = building.cooling_sensitivity_per_c

    # Vectorized hourly cooling calculation per tier
    tier_arrays: dict[int, np.ndarray] = {}
    for tier in (1, 2, 3):
        tier_arr = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        # Spot cooling term (independent of human occupancy, 24/7)
        if p_conn_spot[tier] > 0.0:
            spot_demand = p_conn_spot[tier] * s_t * dt * m_latent
            tier_arr += np.minimum(p_conn_spot[tier], spot_demand)

        # Comfort cooling term (scaled by occupancy_fraction)
        if p_conn_comfort[tier] > 0.0:
            comfort_cap = occ * p_conn_comfort[tier]
            comfort_demand = occ * p_conn_comfort[tier] * s_t * dt * m_latent
            tier_arr += np.minimum(comfort_cap, comfort_demand)

        tier_arrays[tier] = tier_arr

    return TieredDemand(
        tier1_kwh=tier_arrays[1],
        tier2_kwh=tier_arrays[2],
        tier3_kwh=tier_arrays[3],
    )
```

### `src/ccis_sim/demand/equipment.py`

*250 lines. Equipment audit domain model and inventory parser for CCIS simulation.*

```python
"""Equipment audit domain model and inventory parser for CCIS simulation.

Conforms to specifications locked in:
- docs/prompts/CORE-003-PROMPT.md
- docs/decisions/decision-log.md [D-06] (18 rooms layout), [D-10] (Tiers 1, 2, 3)
- docs/decisions/decision-log.md [D-16] (Equipment audit inventory and load assumptions)
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import csv
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Sequence

import pandas as pd


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EquipmentItem:
    """Individual electrical equipment item entry in the building audit.

    Attributes:
        item: Descriptive name of the electrical equipment.
        room_type: Room archetype category (e.g., 'computer_lab', 'lecture_room',
            'hyflex_smart_room', 'dean_and_secretary', 'cod_office', 'faculty_room',
            'building_common').
        count: Total physical count across all rooms of room_type.
        rated_kw: Rated active electrical power per single unit in kW.
        hours_per_day: Nominal operating hours per weekday (0.0 to 24.0).
        tier: Critical priority load shedding tier per [D-10] (1, 2, or 3).
        duty_cycle: Operating diversity factor / duty cycle during active hours (0.0 to 1.0).
        notes: Audit notes, assumption citations, or standard specifications.
    """

    item: str
    room_type: str
    count: int
    rated_kw: float
    hours_per_day: float
    tier: int
    duty_cycle: float = 1.0
    notes: str = ""

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.count <= 0:
            raise ValueError(f"count must be a positive integer, got {self.count}")
        if self.rated_kw < 0.0:
            raise ValueError(f"rated_kw must be non-negative, got {self.rated_kw}")
        if not (0.0 <= self.hours_per_day <= 24.0):
            raise ValueError(f"hours_per_day must be in [0, 24], got {self.hours_per_day}")
        if self.tier not in (1, 2, 3):
            raise ValueError(f"tier must be 1, 2, or 3 per [D-10], got {self.tier}")
        if not (0.0 <= self.duty_cycle <= 1.0):
            raise ValueError(f"duty_cycle must be in [0.0, 1.0], got {self.duty_cycle}")

    @property
    def total_kw(self) -> float:
        """Total nameplate connected electrical capacity for this equipment item (kW)."""
        return self.count * self.rated_kw

    @property
    def nominal_daily_kwh(self) -> float:
        """Nominal daily electrical energy consumption without duty cycle derating (kWh/day)."""
        return self.total_kw * self.hours_per_day

    @property
    def effective_daily_kwh(self) -> float:
        """Effective daily electrical energy consumption accounting for duty cycle (kWh/day)."""
        return self.total_kw * self.hours_per_day * self.duty_cycle

    @property
    def is_cooling(self) -> bool:
        """Determine whether the equipment contributes to sensible/latent cooling load."""
        item_lower = self.item.lower()
        return "ac unit" in item_lower or "split ac" in item_lower or "spot cooling" in item_lower


@dataclass
class EquipmentAudit:
    """Comprehensive building equipment audit inventory.

    Attributes:
        items: List of EquipmentItem records.
        floor_area_m2: Gross floor area of facility in square meters (default 2500.0 m² per [D-06]).
        building_name: Name of facility.
    """

    items: list[EquipmentItem] = field(default_factory=list)
    floor_area_m2: float = 2500.0
    building_name: str = "MMSU College of Computing and Information Sciences"

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.floor_area_m2 <= 0.0:
            raise ValueError(f"floor_area_m2 must be positive, got {self.floor_area_m2}")

    def add_item(self, item: EquipmentItem) -> None:
        """Add an equipment item to the audit inventory."""
        self.items.append(item)

    @classmethod
    def load_from_csv(
        cls,
        csv_path: Path | str,
        floor_area_m2: float = 2500.0,
        building_name: str = "MMSU College of Computing and Information Sciences",
    ) -> EquipmentAudit:
        """Load and parse an equipment audit CSV fixture.

        Expected minimum columns: item, room_type, count, rated_kw, hours_per_day, tier.
        Optional columns: duty_cycle, notes.
        """
        path = Path(csv_path)
        if not path.exists():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Equipment audit fixture file not found: {path}")

        audit = cls(floor_area_m2=floor_area_m2, building_name=building_name)

        with open(path, mode="r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            reader = csv.DictReader(f)
            required_cols = {"item", "room_type", "count", "rated_kw", "hours_per_day", "tier"}
            if not reader.fieldnames or not required_cols.issubset(set(reader.fieldnames)):
                raise ValueError(
                    f"CSV missing required columns: {required_cols - set(reader.fieldnames or [])}"
                )

            for row in reader:
                item = EquipmentItem(
                    item=row["item"].strip(),
                    room_type=row["room_type"].strip(),
                    count=int(row["count"]),
                    rated_kw=float(row["rated_kw"]),
                    hours_per_day=float(row["hours_per_day"]),
                    tier=int(row["tier"]),
                    duty_cycle=float(row.get("duty_cycle", 1.0) or 1.0),
                    notes=row.get("notes", "").strip(),
                )
                audit.add_item(item)

        return audit

    def total_connected_kw(self, tier: int | None = None) -> float:
        """Calculate total connected electrical power capacity (kW), optionally filtered by tier."""
        if tier is not None:
            return sum(item.total_kw for item in self.items if item.tier == tier)
        return sum(item.total_kw for item in self.items)

    def total_nominal_daily_kwh(self, tier: int | None = None) -> float:
        """Calculate total nominal daily energy consumption (kWh/day), optionally filtered by tier."""
        if tier is not None:
            return sum(item.nominal_daily_kwh for item in self.items if item.tier == tier)
        return sum(item.nominal_daily_kwh for item in self.items)

    def total_effective_daily_kwh(self, tier: int | None = None) -> float:
        """Calculate total effective daily energy consumption (kWh/day), optionally filtered by tier."""
        if tier is not None:
            return sum(item.effective_daily_kwh for item in self.items if item.tier == tier)
        return sum(item.effective_daily_kwh for item in self.items)

    def tier_breakdown_kw(self) -> dict[int, float]:
        """Return connected power in kW broken down by tier {1: kW, 2: kW, 3: kW}."""
        return {
            1: self.total_connected_kw(tier=1),
            2: self.total_connected_kw(tier=2),
            3: self.total_connected_kw(tier=3),
        }

    def tier_breakdown_pct(self) -> dict[int, float]:
        """Return percentage of connected power broken down by tier."""
        total = self.total_connected_kw()
        if total == 0.0:
            return {1: 0.0, 2: 0.0, 3: 0.0}
        return {t: (kw / total) * 100.0 for t, kw in self.tier_breakdown_kw().items()}

    def by_room_type(self, room_type: str) -> list[EquipmentItem]:
        """Filter audit items belonging to a specific room archetype."""
        return [item for item in self.items if item.room_type == room_type]

    def summary_by_room_type(self) -> dict[str, dict[str, float]]:
        """Return aggregated summary metrics by room archetype."""
        summary: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
        for item in self.items:
            if item.room_type not in summary:
                summary[item.room_type] = {
                    "total_kw": 0.0,
                    "nominal_daily_kwh": 0.0,
                    "effective_daily_kwh": 0.0,
                    "unit_count": 0.0,
                }
            summary[item.room_type]["total_kw"] += item.total_kw
            summary[item.room_type]["nominal_daily_kwh"] += item.nominal_daily_kwh
            summary[item.room_type]["effective_daily_kwh"] += item.effective_daily_kwh
            summary[item.room_type]["unit_count"] += item.count
        return summary

    def to_dataframe(self) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Convert equipment audit records to a pandas DataFrame."""
        records = [
            {
                "item": item.item,
                "room_type": item.room_type,
                "count": item.count,
                "rated_kw": item.rated_kw,
                "hours_per_day": item.hours_per_day,
                "tier": item.tier,
                "duty_cycle": item.duty_cycle,
                "total_kw": item.total_kw,
                "nominal_daily_kwh": item.nominal_daily_kwh,
                "effective_daily_kwh": item.effective_daily_kwh,
                "is_cooling": item.is_cooling,
                "notes": item.notes,
            }
            for item in self.items
        ]
        return pd.DataFrame(records)

    def validate(self) -> list[str]:
        """Validate inventory consistency against architectural baselines.

        Returns a list of validation warning/error strings (empty if valid).
        """
        errors = []
        if not self.items:
            errors.append("Equipment audit contains no items.")
            return errors

        # Check room types present
        room_types = {item.room_type for item in self.items}
        expected_archetypes = {
            "computer_lab",
            "lecture_room",
            "hyflex_smart_room",
            "dean_and_secretary",
            "cod_office",
            "faculty_room",
        }
        missing = expected_archetypes - room_types
        if missing:
            errors.append(f"Missing expected 18-room archetypes from [D-06]: {missing}")

        # Check total connected power is positive
        if self.total_connected_kw() <= 0.0:
            errors.append("Total connected load must be positive.")

        # Check tier distribution
        pcts = self.tier_breakdown_pct()
        if pcts.get(1, 0.0) <= 0.0:
            errors.append("Tier 1 life safety load must be greater than 0%.")

        return errors
```

### `src/ccis_sim/demand/mock_bills.py`

*299 lines. Synthetic monthly electricity bill generator calibrated against CCIS equipment audit.*

```python
"""Synthetic monthly electricity bill generator calibrated against CCIS equipment audit.

Conforms to specifications locked in:
- docs/prompts/CORE-003-PROMPT.md
- docs/context/complete-context.md §3 (Public & synthetic data only, clearly labelled)
- docs/decisions/decision-log.md [D-03] (Zero institutional data requests)
- docs/decisions/decision-log.md [D-10] (Tiers 1, 2, 3 load shedding classification)
- docs/decisions/decision-log.md [D-16] (CCIS equipment audit & synthetic billing calibration)
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import calendar
import csv
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Mapping, Sequence

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from ccis_sim.demand.equipment import EquipmentAudit


# Standard higher-education academic operational calendar weights for Philippine State Universities (MMSU):
DEFAULT_ACADEMIC_WEIGHTS: dict[int, float] = {
    1: 0.90,   # January: Second semester resumption (~early Jan)
    2: 1.00,   # February: Full semester academic operations
    3: 1.00,   # March: Full semester academic operations (hot season onset)
    4: 1.00,   # April: Full semester academic operations (peak summer heat)
    5: 0.90,   # May: Final examinations and commencement activities
    6: 0.65,   # June: Midyear / summer classes and faculty research
    7: 0.65,   # July: Midyear / summer classes and departmental maintenance
    8: 0.90,   # August: Enrollment, freshmen orientation, semester opening
    9: 1.00,   # September: Full first semester academic operations
    10: 1.00,  # October: Full first semester academic operations (midterms)
    11: 1.00,  # November: Full first semester academic operations
    12: 0.80,  # December: Semestral conclusion and holiday break
}

# Empirical monthly cooling multipliers reflecting Batac, Ilocos Norte Type I tropical climate:
DEFAULT_COOLING_MULTIPLIERS: dict[int, float] = {
    1: 0.88,   # January: Amihan cool dry period
    2: 0.95,   # February: Rising daytime insolation
    3: 1.18,   # March: Dry summer onset, intense thermal load
    4: 1.25,   # April: Peak extreme heat in Ilocos Norte (>36°C)
    5: 1.20,   # May: Severe heat index, early convective rains
    6: 1.05,   # June: Southwest monsoon onset, high humidity
    7: 1.00,   # July: Rainy monsoon season, moderated dry-bulb
    8: 0.98,   # August: Peak tropical cyclone & rainfall period
    9: 1.00,   # September: Monsoon transition
    10: 0.98,  # October: Post-monsoon clearing
    11: 0.92,  # November: Amihan cool air masses begin
    12: 0.85,  # December: Coolest dry month
}


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SyntheticMonthlyBill:
    """Synthetic monthly electricity bill record clearly marked as mock data.

    This record represents a calibrated mock utility bill for the MMSU CCIS
    facility, used strictly for model calibration and validation in the absence
    of institutional utility bills per decision [D-03].
    """

    month: int
    month_name: str
    billing_period_start: str
    billing_period_end: str
    days: int
    weekdays: int
    weekends: int
    total_kwh: float
    peak_kw: float
    tier1_kwh: float
    tier2_kwh: float
    tier3_kwh: float
    eui_kwh_per_m2: float
    academic_factor: float
    cooling_multiplier: float
    is_synthetic: bool = True
    data_origin: str = "SYNTHETIC_MOCK_CALIBRATED_AUDIT"
    notes: str = ""

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.month < 1 or self.month > 12:
            raise ValueError(f"month must be 1..12, got {self.month}")
        if self.total_kwh < 0.0:
            raise ValueError(f"total_kwh must be non-negative, got {self.total_kwh}")
        if self.peak_kw < 0.0:
            raise ValueError(f"peak_kw must be non-negative, got {self.peak_kw}")
        if not self.is_synthetic:
            raise ValueError("is_synthetic must remain True to prevent confusion with real data.")

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Convert bill to a dictionary."""
        return asdict(self)


class SyntheticBillGenerator:
    """Generates 12 monthly synthetic electricity bills calibrated against an equipment audit.

    Incorporates academic calendar weighting, seasonal cooling degree multipliers,
    load tier separation, unoccupied nighttime baseload (DEM-001.3), and realistic meter measurement noise.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        audit: EquipmentAudit,
        floor_area_m2: float = 2500.0,
        seed: int | None = 42,
        academic_weights: dict[int, float] | None = None,
        cooling_multipliers: dict[int, float] | None = None,
        standby_power_kw: float = 8.0,
    ) -> None:
        self.audit = audit
        self.floor_area_m2 = floor_area_m2 if floor_area_m2 > 0 else audit.floor_area_m2
        self.seed = seed
        self.academic_weights = academic_weights or DEFAULT_ACADEMIC_WEIGHTS.copy()
        self.cooling_multipliers = cooling_multipliers or DEFAULT_COOLING_MULTIPLIERS.copy()
        self.standby_power_kw = standby_power_kw

    def generate_annual_bills(
        self,
        year: int = 2026,
        noise_std: float = 0.025,
    ) -> list[SyntheticMonthlyBill]:
        """Generate 12 monthly synthetic bills for the specified calendar year.

        Args:
            year: Calendar year (default 2026).
            noise_std: Relative standard deviation of Gaussian meter noise (default 2.5%).

        Returns:
            List of 12 SyntheticMonthlyBill records.
        """
        rng = np.random.default_rng(self.seed)
        df = self.audit.to_dataframe()

        # Separate items by tier and cooling attribute:
        t1_items = df[df["tier"] == 1]
        t2_247 = df[(df["tier"] == 2) & (df["hours_per_day"] == 24.0)]
        t2_active = df[(df["tier"] == 2) & (df["hours_per_day"] < 24.0)]
        t3_cool = df[(df["tier"] == 3) & (df["is_cooling"])]
        t3_noncool = df[(df["tier"] == 3) & (~df["is_cooling"])]

        # Base daily energy rates:
        t1_daily_base = (t1_items["total_kw"] * 24.0 * t1_items["duty_cycle"]).sum()
        t2_247_daily = (t2_247["total_kw"] * 24.0 * t2_247["duty_cycle"]).sum()

        bills: list[SyntheticMonthlyBill] = []

        for m in range(1, 13):
            num_days = calendar.monthrange(year, m)[1]
            weekdays = sum(1 for d in range(1, num_days + 1) if calendar.weekday(year, m, d) < 5)
            saturdays = sum(1 for d in range(1, num_days + 1) if calendar.weekday(year, m, d) == 5)
            sundays = num_days - weekdays - saturdays
            weekends = saturdays + sundays

            acad = self.academic_weights.get(m, 1.0)
            cool = self.cooling_multipliers.get(m, 1.0)

            # Apply pseudo-random noise factor to simulate billing meter variability:
            noise = float(rng.normal(1.0, noise_std)) if noise_std > 0 else 1.0

            # Tier 1 (constant 24/7/365 life safety & core monitoring):
            t1_month = t1_daily_base * num_days * noise

            # Tier 2 (essential IT and telecom):
            # Weekday: 24/7 loads + active office hours scaled by academic factor
            t2_weekday = t2_247_daily + (
                t2_active["total_kw"] * t2_active["hours_per_day"] * t2_active["duty_cycle"] * acad
            ).sum()
            # Saturday: 24/7 loads + reduced graduate/capstone office access (40%)
            t2_saturday = t2_247_daily + (
                t2_active["total_kw"] * 8.0 * t2_active["duty_cycle"] * acad * 0.40
            ).sum()
            # Sunday: 24/7 baseline only
            t2_sunday = t2_247_daily
            t2_month = (weekdays * t2_weekday + saturdays * t2_saturday + sundays * t2_sunday) * noise

            # Tier 3 (comfort cooling & high-density student lab loads):
            # Weekday: active daytime hours (cooling scaled by cool * acad, non-cooling by acad)
            # plus 12 hours of nighttime standby baseload per DEM-001.3
            t3_weekday = (
                t3_cool["total_kw"] * t3_cool["hours_per_day"] * t3_cool["duty_cycle"] * cool * acad
            ).sum() + (
                t3_noncool["total_kw"] * t3_noncool["hours_per_day"] * t3_noncool["duty_cycle"] * acad
            ).sum() + (self.standby_power_kw * 12.0)

            # Saturday: partial lab utilization (~45% student, ~40% AC) + 16h nighttime standby
            t3_saturday = (
                t3_cool["total_kw"] * 8.0 * t3_cool["duty_cycle"] * cool * acad * 0.40
            ).sum() + (
                t3_noncool["total_kw"] * 8.0 * t3_noncool["duty_cycle"] * acad * 0.45
            ).sum() + (self.standby_power_kw * 16.0)

            # Sunday: unoccupied facility standby load (24h)
            t3_sunday = self.standby_power_kw * 24.0
            t3_month = (weekdays * t3_weekday + saturdays * t3_saturday + sundays * t3_sunday) * noise

            total_kwh = t1_month + t2_month + t3_month

            # Estimated coincident peak demand in kW:
            peak_t1 = t1_items["total_kw"].sum()
            peak_t2 = t2_247["total_kw"].sum() + (t2_active["total_kw"].sum() * acad * 0.85)
            peak_t3 = (
                (t3_cool["total_kw"].sum() * cool * 0.80) + (t3_noncool["total_kw"].sum() * 0.82)
            ) * acad
            coincident_peak_kw = (peak_t1 + peak_t2 + peak_t3) * noise

            eui_mo = total_kwh / self.floor_area_m2
            start_date = f"{year}-{m:02d}-01"
            end_date = f"{year}-{m:02d}-{num_days:02d}"

            notes = (
                f"Synthetic mock bill for {calendar.month_name[m]} {year}; "
                f"acad_factor={acad:.2f}, cool_mult={cool:.2f}"
            )

            bills.append(
                SyntheticMonthlyBill(
                    month=m,
                    month_name=calendar.month_name[m],
                    billing_period_start=start_date,
                    billing_period_end=end_date,
                    days=num_days,
                    weekdays=weekdays,
                    weekends=weekends,
                    total_kwh=round(total_kwh, 2),
                    peak_kw=round(coincident_peak_kw, 2),
                    tier1_kwh=round(t1_month, 2),
                    tier2_kwh=round(t2_month, 2),
                    tier3_kwh=round(t3_month, 2),
                    eui_kwh_per_m2=round(eui_mo, 2),
                    academic_factor=acad,
                    cooling_multiplier=cool,
                    is_synthetic=True,
                    data_origin="SYNTHETIC_MOCK_CALIBRATED_AUDIT",
                    notes=notes,
                )
            )

        return bills

    def compute_annual_eui(self, bills: Sequence[SyntheticMonthlyBill]) -> float:
        """Compute the annual Energy Use Intensity (kWh/m²·yr) from a 12-month bill set."""
        if len(bills) != 12:
            raise ValueError(f"Expected 12 monthly bills, got {len(bills)}")
        total_annual_kwh = sum(b.total_kwh for b in bills)
        return total_annual_kwh / self.floor_area_m2

    @staticmethod
    def to_dataframe(bills: Sequence[SyntheticMonthlyBill]) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Convert a sequence of SyntheticMonthlyBill records to a pandas DataFrame."""
        return pd.DataFrame([b.to_dict() for b in bills])

    def save_to_csv(self, bills: Sequence[SyntheticMonthlyBill], output_path: Path | str) -> Path:
        """Save synthetic monthly bills to CSV with explicit mock provenance metadata."""
        path = Path(output_path)
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        df = self.to_dataframe(bills)
        df.to_csv(path, index=False)
        return path

    @staticmethod
    def load_from_csv(csv_path: Path | str) -> list[SyntheticMonthlyBill]:
        """Load and parse previously saved synthetic monthly bills from CSV."""
        path = Path(csv_path)
        if not path.exists():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Synthetic bills file not found: {path}")

        bills: list[SyntheticMonthlyBill] = []
        with open(path, mode="r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            reader = csv.DictReader(f)
            for row in reader:
                bill = SyntheticMonthlyBill(
                    month=int(row["month"]),
                    month_name=row["month_name"].strip(),
                    billing_period_start=row["billing_period_start"].strip(),
                    billing_period_end=row["billing_period_end"].strip(),
                    days=int(row["days"]),
                    weekdays=int(row["weekdays"]),
                    weekends=int(row["weekends"]),
                    total_kwh=float(row["total_kwh"]),
                    peak_kw=float(row["peak_kw"]),
                    tier1_kwh=float(row["tier1_kwh"]),
                    tier2_kwh=float(row["tier2_kwh"]),
                    tier3_kwh=float(row["tier3_kwh"]),
                    eui_kwh_per_m2=float(row["eui_kwh_per_m2"]),
                    academic_factor=float(row["academic_factor"]),
                    cooling_multiplier=float(row["cooling_multiplier"]),
                    is_synthetic=row["is_synthetic"].lower() in ("true", "1"),
                    data_origin=row["data_origin"].strip(),
                    notes=row.get("notes", "").strip(),
                )
                bills.append(bill)
        return bills
```

### `src/ccis_sim/demand/non_cooling.py`

*105 lines. Diurnal non-cooling electrical demand model for CCIS facility.*

```python
"""Diurnal non-cooling electrical demand model for CCIS facility.

Aggregates bottom-up lighting, IT networking, office computing, and plug loads
across priority tiers 1 to 3 based on the equipment inventory audit and hourly
occupancy schedules, conforming to:
- docs/prompts/DEM-001-PROMPT.md
- docs/context/phase2-interface-contract.md
- docs/decisions/decision-log.md [D-27]
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np

from ccis_sim.core.interfaces import OccupancySchedule, TieredDemand
from ccis_sim.core.models import Building
from ccis_sim.demand.equipment import EquipmentAudit


def compute_non_cooling_demand(
    schedule: OccupancySchedule,
    audit: EquipmentAudit,
    building: Building,
) -> TieredDemand:
    r"""Compute hourly non-cooling electrical demand decomposed across priority tiers 1 to 3.

    Conforms strictly to docs/context/phase2-interface-contract.md:
    `compute_non_cooling_demand(schedule: OccupancySchedule, audit: EquipmentAudit, building: Building) -> TieredDemand`

    Load aggregation logic ([ASSUMPTION / [D-27]]):
    1. Filter out all cooling equipment (`item.is_cooling == False`); cooling response is
       exclusively evaluated in DEM-002.
    2. Classify items into:
       - Continuous 24/7 loads (`hours_per_day >= 24.0`): constant uninterrupted operation
         (e.g., Tier 1 life-safety signs/alarms/CCTV/stairwell; Tier 2 IDF switches and core routers).
       - Occupancy-driven loads (`hours_per_day < 24.0`): scaled linearly with hourly
         `occupancy_fraction` (e.g., PC labs, classroom lighting troffers, faculty workstations).
    3. Calculate total daytime peak non-cooling demand:
       $P_{\text{peak}} = P_{\text{cont, total}} + P_{\text{occ\_peak, total}}$.
    4. Unoccupied Baseload Rule without Double-Counting:
       Target unoccupied non-cooling load is 10% of daytime peak ($0.10 \times P_{\text{peak}}$).
       Continuous 24/7 items already draw $P_{\text{cont, total}}$. The residual standby margin:
       $P_{\text{standby}} = \max(0.0, 0.10 \times P_{\text{peak}} - P_{\text{cont, total}})$.
       This residual represents parasitic standby plug loads (quiescent draw of sleep-state PCs,
       monitors, chargers, and appliances) and is allocated to Tier 3 (sheddable load).
       As occupancy rises ($f \in [0, 1]$), equipment wakes to operational load, and standby
       transitions to active draw: $(1.0 - f) \times P_{\text{standby}}$.
    5. Hourly Tier Composition:
       - Tier 1: $P_{\text{cont}, 1} + f \times P_{\text{occ\_peak}, 1}$
       - Tier 2: $P_{\text{cont}, 2} + f \times P_{\text{occ\_peak}, 2}$
       - Tier 3: $P_{\text{cont}, 3} + f \times P_{\text{occ\_peak}, 3} + (1.0 - f) \times P_{\text{standby}}$

    Args:
        schedule: OccupancySchedule containing hourly occupancy_fraction array.
        audit: EquipmentAudit inventory containing audited building loads.
        building: Building envelope definition.

    Returns:
        TieredDemand instance with tier1_kwh, tier2_kwh, tier3_kwh arrays of length N.
    """
    n = len(schedule)
    if n == 0:
        raise ValueError("OccupancySchedule must not be empty.")

    # Filter for non-cooling electrical equipment
    non_cooling_items = [item for item in audit.items if not item.is_cooling]

    # Pre-accumulate continuous loads and full-occupancy peak loads per tier
    # Units: kW = kWh/h at Δt = 1.0 h
    p_cont = {1: 0.0, 2: 0.0, 3: 0.0}
    p_occ_peak = {1: 0.0, 2: 0.0, 3: 0.0}

    for item in non_cooling_items:
        # Effective kW accounts for duty cycle diversity during operational hours
        effective_kw = item.count * item.rated_kw * item.duty_cycle
        if item.hours_per_day >= 24.0:
            p_cont[item.tier] += effective_kw
        else:
            p_occ_peak[item.tier] += effective_kw

    p_cont_total = p_cont[1] + p_cont[2] + p_cont[3]
    p_occ_total = p_occ_peak[1] + p_occ_peak[2] + p_occ_peak[3]
    p_peak = p_cont_total + p_occ_total

    # Baseload target is 10% of daytime peak non-cooling demand per DEM-001.3
    p_unoccupied_target = 0.10 * p_peak

    # Avoid double-counting: subtract continuous 24/7 loads from 10% baseload target
    p_standby_residual = max(0.0, p_unoccupied_target - p_cont_total)

    f_occ = schedule.occupancy_fraction

    # Vectorized hourly tier load generation
    tier1_arr = np.full(n, p_cont[1], dtype=np.float64)
    if p_occ_peak[1] > 0.0:
        tier1_arr += f_occ * p_occ_peak[1]

    tier2_arr = p_cont[2] + f_occ * p_occ_peak[2]
    tier3_arr = p_cont[3] + f_occ * p_occ_peak[3] + (1.0 - f_occ) * p_standby_residual

    return TieredDemand(
        tier1_kwh=tier1_arr,
        tier2_kwh=tier2_arr,
        tier3_kwh=tier3_arr,
    )
```

### `src/ccis_sim/demand/schedule.py`

*332 lines. Academic calendar and building occupancy schedule engine.*

```python
"""Academic calendar and building occupancy schedule engine.

Generates hourly occupancy fraction profiles and operational state flags
(class day, break, holiday) for the CCIS building conforming to:
- docs/prompts/DEM-001-PROMPT.md
- docs/context/phase2-interface-contract.md
- docs/decisions/decision-log.md [D-27]
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import datetime
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Sequence

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from ccis_sim.core.interfaces import OccupancySchedule


# ==============================================================================
# Philippine Statutory Holidays (Fixture Years 2016 and 2019)
# Reference: Proclamation No. 555 (2019) and Proclamation No. 1105 / 1107 (2016)
# ==============================================================================

# Philippine regular and special non-working holidays for calendar year 2019
PHILIPPINES_HOLIDAYS_2019: frozenset[datetime.date] = frozenset(
    [
        datetime.date(2019, 1, 1),   # New Year's Day (Regular)
        datetime.date(2019, 2, 5),   # Chinese Lunar New Year (Special)
        datetime.date(2019, 2, 25),  # EDSA People Power Revolution (Special)
        datetime.date(2019, 4, 9),   # Araw ng Kagitingan / Day of Valor (Regular)
        datetime.date(2019, 4, 18),  # Maundy Thursday (Regular)
        datetime.date(2019, 4, 19),  # Good Friday (Regular)
        datetime.date(2019, 4, 20),  # Black Saturday (Special)
        datetime.date(2019, 5, 1),   # Labor Day (Regular)
        datetime.date(2019, 6, 5),   # Eid'l Fitr (Regular, Proclamation 729)
        datetime.date(2019, 6, 12),  # Independence Day (Regular)
        datetime.date(2019, 8, 12),  # Eid'l Adha (Regular, Proclamation 789)
        datetime.date(2019, 8, 21),  # Ninoy Aquino Day (Special)
        datetime.date(2019, 8, 26),  # National Heroes Day (Regular, last Mon of Aug)
        datetime.date(2019, 11, 1),  # All Saints' Day (Special)
        datetime.date(2019, 11, 2),  # All Souls' Day (Special)
        datetime.date(2019, 11, 30), # Bonifacio Day (Regular)
        datetime.date(2019, 12, 8),  # Feast of the Immaculate Conception (Special)
        datetime.date(2019, 12, 24), # Christmas Eve (Special)
        datetime.date(2019, 12, 25), # Christmas Day (Regular)
        datetime.date(2019, 12, 30), # Rizal Day (Regular)
        datetime.date(2019, 12, 31), # Last Day of the Year (Special)
    ]
)

# Philippine regular and special non-working holidays for calendar year 2016 (leap year)
PHILIPPINES_HOLIDAYS_2016: frozenset[datetime.date] = frozenset(
    [
        datetime.date(2016, 1, 1),   # New Year's Day (Regular)
        datetime.date(2016, 1, 2),   # Additional special non-working day
        datetime.date(2016, 2, 8),   # Chinese New Year (Special)
        datetime.date(2016, 2, 25),  # EDSA Revolution Anniversary (Special)
        datetime.date(2016, 3, 24),  # Maundy Thursday (Regular)
        datetime.date(2016, 3, 25),  # Good Friday (Regular)
        datetime.date(2016, 3, 26),  # Black Saturday (Special)
        datetime.date(2016, 4, 9),   # Araw ng Kagitingan (Regular)
        datetime.date(2016, 5, 1),   # Labor Day (Regular)
        datetime.date(2016, 5, 9),   # National Election Day (Special)
        datetime.date(2016, 6, 12),  # Independence Day (Regular)
        datetime.date(2016, 7, 6),   # Eid'l Fitr (Regular)
        datetime.date(2016, 8, 21),  # Ninoy Aquino Day (Special)
        datetime.date(2016, 8, 29),  # National Heroes Day (Regular)
        datetime.date(2016, 9, 12),  # Eid'l Adha (Regular)
        datetime.date(2016, 10, 31), # Additional special non-working day
        datetime.date(2016, 11, 1),  # All Saints' Day (Special)
        datetime.date(2016, 11, 30), # Bonifacio Day (Regular)
        datetime.date(2016, 12, 24), # Special non-working day
        datetime.date(2016, 12, 25), # Christmas Day (Regular)
        datetime.date(2016, 12, 26), # Additional special non-working day
        datetime.date(2016, 12, 30), # Rizal Day (Regular)
        datetime.date(2016, 12, 31), # Last Day of the Year (Special)
    ]
)


def get_default_philippine_holidays(year: int) -> frozenset[datetime.date]:
    """Return statutory holiday set for specified calendar year.

    For fixture years 2019 and 2016, returns exact historical gazetted holidays
    (including lunar/movable feasts Eid'l Fitr, Eid'l Adha, and Holy Week).
    For other years, computes fixed-date annual holidays plus National Heroes Day.
    """
    if year == 2019:
        return PHILIPPINES_HOLIDAYS_2019
    if year == 2016:
        return PHILIPPINES_HOLIDAYS_2016

    # Fixed-date statutory holidays
    fixed = {
        datetime.date(year, 1, 1),    # New Year's Day
        datetime.date(year, 2, 25),   # EDSA Revolution
        datetime.date(year, 4, 9),    # Araw ng Kagitingan
        datetime.date(year, 5, 1),    # Labor Day
        datetime.date(year, 6, 12),   # Independence Day
        datetime.date(year, 8, 21),   # Ninoy Aquino Day
        datetime.date(year, 11, 1),   # All Saints' Day
        datetime.date(year, 11, 30),  # Bonifacio Day
        datetime.date(year, 12, 8),   # Immaculate Conception
        datetime.date(year, 12, 24),  # Christmas Eve
        datetime.date(year, 12, 25),  # Christmas Day
        datetime.date(year, 12, 30),  # Rizal Day
        datetime.date(year, 12, 31),  # New Year's Eve
    }

    # National Heroes Day: last Monday of August
    last_day_august = datetime.date(year, 8, 31)
    offset = (last_day_august.weekday() - 0) % 7  # Monday is weekday 0
    nat_heroes_day = datetime.date(year, 8, 31 - offset)
    fixed.add(nat_heroes_day)

    return frozenset(fixed)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AcademicCalendar:
    """Academic calendar boundaries and holiday definitions for Philippine state university.

    Default schedule parameters reflect standard Mariano Marcos State University (MMSU)
    semester and term break alignments ([ASSUMPTION / [D-27]]):
    - Semester 1 (Aug 15 - Dec 15): 18-week academic term
    - Winter / Semestral Break (Dec 16 - Jan 14): semestral vacation and holiday closure
    - Semester 2 (Jan 15 - May 25): 18-week academic term
    - Summer / Midyear Break (May 26 - Aug 14): academic vacation, faculty research, midyear maintenance

    Attributes:
        year: Reference weather year (default 2019).
        sem1_start: (month, day) for Semester 1 commencement (default (8, 15)).
        sem1_end: (month, day) for Semester 1 conclusion (default (12, 15)).
        sem2_start: (month, day) for Semester 2 commencement (default (1, 15)).
        sem2_end: (month, day) for Semester 2 conclusion (default (5, 25)).
        holidays: Set of holiday dates; defaults to Philippine national gazetted holidays.
        break_weekday_admin_ratio: Skeleton faculty/staff occupancy during term break weekdays
            (default 0.15 per [ASSUMPTION / [D-27]]).
        break_lunch_ratio: Skeleton staff occupancy during lunch hour on break weekdays
            (default 0.05 per [ASSUMPTION / [D-27]]).
    """

    year: int = 2019
    sem1_start: tuple[int, int] = (8, 15)
    sem1_end: tuple[int, int] = (12, 15)
    sem2_start: tuple[int, int] = (1, 15)
    sem2_end: tuple[int, int] = (5, 25)
    holidays: frozenset[datetime.date] | None = None
    break_weekday_admin_ratio: float = 0.15
    break_lunch_ratio: float = 0.05

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.holidays is None:
            object.__setattr__(self, "holidays", get_default_philippine_holidays(self.year))
        else:
            object.__setattr__(
                self,
                "holidays",
                frozenset(
                    h if isinstance(h, datetime.date) else pd.to_datetime(h).date()
                    for h in self.holidays
                ),
            )

        if not (0.0 <= self.break_weekday_admin_ratio <= 1.0):
            raise ValueError(
                f"break_weekday_admin_ratio must be in [0, 1], got {self.break_weekday_admin_ratio}"
            )
        if not (0.0 <= self.break_lunch_ratio <= 1.0):
            raise ValueError(f"break_lunch_ratio must be in [0, 1], got {self.break_lunch_ratio}")

    def is_holiday(self, d: datetime.date) -> bool:
        """Check if date is a statutory or special non-working holiday."""
        return d in self.holidays

    def is_semester(self, d: datetime.date) -> bool:
        """Check if date falls within regular semester terms (Sem 1 or Sem 2)."""
        sem1_s = datetime.date(d.year, self.sem1_start[0], self.sem1_start[1])
        sem1_e = datetime.date(d.year, self.sem1_end[0], self.sem1_end[1])
        sem2_s = datetime.date(d.year, self.sem2_start[0], self.sem2_start[1])
        sem2_e = datetime.date(d.year, self.sem2_end[0], self.sem2_end[1])

        return (sem1_s <= d <= sem1_e) or (sem2_s <= d <= sem2_e)

    def is_break(self, d: datetime.date) -> bool:
        """Check if date falls within academic break (winter or summer break)."""
        return not self.is_semester(d)


def build_occupancy_schedule(
    timestamps: pd.Series | Sequence[Any],
    *,
    calendar: AcademicCalendar | None = None,
) -> OccupancySchedule:
    """Build academic building occupancy profile and calendar operational state.

    Conforms strictly to docs/context/phase2-interface-contract.md:
    `build_occupancy_schedule(timestamps: pd.Series, *, calendar: AcademicCalendar | None = None) -> OccupancySchedule`

    Occupancy fraction conventions ([ASSUMPTION / [D-27]]):
    - Regular Class Weekdays (Mon-Fri, non-holiday during semester):
        00:00-07:00: 0.00 (closed)
        07:00-08:00: 0.20 (early arrival / prep)
        08:00-12:00: 1.00 (peak morning lecture/labs)
        12:00-13:00: 0.60 (lunch dip)
        13:00-18:00: 0.95 (afternoon lecture/labs)
        18:00-20:00: 0.30 (evening graduate/extension classes)
        20:00-24:00: 0.00 (closed)
    - Regular Class Saturdays (Sat, non-holiday during semester):
        00:00-08:00: 0.00
        08:00-12:00: 0.50 (morning lab sessions / NSTP)
        12:00-13:00: 0.20 (lunch dip)
        13:00-17:00: 0.30 (afternoon lab sessions)
        17:00-24:00: 0.00
    - Sundays and Gazetted Holidays:
        0.00 all hours (building fully closed)
    - Academic Break Weekdays (Mon-Fri during summer or semestral break, non-holiday):
        08:00-12:00: break_weekday_admin_ratio (0.15 skeleton faculty/admin presence)
        12:00-13:00: break_lunch_ratio (0.05 lunch hour)
        13:00-17:00: break_weekday_admin_ratio (0.15 afternoon office presence)
        all other hours: 0.00
    - Academic Break Weekends (Sat-Sun during break):
        0.00 all hours

    Args:
        timestamps: Series or sequence of ISO strings, DatetimeIndex, or datetime objects.
        calendar: AcademicCalendar instance; if None, defaults to AcademicCalendar(year=...).

    Returns:
        OccupancySchedule container with occupancy_fraction, is_class_day, is_break,
        and is_holiday numpy arrays of length N.
    """
    ts_series = pd.to_datetime(timestamps)
    n = len(ts_series)
    if n == 0:
        raise ValueError("timestamps sequence must not be empty.")

    # Determine reference year from timestamps if calendar not provided
    if calendar is None:
        ref_year = int(ts_series[0].year) if hasattr(ts_series, "iloc") else int(ts_series[0].year)
        cal = AcademicCalendar(year=ref_year)
    else:
        cal = calendar

    occ_arr = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    class_day_arr = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)
    break_arr = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)
    holiday_arr = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)

    # Convert to Python datetime series for fast component access
    dt_index = pd.DatetimeIndex(ts_series)
    dates = dt_index.date
    hours = dt_index.hour
    weekdays = dt_index.weekday  # 0=Monday, 5=Saturday, 6=Sunday

    # Precompute unique dates state to vectorize day-level flags
    unique_dates = np.unique(dates)
    day_is_holiday: dict[datetime.date, bool] = {d: cal.is_holiday(d) for d in unique_dates}
    day_is_break: dict[datetime.date, bool] = {d: cal.is_break(d) for d in unique_dates}
    day_is_sem: dict[datetime.date, bool] = {d: cal.is_semester(d) for d in unique_dates}

    for i in range(n):
        d = dates[i]
        h = hours[i]
        wd = weekdays[i]

        hol = day_is_holiday[d]
        brk = day_is_break[d]
        sem = day_is_sem[d]

        holiday_arr[i] = hol
        break_arr[i] = brk

        # Class day: scheduled semester day, not holiday, not Sunday (Mon-Sat during semester)
        is_class_d = sem and (not hol) and (wd < 6)
        class_day_arr[i] = is_class_d

        if hol or wd == 6:
            # Building closed on holidays and Sundays
            occ_arr[i] = 0.0
        elif is_class_d:
            # Regular semester schedule
            if wd < 5:
                # Weekdays (Mon-Fri)
                if h < 7 or h >= 20:
                    occ_arr[i] = 0.0
                elif h == 7:
                    occ_arr[i] = 0.20
                elif 8 <= h < 12:
                    occ_arr[i] = 1.00
                elif h == 12:
                    occ_arr[i] = 0.60
                elif 13 <= h < 18:
                    occ_arr[i] = 0.95
                elif 18 <= h < 20:
                    occ_arr[i] = 0.30
            else:
                # Saturday
                if h < 8 or h >= 17:
                    occ_arr[i] = 0.0
                elif 8 <= h < 12:
                    occ_arr[i] = 0.50
                elif h == 12:
                    occ_arr[i] = 0.20
                elif 13 <= h < 17:
                    occ_arr[i] = 0.30
        elif brk:
            # Term break schedule
            if wd < 5:
                # Break weekday: administrative presence
                if 8 <= h < 12 or 13 <= h < 17:
                    occ_arr[i] = cal.break_weekday_admin_ratio
                elif h == 12:
                    occ_arr[i] = cal.break_lunch_ratio
                else:
                    occ_arr[i] = 0.0
            else:
                # Break weekend (Sat-Sun)
                occ_arr[i] = 0.0
        else:
            # Fallback for non-class, non-break, non-holiday weekday
            occ_arr[i] = 0.0

    return OccupancySchedule(
        occupancy_fraction=occ_arr,
        is_class_day=class_day_arr,
        is_break=break_arr,
        is_holiday=holiday_arr,
    )
```

### `src/ccis_sim/integration/baseline.py`

*297 lines. Baseline grid-only continuous simulation engine (CORE-005 Phase 1 Exit Gate).*

```python
"""Baseline grid-only continuous simulation engine (CORE-005 Phase 1 Exit Gate).

Implements the minimal grid-only, zero-solar, zero-battery simulation across 8,760 hours,
using a placeholder demand schedule calibrated to 159.41 kWh/m²·yr per:
- docs/prompts/CORE-005-PROMPT.md
- docs/decisions/decision-log.md [D-05], [D-06], [D-07], [D-16], [D-19], [D-20], [D-21], [D-23]
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Sequence

import pandas as pd

from ccis_sim.core.models import Building, HourlyRecord, LoadTier
from ccis_sim.demand.equipment import EquipmentAudit
from ccis_sim.weather.loader import load_weather_fixture

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# Fixed calibration factor derived on 2019 baseline weather fixture to hit 159.41 kWh/m²·yr (398,525 kWh)
CALIBRATION_FACTOR_2019 = 1.2285756306528773

# Assumed average coincident cooling equipment operational duty factor during daytime occupied hours.
# Represents that on average ~35% of the total 77.20 kW connected AC capacity is actively compressor-cycling
# when ambient temperature exceeds the cooling base setpoint (24.0°C per [D-07]).
DEFAULT_COOLING_DUTY_FACTOR = 0.35


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class BaselineMetrics:
    """Summary key performance indicators for baseline grid-only simulation."""

    annual_energy_kwh: float
    peak_demand_kw: float
    peak_timestamp: str
    peak_hour: int
    eui_kwh_per_m2_year: float
    load_factor: float
    monthly_energy_kwh: dict[int, float]

    def summary(self) -> str:
        """Format a human-readable summary of baseline performance metrics."""
        lines = [
            "=== Baseline Simulation Metrics ===",
            f"Annual Energy Consumption: {self.annual_energy_kwh:,.2f} kWh",
            f"Annual Energy Use Intensity (EUI): {self.eui_kwh_per_m2_year:.2f} kWh/m²·yr",
            f"Peak Electrical Demand: {self.peak_demand_kw:.2f} kW (Hour {self.peak_hour} at {self.peak_timestamp})",
            f"Annual Load Factor: {self.load_factor:.4f} ({self.load_factor*100:.2f}%)",
            "Monthly Consumption Breakdown:",
        ]
        month_names = [
            "Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun",
            "Jul", "Aug", "Sep", "Oct", "Nov", "Dec",
        ]
        for m in range(1, 13):
            kwh = self.monthly_energy_kwh.get(m, 0.0)
            lines.append(f"  {month_names[m-1]}: {kwh:,.2f} kWh")
        return "\n".join(lines)


def compute_placeholder_demand(
    weather_df: pd.DataFrame,
    audit: EquipmentAudit,
    building: Building,
    calibration_factor: float = CALIBRATION_FACTOR_2019,
) -> list[float]:
    """Compute hourly electrical demand time-series using a lightweight placeholder model.

    NOTE per [D-23]: This is a deliberately simple placeholder model for Phase 1 baseline
    smoke-testing. Full schedule diversity and psychrometric heat response will be
    implemented by Phase 2 tracks DEM-001 and DEM-002.

    Model components:
    1. Baseload: 10% continuous 24/7 standby power (IDF switches, network, egress lighting).
    2. Occupancy Profile:
       - Weekdays (Mon-Fri): 08:00-12:00 (45%), 12:00-13:00 (30%), 13:00-17:00 (50%), 17:00-19:00 (20%).
       - Saturdays: 08:00-13:00 (15%).
       - Sundays/Nights: 0% occupancy (baseload only).
    3. Cooling Temperature Sensitivity:
       During active daytime hours (07:00-18:00 weekdays, 08:00-13:00 Saturdays), when T_amb > 24.0°C,
       cooling electrical load increases at +4.0%/°C per [D-07].
    4. Calibration:
       Pre-calibrated multiplier applied so 2019 annual total matches the 159.41 kWh/m²·yr
       target established in [D-16]/[D-19] (398,525.0 kWh).

    Args:
        weather_df: Hourly weather DataFrame containing 'timestamp' and 't_amb'.
        audit: CCIS EquipmentAudit instance providing connected power ratings.
        building: Building envelope definition providing t_base_cooling_c and cooling_sensitivity.
        calibration_factor: Multiplier to calibrate demand to target EUI (default CALIBRATION_FACTOR_2019).

    Returns:
        List of hourly gross demand in kWh for all rows in weather_df.
    """
    total_connected_kw = audit.total_connected_kw()
    cooling_connected_kw = sum(item.total_kw for item in audit.items if item.is_cooling)

    timestamps = pd.to_datetime(weather_df["timestamp"])
    hours = timestamps.dt.hour.values
    dayofweek = timestamps.dt.dayofweek.values
    temps = weather_df["t_amb"].values


    demand_series: list[float] = []

    for h, dow, temp in zip(hours, dayofweek, temps):
        f_base = 0.10  # 10% standby baseload
        f_occ = 0.0

        if dow < 5:  # Monday to Friday
            if 8 <= h < 12:
                f_occ = 0.45
            elif 12 <= h < 13:
                f_occ = 0.30
            elif 13 <= h < 17:
                f_occ = 0.50
            elif 17 <= h < 19:
                f_occ = 0.20
        elif dow == 5:  # Saturday
            if 8 <= h < 13:
                f_occ = 0.15

        # Base active schedule load (kW = kWh over 1h)
        p_sched = total_connected_kw * (f_base + f_occ)

        # Temperature-driven cooling adjustment per [D-07]
        cooling_adjustment = 0.0
        if temp > building.t_base_cooling_c and (f_occ > 0.0 or (dow < 5 and 7 <= h <= 18)):
            delta_t = temp - building.t_base_cooling_c
            cooling_adjustment = (
                cooling_connected_kw * DEFAULT_COOLING_DUTY_FACTOR * building.cooling_sensitivity_per_c * delta_t
            )

        hourly_uncalibrated = p_sched + cooling_adjustment
        hourly_demand = hourly_uncalibrated * calibration_factor
        demand_series.append(hourly_demand)

    return demand_series


def simulate_baseline(
    weather_fixture: int | str | Path | pd.DataFrame = 2019,
    equipment_audit: EquipmentAudit | Path | str | None = None,
    building: Building | None = None,
    calibration_factor: float = CALIBRATION_FACTOR_2019,
) -> list[HourlyRecord]:
    """Execute grid-only baseline simulation emitting 8,760 canonical HourlyRecords.

    Args:
        weather_fixture: Calendar year (2016, 2019), path to weather CSV, or DataFrame.
        equipment_audit: EquipmentAudit instance or path to equipment CSV (defaults to CCIS fixture).
        building: Building envelope instance (defaults to standard Building()).
        calibration_factor: Calibration scaling multiplier.

    Returns:
        List of 8,760 HourlyRecord instances with exact physical energy balance.
    """
    # 1. Resolve inputs
    if isinstance(weather_fixture, pd.DataFrame):
        weather_df = weather_fixture.copy()
    else:
        weather_df = load_weather_fixture(weather_fixture)

    if equipment_audit is None:
        fixture_path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3] / "data/fixtures/equipment-audit-ccis.csv"
        audit = EquipmentAudit.load_from_csv(fixture_path)
    elif isinstance(equipment_audit, (str, Path)):
        audit = EquipmentAudit.load_from_csv(equipment_audit)
    else:
        audit = equipment_audit

    bldg = building or Building()

    # 2. Compute placeholder demand series
    demand_series = compute_placeholder_demand(
        weather_df=weather_df,
        audit=audit,
        building=bldg,
        calibration_factor=calibration_factor,
    )

    records: list[HourlyRecord] = []

    hours = weather_df["hour"].values
    timestamps = weather_df["timestamp"].values
    t_ambs = weather_df["t_amb"].values
    rhs = weather_df["rh"].values
    ghis = weather_df["ghi"].values

    for h, ts_raw, t_amb, rh, ghi, demand in zip(
        hours, timestamps, t_ambs, rhs, ghis, demand_series
    ):
        # Format timestamp to ISO 8601 with T
        ts = str(ts_raw).replace(" ", "T")
        demand_val = float(demand)

        # Grid-only baseline dispatch:
        # grid_import exactly equals served demand equals gross demand
        rec = HourlyRecord(
            hour=int(h),
            timestamp=ts,
            temp_ambient_c=float(t_amb),
            humidity_rel_pct=float(rh),
            ghi_w_m2=float(ghi),
            demand_gross_kwh=demand_val,
            demand_served_kwh=demand_val,
            solar_generation_kwh=0.0,
            solar_to_demand_kwh=0.0,
            solar_to_battery_kwh=0.0,
            solar_curtailed_kwh=0.0,
            battery_soc_kwh=0.0,
            battery_charge_kwh=0.0,
            battery_discharge_kwh=0.0,
            grid_import_kwh=demand_val,
            unserved_load_kwh=0.0,
            grid_available=True,
            active_load_tier=LoadTier.TIER_3_SHEDDABLE,
        )
        records.append(rec)


    return records


def compute_baseline_metrics(
    records: Sequence[HourlyRecord],
    building: Building | None = None,
) -> BaselineMetrics:
    """Calculate key headline metrics from baseline simulation records.

    Args:
        records: Sequence of HourlyRecord instances.
        building: Building instance providing gross floor area (default 2,500 m²).

    Returns:
        BaselineMetrics with annual energy, peak demand, EUI, load factor, and monthly totals.
    """
    bldg = building or Building()
    if not records:
        return BaselineMetrics(
            annual_energy_kwh=0.0,
            peak_demand_kw=0.0,
            peak_timestamp="",
            peak_hour=0,
            eui_kwh_per_m2_year=0.0,
            load_factor=0.0,
            monthly_energy_kwh={},
        )

    annual_energy = sum(r.demand_served_kwh for r in records)
    peak_rec = max(records, key=lambda r: r.demand_served_kwh)
    peak_kw = peak_rec.demand_served_kwh
    peak_ts = peak_rec.timestamp
    peak_hour = peak_rec.hour

    eui = annual_energy / bldg.gross_floor_area_m2
    total_hours = len(records)
    load_factor = annual_energy / (peak_kw * total_hours) if peak_kw > 0 and total_hours > 0 else 0.0

    # Monthly breakdown using ISO format YYYY-MM-DD
    monthly_energy: dict[int, float] = {m: 0.0 for m in range(1, 13)}
    for r in records:
        month = int(r.timestamp[5:7])
        monthly_energy[month] += r.demand_served_kwh


    return BaselineMetrics(
        annual_energy_kwh=annual_energy,
        peak_demand_kw=peak_kw,
        peak_timestamp=peak_ts,
        peak_hour=peak_hour,
        eui_kwh_per_m2_year=eui,
        load_factor=load_factor,
        monthly_energy_kwh=monthly_energy,
    )


def run_baseline_simulation(
    weather_fixture: int | str | Path | pd.DataFrame = 2019,
    equipment_audit: EquipmentAudit | Path | str | None = None,
    building: Building | None = None,
    calibration_factor: float = CALIBRATION_FACTOR_2019,
) -> tuple[list[HourlyRecord], BaselineMetrics]:
    """Convenience driver executing baseline simulation and returning records plus metrics."""
    bldg = building or Building()
    records = simulate_baseline(
        weather_fixture=weather_fixture,
        equipment_audit=equipment_audit,
        building=bldg,
        calibration_factor=calibration_factor,
    )
    metrics = compute_baseline_metrics(records=records, building=bldg)
    return records, metrics
```

### `src/ccis_sim/integration/engine.py`

*490 lines. Integrated time-step simulation engine for CCIS microgrid.*

```python
"""Integrated time-step simulation engine for CCIS microgrid.

Composes demand (DEM-001/002), solar generation (SOL-001/002), stochastic outages
(OUT-001), priority load shedding (OUT-002), battery dispatch dynamics (BAT-001),
and reserve constraints/idle losses (BAT-002) into a unified deterministic hourly loop.

Conforms strictly to Phase 2 interface contract (docs/context/phase2-interface-contract.md)
and architectural decisions [D-26] through [D-35].
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from collections.abc import Sequence
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Literal

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from ccis_sim.battery.dynamics import step_battery
from ccis_sim.battery.reserve import apply_idle_losses, soc_floor_kwh
from ccis_sim.core.interfaces import OutageSchedule, TieredDemand
from ccis_sim.core.models import Battery, Building, HourlyRecord, LoadTier, SolarArray
from ccis_sim.core.verification import assert_simulation_valid
from ccis_sim.demand.cooling import compute_cooling_demand
from ccis_sim.demand.equipment import EquipmentAudit
from ccis_sim.demand.non_cooling import compute_non_cooling_demand
from ccis_sim.demand.schedule import AcademicCalendar, build_occupancy_schedule
from ccis_sim.outage.sampler import apply_ghi_multiplier, sample_outage_schedule
from ccis_sim.outage.shedding import UnservedTracker, shed_for_hour
from ccis_sim.solar.array import compute_ac_generation
from ccis_sim.solar.irradiance import compute_dc_power_per_kwp
from ccis_sim.weather.loader import load_weather_fixture


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SimulationConfig:
    """Configuration parameters for the CCIS hourly microgrid simulation engine.

    All parameters are strictly validated upon initialization.
    """

    solar_kwp: float = 0.0
    """Installed DC capacity of the rooftop solar PV array (kWp). Range: [0.0, 200.0]."""

    battery_kwh: float = 0.0
    """Rated nominal capacity of the Lithium Iron Phosphate (LFP) battery (kWh). Range: [0.0, 200.0]."""

    weather_year: int = 2019
    """Weather reference fixture year: 2019 (baseline) or 2016 (El Niño hot year)."""

    temp_offset_c: float = 0.0
    """Additive ambient temperature perturbation in °C (e.g. +2.0°C for climate sensitivity)."""

    outage_regime: Literal["normal", "typhoon"] = "normal"
    """Stochastic utility outage arrival regime ('normal' or 'typhoon') per [D-11]."""

    seed: int = 42
    """Deterministic random seed for the numpy random generator driving stochastic outages."""

    initial_soc_kwh: float | None = None
    """Initial battery stored chemical energy (kWh). If None, defaults to full battery capacity."""

    tier2_shed_soc_fraction: float = 0.20
    """Battery SoC fraction threshold below which Tier 2 load is shed during blackouts per [D-34]."""

    emergency_reserve_pct: float = 0.20
    """Operational blackout reserve buffer fraction held from routine daily cycling per [D-17]."""

    allow_emergency_drawdown: bool = False
    """Permit catastrophic emergency drawdown below 10% min_soc during blackouts per [D-25]."""

    demand_scale: float = 1.0
    """Scalar multiplier applied to building electrical demand arrays (default 1.0). Must be > 0.0."""

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.demand_scale <= 0.0:
            raise ValueError(f"demand_scale must be > 0.0, got {self.demand_scale}")
        if self.solar_kwp < 0.0 or self.solar_kwp > 200.0:
            raise ValueError(f"solar_kwp must be in [0.0, 200.0], got {self.solar_kwp}")
        if self.battery_kwh < 0.0 or self.battery_kwh > 200.0:
            raise ValueError(f"battery_kwh must be in [0.0, 200.0], got {self.battery_kwh}")
        if self.weather_year not in (2016, 2019):
            raise ValueError(f"weather_year must be 2016 or 2019, got {self.weather_year}")
        if self.outage_regime not in ("normal", "typhoon"):
            raise ValueError(f"outage_regime must be 'normal' or 'typhoon', got '{self.outage_regime}'")
        if not (0.0 <= self.tier2_shed_soc_fraction <= 1.0):
            raise ValueError(
                f"tier2_shed_soc_fraction must be in [0.0, 1.0], got {self.tier2_shed_soc_fraction}"
            )
        if not (0.0 <= self.emergency_reserve_pct <= 1.0):
            raise ValueError(
                f"emergency_reserve_pct must be in [0.0, 1.0], got {self.emergency_reserve_pct}"
            )
        if self.initial_soc_kwh is not None:
            if self.battery_kwh > 0.0:
                if not (0.0 <= self.initial_soc_kwh <= self.battery_kwh):
                    raise ValueError(
                        f"initial_soc_kwh must be in [0.0, {self.battery_kwh}], got {self.initial_soc_kwh}"
                    )
            elif self.initial_soc_kwh != 0.0:
                raise ValueError("initial_soc_kwh must be 0.0 when battery_kwh is 0.0")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SimulationResult:
    """Complete output artifact from an annual simulation execution.

    Contains the configuration, chronological hourly records, aggregate energy metrics,
    the active outage schedule, and the load shedding tracker summary.
    """

    config: SimulationConfig
    records: list[HourlyRecord]
    metrics: dict[str, Any]
    outage_schedule: OutageSchedule
    tracker_summary: dict[str, Any]

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Convert simulation outcome to a dictionary representation."""
        return {
            "config": asdict(self.config),
            "metrics": self.metrics,
            "tracker_summary": self.tracker_summary,
            "record_count": len(self.records),
        }


def _compute_simulation_metrics(
    records: Sequence[HourlyRecord],
    config: SimulationConfig,
    tracker: UnservedTracker,
    building: Building,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Calculate key headline, performance, and reliability metrics from simulation records."""
    if not records:
        return {}

    annual_gross_demand = sum(r.demand_gross_kwh for r in records)
    annual_served_demand = sum(r.demand_served_kwh for r in records)
    annual_unserved_load = sum(r.unserved_load_kwh for r in records)
    annual_grid_import = sum(r.grid_import_kwh for r in records)
    annual_solar_generation = sum(r.solar_generation_kwh for r in records)
    annual_solar_to_demand = sum(r.solar_to_demand_kwh for r in records)
    annual_solar_to_battery = sum(r.solar_to_battery_kwh for r in records)
    annual_solar_curtailed = sum(r.solar_curtailed_kwh for r in records)
    annual_battery_charge = sum(r.battery_charge_kwh for r in records)
    annual_battery_discharge = sum(r.battery_discharge_kwh for r in records)

    peak_record = max(records, key=lambda r: r.demand_gross_kwh)
    peak_demand_kw = peak_record.demand_gross_kwh
    peak_timestamp = peak_record.timestamp
    peak_hour = peak_record.hour

    eui_kwh_per_m2 = annual_served_demand / building.gross_floor_area_m2
    gross_eui_kwh_per_m2 = annual_gross_demand / building.gross_floor_area_m2

    self_sufficiency_pct = (
        (1.0 - annual_grid_import / annual_served_demand) * 100.0
        if annual_served_demand > 0.0
        else 0.0
    )

    solar_specific_yield_kwh_per_kwp = (
        annual_solar_generation / config.solar_kwp
        if config.solar_kwp > 0.0
        else 0.0
    )

    max_residual = max(r.energy_balance_residual_kwh for r in records)
    net_drift = sum(r.signed_residual_kwh for r in records)

    return {
        "annual_gross_demand_kwh": annual_gross_demand,
        "annual_served_demand_kwh": annual_served_demand,
        "annual_unserved_load_kwh": annual_unserved_load,
        "annual_grid_import_kwh": annual_grid_import,
        "annual_solar_generation_kwh": annual_solar_generation,
        "annual_solar_to_demand_kwh": annual_solar_to_demand,
        "annual_solar_to_battery_kwh": annual_solar_to_battery,
        "annual_solar_curtailed_kwh": annual_solar_curtailed,
        "annual_battery_charge_kwh": annual_battery_charge,
        "annual_battery_discharge_kwh": annual_battery_discharge,
        "battery_throughput_kwh": annual_battery_discharge,
        "solar_specific_yield_kwh_per_kwp": solar_specific_yield_kwh_per_kwp,
        "peak_demand_kw": peak_demand_kw,
        "peak_hour": peak_hour,
        "peak_timestamp": peak_timestamp,
        "eui_kwh_per_m2_year": eui_kwh_per_m2,
        "gross_eui_kwh_per_m2_year": gross_eui_kwh_per_m2,
        "self_sufficiency_pct": self_sufficiency_pct,
        "total_outage_hours": tracker.total_outage_hours,
        "unserved_tier1_kwh": tracker.unserved_tier1_kwh,
        "unserved_tier2_kwh": tracker.unserved_tier2_kwh,
        "unserved_tier3_kwh": tracker.unserved_tier3_kwh,
        "outage_hours_tier1_served": tracker.outage_hours_tier1_served,
        "outage_hours_tier2_served": tracker.outage_hours_tier2_served,
        "outage_hours_tier3_served": tracker.outage_hours_tier3_served,
        "outage_tier1_coverage_pct": tracker.outage_hours_tier1_served_pct,
        "outage_tier2_coverage_pct": tracker.outage_hours_tier2_served_pct,
        "outage_tier3_coverage_pct": tracker.outage_hours_tier3_served_pct,
        "max_energy_balance_residual_kwh": max_residual,
        "cumulative_net_drift_kwh": net_drift,
    }


def simulate(
    config: SimulationConfig,
    weather_df: pd.DataFrame | None = None,
    seed: int | None = None,
    *,
    audit: EquipmentAudit | None = None,
    building: Building | None = None,
    calendar: AcademicCalendar | None = None,
    verify: bool = True,
) -> SimulationResult:
    """Execute integrated annual simulation across all microgrid sub-models.

    Follows the 8-step dispatch sequence defined in docs/context/phase2-interface-contract.md §5:
    1. Demand decomposition: non-cooling (DEM-001) + cooling response (DEM-002) summed per tier.
    2. Grid availability query: OutageSchedule (OUT-001).
    3. Priority load shedding: shed_for_hour (OUT-002).
    4. Solar AC generation: irradiance transposition (SOL-001) + inverter clipping (SOL-002)
       with typhoon GHI multiplier applied to weather before transposition.
    5. Bus energy balance & battery dispatch: step_battery (BAT-001) with soc_floor_kwh (BAT-002).
    6. Idle losses: apply_idle_losses on resulting chemical SoC (BAT-002).
    7. Grid balance & unserved load tracking: import or blackout unserved tracking (OUT-002).
    8. Canonical record construction: HourlyRecord creation and physical invariant checks.

    Parameters
    ----------
    config : SimulationConfig
        Sizing, weather, and operational regime configuration.
    weather_df : pd.DataFrame, optional
        Pre-loaded weather DataFrame. If None, loads canonical fixture for config.weather_year.
    seed : int, optional
        Random seed override for stochastic outage generation.
    audit : EquipmentAudit, optional
        Equipment inventory audit instance. If None, loads canonical CSV fixture.
    building : Building, optional
        Building archetype instance. If None, initializes default CCIS Building.
    calendar : AcademicCalendar, optional
        SUC academic calendar instance. If None, initializes default AcademicCalendar.
    verify : bool, optional
        If True, invokes assert_simulation_valid across records before returning (default: True).

    Returns
    -------
    SimulationResult
        Completed simulation run containing hourly records, summary metrics, and trackers.
    """
    bldg = building or Building()
    cal = calendar or AcademicCalendar()
    aud = audit or EquipmentAudit.load_from_csv("data/fixtures/equipment-audit-ccis.csv")

    # 1. Ingest & sanitize weather input
    if weather_df is None:
        raw_weather = load_weather_fixture(config.weather_year)
    else:
        raw_weather = weather_df.copy(deep=True)

    w_df = raw_weather.copy(deep=True)
    if config.temp_offset_c != 0.0:
        w_df["t_amb"] = w_df["t_amb"] + config.temp_offset_c

    n_hours = len(w_df)
    if n_hours == 0:
        raise ValueError("Weather DataFrame must not be empty.")

    # 2. Stochastic Outage Schedule (OUT-001)
    run_seed = seed if seed is not None else config.seed
    rng = np.random.default_rng(run_seed)
    outage_schedule = sample_outage_schedule(config.outage_regime, rng, n_hours=n_hours)

    # 3. Demand Sub-Models (DEM-001 + DEM-002)
    # [ASSUMPTION] Build annual occupancy schedule and non-cooling demand per [D-27]
    occupancy_schedule = build_occupancy_schedule(w_df["timestamp"], calendar=cal)
    non_cooling_demand = compute_non_cooling_demand(occupancy_schedule, aud, bldg)

    # [ASSUMPTION] Compute temperature/humidity cooling demand response per [D-28]
    cooling_demand = compute_cooling_demand(
        w_df,
        occupancy_schedule.occupancy_fraction,
        aud,
        bldg,
    )

    # Linear tier summation per Phase 2 contract §4.1, scaled by demand_scale
    t1_demand_arr = (non_cooling_demand.tier1_kwh + cooling_demand.tier1_kwh) * config.demand_scale
    t2_demand_arr = (non_cooling_demand.tier2_kwh + cooling_demand.tier2_kwh) * config.demand_scale
    t3_demand_arr = (non_cooling_demand.tier3_kwh + cooling_demand.tier3_kwh) * config.demand_scale

    # 4. Solar Sub-Models (SOL-001 + SOL-002)
    # Apply typhoon solar GHI depression window if active per [D-11] and [D-33]
    weather_for_solar = apply_ghi_multiplier(w_df, outage_schedule)

    if config.solar_kwp > 0.0:
        dc_per_kwp = compute_dc_power_per_kwp(weather_for_solar)
        solar_array = SolarArray(capacity_kwp=config.solar_kwp)
        ac_generation_arr = compute_ac_generation(dc_per_kwp, solar_array)
    else:
        ac_generation_arr = np.zeros(n_hours, dtype=np.float64)

    # 5. Battery Initialization (BAT-001 + BAT-002)
    battery = Battery(
        capacity_kwh=config.battery_kwh,
        emergency_reserve_pct=config.emergency_reserve_pct,
    )

    if config.battery_kwh > 0.0:
        current_soc_kwh = (
            config.initial_soc_kwh
            if config.initial_soc_kwh is not None
            else config.battery_kwh  # Default full battery at simulation start
        )
    else:
        current_soc_kwh = 0.0

    initial_soc_for_verify = current_soc_kwh

    # 6. Hourly Dispatch Loop
    records: list[HourlyRecord] = []
    tracker = UnservedTracker()

    hours_list = w_df["hour"].to_numpy(dtype=int)
    timestamps_list = w_df["timestamp"].to_list()
    t_amb_list = w_df["t_amb"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
    rh_list = w_df["rh"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
    ghi_list = w_df["ghi"].to_numpy(dtype=float)

    for i in range(n_hours):
        hour_val = int(hours_list[i])
        ts_val = str(timestamps_list[i])
        t_amb_val = float(t_amb_list[i])
        rh_val = float(rh_list[i])
        ghi_val = float(ghi_list[i])

        # Step 1: Tiered demand components for hour i
        t1_val = float(t1_demand_arr[i])
        t2_val = float(t2_demand_arr[i])
        t3_val = float(t3_demand_arr[i])
        tier_demand_kwh = (t1_val, t2_val, t3_val)
        demand_gross_kwh = t1_val + t2_val + t3_val

        # Step 2: Grid availability for hour i
        grid_avail = bool(outage_schedule.grid_available[i])

        # Step 3: Priority load shedding
        shed_res = shed_for_hour(
            tier_demand_kwh,
            grid_available=grid_avail,
            soc_kwh=current_soc_kwh,
            battery=battery,
            tier2_shed_soc_fraction=config.tier2_shed_soc_fraction,
        )
        active_tier_int = int(shed_res.active_tier)
        target_served_kwh = sum(shed_res.served_kwh)

        # Step 4: Solar generation available
        solar_gen_kwh = float(ac_generation_arr[i])

        # Step 5: Bus energy routing & battery request
        if solar_gen_kwh >= target_served_kwh:
            # Solar surplus: load is fully served by solar
            solar_to_demand_kwh = target_served_kwh
            surplus_solar_kwh = solar_gen_kwh - target_served_kwh

            charge_req_kwh = surplus_solar_kwh
            discharge_req_kwh = 0.0
            floor_kwh = 0.0

            step_res = step_battery(
                current_soc_kwh,
                battery,
                charge_req_kwh,
                0.0,
                soc_floor_kwh=floor_kwh,
                delta_t_hours=1.0,
            )

            battery_charge_kwh = step_res.charge_kwh
            battery_discharge_kwh = 0.0
            solar_to_battery_kwh = step_res.charge_kwh
            solar_curtailed_kwh = step_res.charge_curtailed_kwh

            grid_import_kwh = 0.0
            shortfall_kwh = 0.0
            soc_after_step = step_res.soc_kwh
        else:
            # Solar deficit: all solar serves load, deficit requested from battery
            solar_to_demand_kwh = solar_gen_kwh
            solar_to_battery_kwh = 0.0
            solar_curtailed_kwh = 0.0
            battery_charge_kwh = 0.0

            deficit_kwh = target_served_kwh - solar_gen_kwh

            floor_kwh = soc_floor_kwh(
                battery,
                grid_available=grid_avail,
                active_load_tier=active_tier_int,
                allow_emergency_drawdown=config.allow_emergency_drawdown,
            )

            step_res = step_battery(
                current_soc_kwh,
                battery,
                0.0,
                deficit_kwh,
                soc_floor_kwh=floor_kwh,
                delta_t_hours=1.0,
            )

            battery_discharge_kwh = step_res.discharge_kwh
            shortfall_kwh = deficit_kwh - battery_discharge_kwh
            soc_after_step = step_res.soc_kwh

            if grid_avail:
                grid_import_kwh = shortfall_kwh
                shortfall_kwh = 0.0
            else:
                grid_import_kwh = 0.0

        # Step 6: Battery chemical idle losses (ambient self-discharge + tare)
        if config.battery_kwh > 0.0:
            final_soc_kwh, idle_loss_kwh = apply_idle_losses(
                soc_after_step,
                battery,
                delta_t_hours=1.0,
            )
        else:
            final_soc_kwh = 0.0

        # Step 7: Unserved energy tracking & allocation
        act_served_tiers, act_unserved_tiers = tracker.record(
            shed_res,
            grid_available=grid_avail,
            battery_shortfall_kwh=shortfall_kwh,
        )

        demand_served_kwh = sum(act_served_tiers)
        unserved_load_kwh = sum(act_unserved_tiers)
```
