```python
        # Advance SoC for next chronological hour
        current_soc_kwh = final_soc_kwh

        # Step 8: Construct canonical HourlyRecord
        record = HourlyRecord(
            hour=hour_val,
            timestamp=ts_val,
            temp_ambient_c=t_amb_val,
            humidity_rel_pct=rh_val,
            ghi_w_m2=ghi_val,
            demand_gross_kwh=demand_gross_kwh,
            demand_served_kwh=demand_served_kwh,
            solar_generation_kwh=solar_gen_kwh,
            solar_to_demand_kwh=solar_to_demand_kwh,
            solar_to_battery_kwh=solar_to_battery_kwh,
            solar_curtailed_kwh=solar_curtailed_kwh,
            battery_soc_kwh=final_soc_kwh,
            battery_charge_kwh=battery_charge_kwh,
            battery_discharge_kwh=battery_discharge_kwh,
            grid_import_kwh=grid_import_kwh,
            unserved_load_kwh=unserved_load_kwh,
            grid_available=grid_avail,
            active_load_tier=active_tier_int,
        )
        records.append(record)

    # 7. Verification Invariant Check
    if verify:
        assert_simulation_valid(
            records=records,
            battery=battery if config.battery_kwh > 0.0 else None,
            allow_emergency_drawdown=config.allow_emergency_drawdown,
            initial_soc_kwh=initial_soc_for_verify if config.battery_kwh > 0.0 else None,
        )

    # 8. Compute headline & reliability metrics
    metrics = _compute_simulation_metrics(records, config, tracker, bldg)

    return SimulationResult(
        config=config,
        records=records,
        metrics=metrics,
        outage_schedule=outage_schedule,
        tracker_summary=tracker.summary(),
    )
```

### `src/ccis_sim/integration/exporters.py`

*220 lines. Data exporters for CCIS microgrid simulation outputs.*

```python
"""Data exporters for CCIS microgrid simulation outputs.

Serializes simulation runs into hourly CSVs, daily aggregate CSVs, and annual summary
JSON files per task specification INT-001.3.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import json
from collections import defaultdict
from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from ccis_sim.core.models import HourlyRecord
from ccis_sim.integration.engine import SimulationResult


def export_hourly_csv(
    records: Sequence[HourlyRecord],
    output_path: Path | str,
) -> Path:
    """Export time-series of HourlyRecord instances to a structured CSV file.

    Parameters
    ----------
    records : Sequence[HourlyRecord]
        List or sequence of HourlyRecord objects.
    output_path : Path | str
        Destination filesystem path.

    Returns
    -------
    Path
        Resolved path of the created CSV file.
    """
    path = Path(output_path).resolve()
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    if not records:
        path.write_text("", encoding="utf-8")
        return path

    fieldnames = list(records[0].to_dict().keys())

    with open(path, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=fieldnames)
        writer.writeheader()
        for r in records:
            writer.writerow(r.to_dict())

    return path


def export_daily_csv(
    records: Sequence[HourlyRecord],
    output_path: Path | str,
) -> Path:
    """Aggregate hourly simulation records into daily totals and export to CSV.

    Parameters
    ----------
    records : Sequence[HourlyRecord]
        List or sequence of HourlyRecord objects.
    output_path : Path | str
        Destination filesystem path.

    Returns
    -------
    Path
        Resolved path of the created CSV file.
    """
    path = Path(output_path).resolve()
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    if not records:
        path.write_text("", encoding="utf-8")
        return path

    # Group records by ISO date YYYY-MM-DD
    daily_groups: dict[str, list[HourlyRecord]] = defaultdict(list)
    for r in records:
        date_str = r.timestamp[:10]  # Extracts YYYY-MM-DD
        daily_groups[date_str].append(r)

    fieldnames = [
        "date",
        "temp_ambient_mean_c",
        "temp_ambient_max_c",
        "ghi_total_wh_m2",
        "demand_gross_kwh",
        "demand_served_kwh",
        "unserved_load_kwh",
        "solar_generation_kwh",
        "solar_to_demand_kwh",
        "solar_to_battery_kwh",
        "solar_curtailed_kwh",
        "battery_charge_kwh",
        "battery_discharge_kwh",
        "battery_soc_end_kwh",
        "grid_import_kwh",
        "outage_hours",
        "active_tier_min",
    ]

    with open(path, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=fieldnames)
        writer.writeheader()

        for date_str, day_recs in sorted(daily_groups.items()):
            n_hrs = len(day_recs)
            mean_temp = sum(r.temp_ambient_c for r in day_recs) / n_hrs if n_hrs > 0 else 0.0
            max_temp = max((r.temp_ambient_c for r in day_recs), default=0.0)
            ghi_total = sum(r.ghi_w_m2 for r in day_recs)  # Sum of hourly average W/m^2 = Wh/m^2
            gross_dem = sum(r.demand_gross_kwh for r in day_recs)
            served_dem = sum(r.demand_served_kwh for r in day_recs)
            unserved = sum(r.unserved_load_kwh for r in day_recs)
            solar_gen = sum(r.solar_generation_kwh for r in day_recs)
            solar_to_dem = sum(r.solar_to_demand_kwh for r in day_recs)
            solar_to_bat = sum(r.solar_to_battery_kwh for r in day_recs)
            curtailed = sum(r.solar_curtailed_kwh for r in day_recs)
            chg = sum(r.battery_charge_kwh for r in day_recs)
            dis = sum(r.battery_discharge_kwh for r in day_recs)
            soc_end = day_recs[-1].battery_soc_kwh
            grid_imp = sum(r.grid_import_kwh for r in day_recs)
            outage_hrs = sum(1 for r in day_recs if not r.grid_available)
            tier_min = min(r.active_load_tier for r in day_recs)

            row = {
                "date": date_str,
                "temp_ambient_mean_c": round(mean_temp, 3),
                "temp_ambient_max_c": round(max_temp, 3),
                "ghi_total_wh_m2": round(ghi_total, 2),
                "demand_gross_kwh": round(gross_dem, 4),
                "demand_served_kwh": round(served_dem, 4),
                "unserved_load_kwh": round(unserved, 4),
                "solar_generation_kwh": round(solar_gen, 4),
                "solar_to_demand_kwh": round(solar_to_dem, 4),
                "solar_to_battery_kwh": round(solar_to_bat, 4),
                "solar_curtailed_kwh": round(curtailed, 4),
                "battery_charge_kwh": round(chg, 4),
                "battery_discharge_kwh": round(dis, 4),
                "battery_soc_end_kwh": round(soc_end, 4),
                "grid_import_kwh": round(grid_imp, 4),
                "outage_hours": outage_hrs,
                "active_tier_min": tier_min,
            }
            writer.writerow(row)

    return path


def export_summary_json(
    result: SimulationResult,
    output_path: Path | str,
    *,
    indent: int = 2,
) -> Path:
    """Export annual simulation summary metrics and configuration to JSON.

    Parameters
    ----------
    result : SimulationResult
        Completed simulation result instance.
    output_path : Path | str
        Destination filesystem path.
    indent : int, optional
        JSON indentation spacing (default: 2).

    Returns
    -------
    Path
        Resolved path of the created JSON file.
    """
    path = Path(output_path).resolve()
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    summary_data = result.to_dict()

    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(summary_data, f, indent=indent, default=str)

    return path


def export_all(
    result: SimulationResult,
    output_dir: Path | str,
    *,
    prefix: str = "ccis_sim",
) -> dict[str, Path]:
    """Convenience exporter that writes hourly CSV, daily CSV, and summary JSON.

    Parameters
    ----------
    result : SimulationResult
        Completed simulation result instance.
    output_dir : Path | str
        Destination directory.
    prefix : str, optional
        Filename prefix for the generated files (default: 'ccis_sim').

    Returns
    -------
    dict[str, Path]
        Mapping with keys 'hourly_csv', 'daily_csv', 'summary_json'.
    """
    out_dir = Path(output_dir).resolve()
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    hourly_path = export_hourly_csv(result.records, out_dir / f"{prefix}_hourly.csv")
    daily_path = export_daily_csv(result.records, out_dir / f"{prefix}_daily.csv")
    json_path = export_summary_json(result, out_dir / f"{prefix}_summary.json")

    return {
        "hourly_csv": hourly_path,
        "daily_csv": daily_path,
        "summary_json": json_path,
    }
```

### `src/ccis_sim/integration/scenarios.py`

*342 lines. Scenario Grid and Monte Carlo Simulation Runner (INT-002).*

```python
"""Scenario Grid and Monte Carlo Simulation Runner (INT-002).

Executes the full comparative scenario matrix across solar PV capacities, battery storage sizes,
weather years, stochastic outage regimes, and demand calibration scales using multiprocessing.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import multiprocessing as mp
import os
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Sequence

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from scipy import stats

from ccis_sim.integration.engine import SimulationConfig, simulate
from ccis_sim.weather.loader import load_weather_fixture

# Global worker cache for weather fixtures to avoid redundant file I/O per task
_WORKER_WEATHER_CACHE: dict[int, pd.DataFrame] = {}


def _init_worker() -> None:
    """Initialize multiprocessing worker process by caching canonical weather fixtures."""
    global _WORKER_WEATHER_CACHE
    try:
        _WORKER_WEATHER_CACHE = {
            2016: load_weather_fixture(2016),
            2019: load_weather_fixture(2019),
        }
    except Exception:
        # Fallback if fixtures cannot be loaded immediately
        _WORKER_WEATHER_CACHE = {}


def compute_bill_calibrated_scale() -> float:
    """Compute the single empirical scale factor reconciling bottom-up demand to synthetic mock bills.

    Scale = bills_total / bottom_up_total ≈ 2.1047 (Phase 1 synthetic billing fixture vs DEM-001/002).
    """
    bills_path = Path("data/fixtures/synthetic-mock-bills-ccis.csv")
    if not bills_path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Synthetic bills fixture not found at {bills_path}")

    bills_df = pd.read_csv(bills_path)
    bills_total = float(bills_df["total_kwh"].sum())

    cfg = SimulationConfig(
        solar_kwp=0.0,
        battery_kwh=0.0,
        weather_year=2019,
        outage_regime="normal",
        seed=42,
        demand_scale=1.0,
    )
    res = simulate(cfg, verify=False)
    bottom_up_total = float(res.metrics["annual_gross_demand_kwh"])

    if bottom_up_total <= 0.0:
        raise ValueError("Bottom-up annual demand must be strictly positive.")

    return bills_total / bottom_up_total


def _run_single_simulation(config: SimulationConfig) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Execute a single simulation run and extract flat tabular scenario metrics.

    Args:
        config: SimulationConfig defining sizing, weather, regime, seed, and scaling.

    Returns:
        Dictionary mapping column names to scalar summary metrics.
    """
    global _WORKER_WEATHER_CACHE
    if not _WORKER_WEATHER_CACHE:
        _init_worker()

    w_df = _WORKER_WEATHER_CACHE.get(config.weather_year)
    res = simulate(config, weather_df=w_df, verify=True)
    m = res.metrics
    records = res.records

    peak_grid_kw = max((r.grid_import_kwh for r in records), default=0.0)
    battery_cycles = (
        m["annual_battery_discharge_kwh"] / config.battery_kwh
        if config.battery_kwh > 0.0
        else 0.0
    )
    self_sufficiency = m["self_sufficiency_pct"] / 100.0

    return {
        "solar_kwp": float(config.solar_kwp),
        "battery_kwh": float(config.battery_kwh),
        "weather_year": int(config.weather_year),
        "outage_regime": str(config.outage_regime),
        "demand_scale": float(config.demand_scale),
        "temp_offset_c": float(config.temp_offset_c),
        "seed": int(config.seed),
        "grid_import_kwh": float(m["annual_grid_import_kwh"]),
        "solar_gen_kwh": float(m["annual_solar_generation_kwh"]),
        "curtailed_kwh": float(m["annual_solar_curtailed_kwh"]),
        "unserved_t1_kwh": float(m["unserved_tier1_kwh"]),
        "unserved_t2_kwh": float(m["unserved_tier2_kwh"]),
        "unserved_t3_kwh": float(m["unserved_tier3_kwh"]),
        "total_outage_hours": int(m["total_outage_hours"]),
        "outage_hours_t1_served": int(m["outage_hours_tier1_served"]),
        "outage_hours_t2_served": int(m["outage_hours_tier2_served"]),
        "battery_cycles": float(battery_cycles),
        "self_sufficiency": float(self_sufficiency),
        "peak_grid_kw": float(peak_grid_kw),
    }


def generate_scenario_configs(
    bill_scale: float | None = None,
) -> list[SimulationConfig]:
    """Generate the full INT-002 simulation scenario configuration suite.

    Consists of:
    1. Main grid: 4 solar × 4 battery × 2 weather × 2 regimes × 30 seeds (demand_scale=1.0) = 1,920 runs
    2. Sensitivity grid: 4 solar × 4 battery × 1 weather (2019) × 2 regimes × 15 seeds (demand_scale=bill_scale) = 480 runs
    3. Climate +2°C case: 2 solar ({0, 100}) × 2 battery ({0, 100}) × 2019 normal × 15 seeds (temp_offset_c=2.0) = 60 runs
    Total: 2,460 runs.

    Common Random Numbers (CRN) are preserved across sizings for identical (weather, regime, seed) tuples.
    """
    if bill_scale is None:
        bill_scale = compute_bill_calibrated_scale()

    solar_sizes = [0.0, 50.0, 100.0, 200.0]
    battery_sizes = [0.0, 50.0, 100.0, 200.0]
    configs: list[SimulationConfig] = []

    # 1. Main Grid (demand_scale = 1.0, 30 seeds)
    for year in (2019, 2016):
        for regime in ("normal", "typhoon"):
            for seed in range(30):
                for sol in solar_sizes:
                    for bat in battery_sizes:
                        configs.append(
                            SimulationConfig(
                                solar_kwp=sol,
                                battery_kwh=bat,
                                weather_year=year,
                                outage_regime=regime,
                                seed=seed,
                                demand_scale=1.0,
                                temp_offset_c=0.0,
                            )
                        )

    # 2. Sensitivity Grid (demand_scale = bill_scale, 2019 only, 15 seeds)
    for regime in ("normal", "typhoon"):
        for seed in range(15):
            for sol in solar_sizes:
                for bat in battery_sizes:
                    configs.append(
                        SimulationConfig(
                            solar_kwp=sol,
                            battery_kwh=bat,
                            weather_year=2019,
                            outage_regime=regime,
                            seed=seed,
                            demand_scale=bill_scale,
                            temp_offset_c=0.0,
                        )
                    )

    # 3. Climate Sensitivity (+2.0°C perturbation, 2019 normal, 15 seeds)
    for seed in range(15):
        for sol in (0.0, 100.0):
            for bat in (0.0, 100.0):
                configs.append(
                    SimulationConfig(
                        solar_kwp=sol,
                        battery_kwh=bat,
                        weather_year=2019,
                        outage_regime="normal",
                        seed=seed,
                        demand_scale=1.0,
                        temp_offset_c=2.0,
                    )
                )

    return configs


def compute_mc_summary(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Compute Monte Carlo summary statistics per scenario cell.

    Calculates mean, std, 5th percentile, and 95th percentile for:
    - unserved Tier 1 + Tier 2 load (kWh)
    - fraction of outage hours with Tier 1 and Tier 2 fully served
    """
    df_calc = df.copy()
    df_calc["unserved_t12_kwh"] = df_calc["unserved_t1_kwh"] + df_calc["unserved_t2_kwh"]
    df_calc["outage_t12_served_frac"] = np.where(
        df_calc["total_outage_hours"] > 0,
        df_calc["outage_hours_t2_served"] / df_calc["total_outage_hours"],
        1.0,
    )

    group_cols = [
        "solar_kwp",
        "battery_kwh",
        "weather_year",
        "outage_regime",
        "demand_scale",
        "temp_offset_c",
    ]

    rows = []
    for keys, grp in df_calc.groupby(group_cols, sort=True):
        u12 = grp["unserved_t12_kwh"].to_numpy()
        frac = grp["outage_t12_served_frac"].to_numpy()
        n = len(grp)

        rows.append(
            {
                "solar_kwp": keys[0],
                "battery_kwh": keys[1],
                "weather_year": keys[2],
                "outage_regime": keys[3],
                "demand_scale": keys[4],
                "temp_offset_c": keys[5],
                "sample_size": n,
                "unserved_t12_kwh_mean": float(np.mean(u12)),
                "unserved_t12_kwh_std": float(np.std(u12, ddof=1)) if n > 1 else 0.0,
                "unserved_t12_kwh_p5": float(np.percentile(u12, 5)),
                "unserved_t12_kwh_p95": float(np.percentile(u12, 95)),
                "outage_t12_served_frac_mean": float(np.mean(frac)),
                "outage_t12_served_frac_std": float(np.std(frac, ddof=1)) if n > 1 else 0.0,
                "outage_t12_served_frac_p5": float(np.percentile(frac, 5)),
                "outage_t12_served_frac_p95": float(np.percentile(frac, 95)),
            }
        )

    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


def check_convergence(df: pd.DataFrame) -> dict[int, dict[str, Any]]:
    """Evaluate Monte Carlo convergence for the benchmark (100 kWp, 100 kWh, 2019, typhoon) cell.

    Reports sample mean, standard error, 95% Student-t confidence interval,
    and relative CI half-width at N = 10, 20, 30 seeds.
    """
    mask = (
        (df["solar_kwp"] == 100.0)
        & (df["battery_kwh"] == 100.0)
        & (df["weather_year"] == 2019)
        & (df["outage_regime"] == "typhoon")
        & (df["demand_scale"] == 1.0)
        & (df["temp_offset_c"] == 0.0)
    )
    cell_df = df[mask].sort_values("seed")
    u12 = (cell_df["unserved_t1_kwh"] + cell_df["unserved_t2_kwh"]).to_numpy()

    results: dict[int, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for n in (10, 20, 30):
        if len(u12) < n:
            continue
        sample = u12[:n]
        mean = float(np.mean(sample))
        std = float(np.std(sample, ddof=1)) if n > 1 else 0.0
        se = std / np.sqrt(n)
        t_crit = float(stats.t.ppf(0.975, df=n - 1)) if n > 1 else 1.96
        half_width = float(t_crit * se)
        ci_lower = max(0.0, mean - half_width)
        ci_upper = mean + half_width

        is_converged = False
        rel_half_width = 0.0
        if mean == 0.0:
            is_converged = True
            convergence_note = "Exact zero unserved load (battery fully covers all sampled outages)"
        else:
            rel_half_width = half_width / mean
            is_converged = rel_half_width < 0.10
            convergence_note = (
                f"CI half-width is {rel_half_width * 100:.2f}% of mean "
                f"({'< 10% target met' if is_converged else '>= 10%'})"
            )

        results[n] = {
            "n": n,
            "mean": mean,
            "std": std,
            "se": se,
            "ci_95": (ci_lower, ci_upper),
            "half_width": half_width,
            "rel_half_width": rel_half_width,
            "is_converged": is_converged,
            "note": convergence_note,
        }

    return results


def run_grid(
    configs: Sequence[SimulationConfig] | None = None,
    *,
    output_dir: str | Path = "results",
    num_workers: int | None = None,
    return_df: bool = True,
) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]:
    """Execute scenario grid simulations using multiprocessing and serialize output CSVs.

    Args:
        configs: List of SimulationConfig instances to run. If None, runs full INT-002 suite (2,460 runs).
        output_dir: Directory where scenario_grid.csv and mc_summary.csv will be written.
        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: min(os.cpu_count() or 1, 8)).
        return_df: If True, returns (scenario_grid_df, mc_summary_df).

    Returns:
        Tuple of (scenario_grid_df, mc_summary_df).
    """
    out_path = Path(output_dir)
    out_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    if configs is None:
        configs = generate_scenario_configs()

    n_workers = num_workers or min(os.cpu_count() or 1, 8)

    if n_workers > 1 and len(configs) > 1:
        with mp.Pool(processes=n_workers, initializer=_init_worker) as pool:
            results = pool.map(_run_single_simulation, configs)
    else:
        _init_worker()
        results = [_run_single_simulation(cfg) for cfg in configs]

    scenario_grid_df = pd.DataFrame(results)
    scenario_grid_csv = out_path / "scenario_grid.csv"
    scenario_grid_df.to_csv(scenario_grid_csv, index=False)

    mc_summary_df = compute_mc_summary(scenario_grid_df)
    mc_summary_csv = out_path / "mc_summary.csv"
    mc_summary_df.to_csv(mc_summary_csv, index=False)

    return scenario_grid_df, mc_summary_df
```

### `src/ccis_sim/outage/__init__.py`

*53 lines. Stochastic utility outage generation (Poisson/Lognormal) and priority load shedding.*

```python
"""Stochastic utility outage generation (Poisson/Lognormal) and priority load shedding."""

from ccis_sim.outage.sampler import (
    DEFAULT_ANNUAL_HOURS,
    NORMAL_LAMBDA,
    NORMAL_LOGNORMAL_MU,
    NORMAL_LOGNORMAL_SIGMA,
    TYPHOON_GHI_MULTIPLIER_MAX,
    TYPHOON_GHI_MULTIPLIER_MIN,
    TYPHOON_LAMBDA,
    TYPHOON_LOGNORMAL_MU,
    TYPHOON_LOGNORMAL_SIGMA,
    TYPHOON_STORM_DAYS_MAX,
    TYPHOON_STORM_DAYS_MIN,
    OutageDistributionValidationResult,
    apply_ghi_multiplier,
    sample_outage_schedule,
    sample_raw_arrivals,
    sample_raw_durations,
    validate_duration_distribution,
)
from ccis_sim.outage.shedding import (
    TierAuditComparison,
    UnservedTracker,
    compute_tier_audit_shares,
    shed_for_hour,
)

__all__ = [
    # Shedding (OUT-002)
    "shed_for_hour",
    "UnservedTracker",
    "TierAuditComparison",
    "compute_tier_audit_shares",
    # Sampler (OUT-001)
    "sample_outage_schedule",
    "apply_ghi_multiplier",
    "validate_duration_distribution",
    "OutageDistributionValidationResult",
    "sample_raw_durations",
    "sample_raw_arrivals",
    "NORMAL_LAMBDA",
    "NORMAL_LOGNORMAL_MU",
    "NORMAL_LOGNORMAL_SIGMA",
    "TYPHOON_LAMBDA",
    "TYPHOON_LOGNORMAL_MU",
    "TYPHOON_LOGNORMAL_SIGMA",
    "TYPHOON_STORM_DAYS_MIN",
    "TYPHOON_STORM_DAYS_MAX",
    "TYPHOON_GHI_MULTIPLIER_MIN",
    "TYPHOON_GHI_MULTIPLIER_MAX",
    "DEFAULT_ANNUAL_HOURS",
]
```

### `src/ccis_sim/outage/sampler.py`

*510 lines. Stochastic utility outage generation and typhoon solar depression sampler.*

```python
"""Stochastic utility outage generation and typhoon solar depression sampler.

Implements Poisson arrival processes, Lognormal duration distributions,
integer-hour discretization, interval merging, and typhoon multi-day solar
depression coupling per decisions [D-11], [D-26], and [D-33].
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from collections.abc import Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Literal

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import scipy.stats as stats

from ccis_sim.core.interfaces import OutageSchedule
from ccis_sim.core.models import OutageEvent, OutageType

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Stochastic Distribution Parameters per [D-11]
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
NORMAL_LAMBDA: float = 3.0
"""Annual Poisson arrival rate for normal / baseline weather years (events/year)."""

NORMAL_LOGNORMAL_MU: float = 1.2
"""Log-mean parameter mu for normal year duration distribution on ln(hours)."""

NORMAL_LOGNORMAL_SIGMA: float = 0.8
"""Log-standard-deviation parameter sigma for normal year duration distribution."""

TYPHOON_LAMBDA: float = 8.0
"""Annual Poisson arrival rate for typhoon / extreme weather years (events/year)."""

TYPHOON_LOGNORMAL_MU: float = 2.3
"""Log-mean parameter mu for typhoon year duration distribution on ln(hours)."""

TYPHOON_LOGNORMAL_SIGMA: float = 1.0
"""Log-standard-deviation parameter sigma for typhoon year duration distribution."""

TYPHOON_STORM_DAYS_MIN: int = 3
"""Minimum duration of typhoon solar depression window in consecutive days per [D-11]."""

TYPHOON_STORM_DAYS_MAX: int = 5
"""Maximum duration of typhoon solar depression window in consecutive days per [D-11]."""

TYPHOON_GHI_MULTIPLIER_MIN: float = 0.20
"""Minimum solar GHI multiplier during active typhoon depression window (20% normal GHI)."""

TYPHOON_GHI_MULTIPLIER_MAX: float = 0.30
"""Maximum solar GHI multiplier during active typhoon depression window (30% normal GHI)."""

DEFAULT_ANNUAL_HOURS: int = 8760
"""Standard hours in an annual simulation cycle."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class OutageDistributionValidationResult:
    """Goodness-of-fit validation outcome for outage duration and arrival processes.

    Returned by validate_duration_distribution per OUT-001.4.
    """

    ks_statistic: float
    """Kolmogorov-Smirnov test statistic comparing sample durations to target Lognormal."""

    ks_pvalue: float
    """Two-sided p-value from the Kolmogorov-Smirnov test."""

    ks_passed: bool
    """True if ks_pvalue >= alpha (null hypothesis of target Lognormal not rejected)."""

    alpha: float
    """Significance level threshold (e.g. 0.01)."""

    sample_size: int
    """Number of sampled durations evaluated."""

    target_mu: float
    """Target Lognormal log-mean parameter."""

    target_sigma: float
    """Target Lognormal log-std parameter."""

    arrival_passed: bool | None = None
    """True if arrival count is statistically consistent with Poisson(lambda), or None if skipped."""

    mean_arrivals: float | None = None
    """Sample mean annual arrival count across evaluated years, or None if skipped."""

    target_lambda: float | None = None
    """Target Poisson arrival rate parameter, or None if skipped."""

    arrival_pvalue: float | None = None
    """Two-sided p-value from arrival rate z-test, or None if skipped."""

    passed: bool = True
    """Overall test outcome: True if all evaluated tests pass."""

    details: str = ""
    """Human-readable diagnostic summary."""


def sample_raw_durations(
    year_type: str,
    n_samples: int,
    rng: np.random.Generator,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Sample continuous pre-discretization outage durations from target Lognormal(mu, sigma).

    Parameters
    ----------
    year_type : str
        Climate reliability regime: 'normal' or 'typhoon'.
    n_samples : int
        Number of duration samples to draw.
    rng : np.random.Generator
        Deterministic numpy random number generator.

    Returns
    -------
    np.ndarray
        1D float array of continuous duration samples in hours.
    """
    regime = year_type.strip().lower()
    if regime == "normal":
        mu, sigma = NORMAL_LOGNORMAL_MU, NORMAL_LOGNORMAL_SIGMA
    elif regime == "typhoon":
        mu, sigma = TYPHOON_LOGNORMAL_MU, TYPHOON_LOGNORMAL_SIGMA
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"year_type must be 'normal' or 'typhoon', got '{year_type}'")

    if n_samples <= 0:
        raise ValueError(f"n_samples must be positive, got {n_samples}")

    return rng.lognormal(mean=mu, sigma=sigma, size=n_samples)


def sample_raw_arrivals(
    year_type: str,
    n_years: int,
    rng: np.random.Generator,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Sample annual outage arrival counts from target Poisson(lambda).

    Parameters
    ----------
    year_type : str
        Climate reliability regime: 'normal' or 'typhoon'.
    n_years : int
        Number of annual arrival counts to draw.
    rng : np.random.Generator
        Deterministic numpy random number generator.

    Returns
    -------
    np.ndarray
        1D integer array of annual arrival counts.
    """
    regime = year_type.strip().lower()
    if regime == "normal":
        lam = NORMAL_LAMBDA
    elif regime == "typhoon":
        lam = TYPHOON_LAMBDA
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"year_type must be 'normal' or 'typhoon', got '{year_type}'")

    if n_years <= 0:
        raise ValueError(f"n_years must be positive, got {n_years}")

    return rng.poisson(lam=lam, size=n_years)


def sample_outage_schedule(
    year_type: str,
    rng: np.random.Generator,
    *,
    n_hours: int = DEFAULT_ANNUAL_HOURS,
) -> OutageSchedule:
    """Generate stochastic Poisson outage arrivals, lognormal durations, and typhoon storm windows.

    Conforms to Phase 2 interface contract (docs/context/phase2-interface-contract.md).
    All stochasticity is sourced exclusively from the provided `rng` instance.

    Parameters
    ----------
    year_type : str
        Reliability regime: 'normal' (lambda=3, mu=1.2, sigma=0.8) or
        'typhoon' (lambda=8, mu=2.3, sigma=1.0) per [D-11].
    rng : np.random.Generator
        Deterministic numpy random number generator.
    n_hours : int, optional
        Simulation horizon in hours (defaults to 8,760 for an annual cycle).

    Returns
    -------
    OutageSchedule
        Immutable schedule with non-overlapping OutageEvent records, grid_available
        boolean mask, and solar ghi_multiplier array.
    """
    regime = year_type.strip().lower()
    if regime not in ("normal", "typhoon"):
        raise ValueError(f"year_type must be 'normal' or 'typhoon', got '{year_type}'")

    if n_hours <= 0:
        raise ValueError(f"n_hours must be positive, got {n_hours}")

    if regime == "normal":
        lam = NORMAL_LAMBDA
        mu = NORMAL_LOGNORMAL_MU
        sigma = NORMAL_LOGNORMAL_SIGMA
        outage_type = OutageType.NORMAL
    else:
        lam = TYPHOON_LAMBDA
        mu = TYPHOON_LOGNORMAL_MU
        sigma = TYPHOON_LOGNORMAL_SIGMA
        outage_type = OutageType.TYPHOON

    # Sample arrival count from Poisson process
    k = int(rng.poisson(lam=lam))

    if k == 0:
        return OutageSchedule(
            events=(),
            grid_available=np.ones(n_hours, dtype=bool),
            year_type=regime,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
            ghi_multiplier=np.ones(n_hours, dtype=np.float64),
        )

    # [ASSUMPTION] Arrival start hours are sampled uniformly over the annual timeline [1, n_hours].
    # Uniform start distribution is adopted as the rigorous baseline, deferring seasonal typhoon
    # clustering (June-November) to future sensitivity refinements per [D-33].
    raw_starts = rng.integers(1, n_hours + 1, size=k)
    raw_durations = rng.lognormal(mean=mu, sigma=sigma, size=k)

    # [ASSUMPTION] Discretization to integer hours via ceiling (math.ceil) with minimum 1 hour:
    # duration_h = max(1, int(math.ceil(d_raw))). This ensures any non-zero outage duration spans
    # at least one discrete simulation hour, introducing a slight upward bias (+0.5h on average).
    order = np.argsort(raw_starts)
    sorted_starts = [int(raw_starts[i]) for i in order]
    sorted_durations = [max(1, int(math.ceil(raw_durations[i]))) for i in order]

    # [ASSUMPTION] Overlapping or contiguous outage intervals are merged into a single continuous
    # event with start = start_1 and end = max(end_1, end_2). This prevents simultaneous/nested
    # outages and ensures grid_available is strictly consistent with discrete OutageEvents.
    # [ASSUMPTION] Events extending past n_hours are clipped at hour n_hours + 1.
    merged_intervals: list[tuple[int, int]] = []
    for s, d in zip(sorted_starts, sorted_durations):
        if s > n_hours:
            continue
        e = min(s + d, n_hours + 1)
        if not merged_intervals:
            merged_intervals.append((s, e))
        else:
            last_s, last_e = merged_intervals[-1]
            if s <= last_e:
                # Contiguous or overlapping: merge
                merged_intervals[-1] = (last_s, max(last_e, e))
            else:
                merged_intervals.append((s, e))

    # Build grid_available boolean mask (True = connected, False = blackout)
    grid_available = np.ones(n_hours, dtype=bool)
    for s, e in merged_intervals:
        grid_available[s - 1 : e - 1] = False

    # Build solar GHI multiplier and OutageEvents
    ghi_multiplier = np.ones(n_hours, dtype=np.float64)
    events: list[OutageEvent] = []

    if regime == "normal":
        for s, e in merged_intervals:
            events.append(
                OutageEvent(
                    start_hour=s,
                    duration_hours=float(e - s),
                    outage_type=outage_type,
                    solar_depression_factor=1.0,
                    solar_depression_duration_hours=0.0,
                )
            )
    else:
        # Typhoon regime: assign solar depression windows
        # [ASSUMPTION] Resolve [D-11] ambiguity ("depressed solar irradiance (20-30% normal GHI)")
        # as an attenuation multiplier m in [0.20, 0.30] (GHI reduced to 20-30% of normal clear-sky
        # level due to dense typhoon cloud cover) per [D-33].
        # [ASSUMPTION] 1 to 2 storm episodes are coupled with the longest outage events, lasting
        # 3 to 5 consecutive days (72-120 hours) starting at the outage event start hour.
        if merged_intervals:
            # Rank merged intervals by duration descending to identify major storm events
            durations = [e - s for s, e in merged_intervals]
            sorted_by_duration = np.argsort(durations)[::-1]
            n_episodes = min(len(merged_intervals), int(rng.integers(1, 3)))
            storm_indices = set(sorted_by_duration[:n_episodes])

            storm_params: dict[int, tuple[float, float]] = {}
            for idx in storm_indices:
                storm_days = int(rng.integers(TYPHOON_STORM_DAYS_MIN, TYPHOON_STORM_DAYS_MAX + 1))
                dep_hours = float(storm_days * 24)
                dep_factor = float(rng.uniform(TYPHOON_GHI_MULTIPLIER_MIN, TYPHOON_GHI_MULTIPLIER_MAX))
                storm_params[idx] = (dep_factor, dep_hours)

                s_storm = merged_intervals[idx][0]
                dep_end = min(n_hours, s_storm - 1 + int(dep_hours))
                ghi_multiplier[s_storm - 1 : dep_end] = np.minimum(
                    ghi_multiplier[s_storm - 1 : dep_end],
                    dep_factor,
                )

            for idx, (s, e) in enumerate(merged_intervals):
                if idx in storm_params:
                    factor, dur_h = storm_params[idx]
                    events.append(
                        OutageEvent(
                            start_hour=s,
                            duration_hours=float(e - s),
                            outage_type=OutageType.TYPHOON,
                            solar_depression_factor=factor,
                            solar_depression_duration_hours=dur_h,
                        )
                    )
                else:
                    events.append(
                        OutageEvent(
                            start_hour=s,
                            duration_hours=float(e - s),
                            outage_type=OutageType.TYPHOON,
                            solar_depression_factor=1.0,
                            solar_depression_duration_hours=0.0,
                        )
                    )

    return OutageSchedule(
        events=tuple(events),
        grid_available=grid_available,
        year_type=regime,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        ghi_multiplier=ghi_multiplier,
    )


def apply_ghi_multiplier(
    weather_df: pd.DataFrame,
    schedule: OutageSchedule,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Attenuate solar GHI during active typhoon depression windows.

    Conforms to Phase 2 interface contract (docs/context/phase2-interface-contract.md).
    Returns a copy of weather_df with the 'ghi' column scaled by schedule.ghi_multiplier.

    Parameters
    ----------
    weather_df : pd.DataFrame
        Hourly weather dataframe containing at least a 'ghi' column.
    schedule : OutageSchedule
        Outage schedule containing the ghi_multiplier array.

    Returns
    -------
    pd.DataFrame
        Deep copy of weather_df with 'ghi' multiplied by schedule.ghi_multiplier.
    """
    if not isinstance(weather_df, pd.DataFrame):
        raise TypeError(f"weather_df must be a pandas DataFrame, got {type(weather_df)}")

    if "ghi" not in weather_df.columns:
        raise ValueError("weather_df must contain a 'ghi' column.")

    if len(weather_df) != len(schedule.ghi_multiplier):
        raise ValueError(
            f"Length mismatch: weather_df ({len(weather_df)}) vs "
            f"schedule.ghi_multiplier ({len(schedule.ghi_multiplier)})"
        )

    df_copy = weather_df.copy(deep=True)
    df_copy["ghi"] = df_copy["ghi"] * schedule.ghi_multiplier
    return df_copy


def validate_duration_distribution(
    durations: Sequence[float] | np.ndarray,
    year_type: str = "normal",
    *,
    target_mu: float | None = None,
    target_sigma: float | None = None,
    alpha: float = 0.01,
    arrival_counts: Sequence[int] | np.ndarray | None = None,
    target_lambda: float | None = None,
) -> OutageDistributionValidationResult:
    """Validate sample outage durations against Lognormal and arrival counts against Poisson.

    Performs a two-sided Kolmogorov-Smirnov test on pre-discretization durations against
    the continuous Lognormal(mu, sigma) distribution, and an optional arrival count z-test
    against Poisson(lambda) per OUT-001.4.

    Parameters
    ----------
    durations : Sequence[float] | np.ndarray
        Continuous (pre-discretization) duration samples in hours.
    year_type : str, optional
        Regime to determine default parameters: 'normal' or 'typhoon'.
    target_mu : float, optional
        Target log-mean parameter (overrides year_type default if provided).
    target_sigma : float, optional
        Target log-std parameter (overrides year_type default if provided).
    alpha : float, optional
        Significance level threshold (defaults to 0.01).
    arrival_counts : Sequence[int] | np.ndarray, optional
        Sample annual arrival counts from multiple simulated years.
    target_lambda : float, optional
        Target Poisson arrival rate (overrides year_type default if provided).

    Returns
    -------
    OutageDistributionValidationResult
        Diagnostic object containing test statistics, p-values, and pass/fail verdicts.
    """
    regime = year_type.strip().lower()

    # Resolve target Lognormal parameters
    if target_mu is not None and target_sigma is not None:
        mu = float(target_mu)
        sigma = float(target_sigma)
    elif regime == "normal":
        mu = NORMAL_LOGNORMAL_MU
        sigma = NORMAL_LOGNORMAL_SIGMA
    elif regime == "typhoon":
        mu = TYPHOON_LOGNORMAL_MU
        sigma = TYPHOON_LOGNORMAL_SIGMA
    else:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Unknown year_type '{year_type}', expected 'normal' or 'typhoon' "
            "unless target_mu and target_sigma are explicitly provided."
        )

    # Resolve target Poisson arrival rate
    if target_lambda is not None:
        lam = float(target_lambda)
    elif regime == "normal":
        lam = NORMAL_LAMBDA
    elif regime == "typhoon":
        lam = TYPHOON_LAMBDA
    else:
        lam = NORMAL_LAMBDA

    d_arr = np.asarray(durations, dtype=np.float64)
    if len(d_arr) < 2:
        raise ValueError("Need at least 2 duration samples to perform Kolmogorov-Smirnov test.")

    if np.any(d_arr <= 0.0):
        raise ValueError("Durations must be strictly positive for Lognormal distribution.")

    # Two-sided Kolmogorov-Smirnov test
    # In scipy.stats.lognorm: s = sigma, loc = 0, scale = exp(mu)
    ks_res = stats.kstest(d_arr, "lognorm", args=(sigma, 0.0, np.exp(mu)))
    ks_stat = float(ks_res.statistic)
    ks_pval = float(ks_res.pvalue)
    ks_passed = bool(ks_pval >= alpha)

    # Optional Poisson arrival check
    arrival_passed: bool | None = None
    mean_arrivals: float | None = None
    arrival_pval: float | None = None

    if arrival_counts is not None:
        k_arr = np.asarray(arrival_counts, dtype=np.float64)
        m_years = len(k_arr)
        if m_years == 0:
            raise ValueError("arrival_counts array must not be empty.")

        mean_arrivals = float(np.mean(k_arr))
        # For sample mean from Poisson(lambda), standard error is sqrt(lambda / M)
        se = math.sqrt(lam / m_years)
        if se > 0.0:
            z = (mean_arrivals - lam) / se
            # Two-sided normal test
            arrival_pval = float(2.0 * (1.0 - stats.norm.cdf(abs(z))))
            arrival_passed = bool(arrival_pval >= alpha)
        else:
            arrival_passed = bool(math.isclose(mean_arrivals, lam))
            arrival_pval = 1.0 if arrival_passed else 0.0

    overall_passed = ks_passed and (arrival_passed is None or arrival_passed)

    details = (
        f"KS Test: stat={ks_stat:.4f}, pval={ks_pval:.4e}, passed={ks_passed} (alpha={alpha}). "
        f"Target Lognormal(mu={mu}, sigma={sigma}), N={len(d_arr)}."
    )
    if arrival_counts is not None:
        details += (
            f" Arrival Check: mean={mean_arrivals:.4f}, target={lam:.4f}, "
            f"pval={arrival_pval:.4e}, passed={arrival_passed}."
        )

    return OutageDistributionValidationResult(
        ks_statistic=ks_stat,
        ks_pvalue=ks_pval,
        ks_passed=ks_passed,
        alpha=alpha,
        sample_size=len(d_arr),
        target_mu=mu,
        target_sigma=sigma,
        arrival_passed=arrival_passed,
        mean_arrivals=mean_arrivals,
        target_lambda=lam,
        arrival_pvalue=arrival_pval,
        passed=overall_passed,
        details=details,
    )
```

### `src/ccis_sim/outage/shedding.py`

*402 lines. Priority-based tiered load shedding controller and unserved energy tracker (OUT-002).*

```python
"""Priority-based tiered load shedding controller and unserved energy tracker (OUT-002).

Implements deterministic hourly load shedding policies and unserved energy accounting
conforming to:
- docs/context/phase2-interface-contract.md (§3 Manifest, §4.3 Shedding vs Reserve)
- docs/decisions/decision-log.md [D-10] (Priority tiers), [D-16]/[D-19] (Audit figures),
  [D-17] (Emergency reserve separation), [D-34] (Shedding policy & reserve threshold).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any, Mapping

from ccis_sim.core.interfaces import ShedResult
from ccis_sim.core.models import Battery, LoadTier
from ccis_sim.demand.equipment import EquipmentAudit


def shed_for_hour(
    tier_demand_kwh: tuple[float, float, float],
    *,
    grid_available: bool,
    soc_kwh: float,
    battery: Battery,
    tier2_shed_soc_fraction: float = 0.20,
) -> ShedResult:
    """Evaluate priority-based load shedding for a single simulation hour.

    Operational Policy per [D-10], [D-17], and [D-34]:
    1. Grid Connected (grid_available = True):
       - Normal grid supply is available. All electrical load tiers are fully served.
       - Active policy tier is LoadTier.TIER_3_SHEDDABLE (all tiers served).
       - Served = tier_demand_kwh, Unserved = (0.0, 0.0, 0.0).
    2. Utility Outage / Blackout (grid_available = False):
       - Tier 3 (comfort cooling HVAC, non-essential PC labs, discretionary loads) is
         shed immediately upon grid failure ([D-10]).
       - Tier 2 Shedding Rule:
         If battery State-of-Charge is strictly below `tier2_shed_soc_fraction * capacity_kwh`
         (defaulting to 20% SoC per [D-34]), Tier 2 (faculty rooms, admin, servers/smart rooms)
         is also shed to preserve remaining chemical capacity for Tier 1 life-safety.
         - active_tier = LoadTier.TIER_1_CRITICAL (value 1)
         - Served = (tier1, 0.0, 0.0), Unserved = (0.0, tier2, tier3).
       - If SoC is greater than or equal to the threshold (`soc_kwh >= threshold`):
         Both Tier 1 and Tier 2 are served.
         - active_tier = LoadTier.TIER_2_ESSENTIAL (value 2)
         - Served = (tier1, tier2, 0.0), Unserved = (0.0, 0.0, tier3).

    Conservation Invariant:
        For every tier i in (0, 1, 2):
        served_kwh[i] + unserved_kwh[i] == tier_demand_kwh[i]

    Args:
        tier_demand_kwh: 3-tuple containing hourly electrical demand in kWh for
            (Tier 1 Critical, Tier 2 Essential, Tier 3 Sheddable).
        grid_available: Boolean indicating utility grid energization status (True = normal, False = outage).
        soc_kwh: Current battery usable State-of-Charge at start of hour in kWh.
        battery: Battery specification dataclass defining total storage capacity.
        tier2_shed_soc_fraction: SoC fraction threshold below which Tier 2 loads are shed
            during blackouts ([ASSUMPTION]: defaults to 0.20 per [D-34], independent of
            Battery.emergency_reserve_pct).

    Returns:
        ShedResult containing active_tier, served_kwh 3-tuple, and unserved_kwh 3-tuple.

    Raises:
        TypeError: If tier_demand_kwh is not a tuple or battery is not a Battery instance.
        ValueError: If tier_demand_kwh elements or soc_kwh are negative or non-finite,
            or if tier2_shed_soc_fraction is not in [0.0, 1.0].
    """
    # Input validations
    if not isinstance(tier_demand_kwh, tuple) or len(tier_demand_kwh) != 3:
        raise TypeError(f"tier_demand_kwh must be a 3-tuple, got {type(tier_demand_kwh)}")

    for i, d in enumerate(tier_demand_kwh):
        if not isinstance(d, (int, float)):
            raise TypeError(f"tier_demand_kwh[{i}] must be numeric, got {type(d)}")
        if math.isnan(d) or math.isinf(d):
            raise ValueError(f"tier_demand_kwh[{i}] must be finite, got {d}")
        if d < 0.0:
            raise ValueError(f"tier_demand_kwh[{i}] must be non-negative, got {d}")

    if not isinstance(soc_kwh, (int, float)):
        raise TypeError(f"soc_kwh must be numeric, got {type(soc_kwh)}")
    if math.isnan(soc_kwh) or math.isinf(soc_kwh):
        raise ValueError(f"soc_kwh must be finite, got {soc_kwh}")
    if soc_kwh < 0.0:
        raise ValueError(f"soc_kwh must be non-negative, got {soc_kwh}")

    if not isinstance(tier2_shed_soc_fraction, (int, float)):
        raise TypeError(f"tier2_shed_soc_fraction must be numeric, got {type(tier2_shed_soc_fraction)}")
    if not (0.0 <= tier2_shed_soc_fraction <= 1.0):
        raise ValueError(f"tier2_shed_soc_fraction must be in [0.0, 1.0], got {tier2_shed_soc_fraction}")

    t1, t2, t3 = float(tier_demand_kwh[0]), float(tier_demand_kwh[1]), float(tier_demand_kwh[2])

    if grid_available:
        # [ASSUMPTION]: Grid up -> all tiers served, active tier 3
        return ShedResult(
            active_tier=LoadTier.TIER_3_SHEDDABLE,
            served_kwh=(t1, t2, t3),
            unserved_kwh=(0.0, 0.0, 0.0),
        )

    # Grid Unavailable (Outage / Blackout)
    # [ASSUMPTION]: Tier 3 shed immediately upon utility failure
    tier2_threshold_kwh = tier2_shed_soc_fraction * battery.capacity_kwh

    if soc_kwh < tier2_threshold_kwh:
        # [ASSUMPTION]: Deep discharge protection: below 20% SoC, shed Tier 2 to preserve Tier 1
        return ShedResult(
            active_tier=LoadTier.TIER_1_CRITICAL,
            served_kwh=(t1, 0.0, 0.0),
            unserved_kwh=(0.0, t2, t3),
        )

    # SoC is at or above the Tier 2 shedding threshold: Tier 1 and Tier 2 served
    return ShedResult(
        active_tier=LoadTier.TIER_2_ESSENTIAL,
        served_kwh=(t1, t2, 0.0),
        unserved_kwh=(0.0, 0.0, t3),
    )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TierAuditComparison:
    """Summary of audited equipment inventory load distribution compared to D-10 nominal targets."""

    total_connected_kw: float
    tier1_connected_kw: float
    tier2_connected_kw: float
    tier3_connected_kw: float
    tier1_connected_pct: float
    tier2_connected_pct: float
    tier3_connected_pct: float
    nominal_tier1_pct: float = 2.0
    nominal_tier2_pct: float = 18.0
    nominal_tier3_pct: float = 80.0

    @property
    def tier1_delta_pct(self) -> float:
        """Difference between audited connected percentage and nominal D-10 target (percentage points)."""
        return self.tier1_connected_pct - self.nominal_tier1_pct

    @property
    def tier2_delta_pct(self) -> float:
        """Difference between audited connected percentage and nominal D-10 target (percentage points)."""
        return self.tier2_connected_pct - self.nominal_tier2_pct

    @property
    def tier3_delta_pct(self) -> float:
        """Difference between audited connected percentage and nominal D-10 target (percentage points)."""
        return self.tier3_connected_pct - self.nominal_tier3_pct

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, float]:
        """Convert comparison metrics to dictionary representation."""
        return {
            "total_connected_kw": round(self.total_connected_kw, 4),
            "tier1_connected_kw": round(self.tier1_connected_kw, 4),
            "tier2_connected_kw": round(self.tier2_connected_kw, 4),
            "tier3_connected_kw": round(self.tier3_connected_kw, 4),
            "tier1_connected_pct": round(self.tier1_connected_pct, 4),
            "tier2_connected_pct": round(self.tier2_connected_pct, 4),
            "tier3_connected_pct": round(self.tier3_connected_pct, 4),
            "nominal_tier1_pct": self.nominal_tier1_pct,
            "nominal_tier2_pct": self.nominal_tier2_pct,
            "nominal_tier3_pct": self.nominal_tier3_pct,
            "tier1_delta_pct": round(self.tier1_delta_pct, 4),
            "tier2_delta_pct": round(self.tier2_delta_pct, 4),
            "tier3_delta_pct": round(self.tier3_delta_pct, 4),
        }


def compute_tier_audit_shares(audit: EquipmentAudit) -> TierAuditComparison:
    """Compute tier shares of connected electrical capacity from equipment audit inventory (OUT-002.1).

    Compares the audited nameplate capacity against the nominal 2/18/80 split established in [D-10].
    Per [D-16], [D-19], and [D-34], the audit yields:
    - Total: 147.805 kW (~147.81 kW)
    - Tier 1: 1.215 kW (~0.82% connected, ~1.5% daytime peak)
    - Tier 2: 15.360 kW (~10.39% connected, ~17.0% daytime peak)
    - Tier 3: 131.230 kW (~88.79% connected, ~81.5% daytime peak)

    The discrepancy between connected shares and nominal targets is reported without forcing artificial scaling.

    Args:
        audit: Loaded EquipmentAudit instance containing facility electrical equipment list.

    Returns:
        TierAuditComparison dataclass containing exact connected capacities and percentage deltas.
    """
    t1_kw = audit.total_connected_kw(1)
    t2_kw = audit.total_connected_kw(2)
    t3_kw = audit.total_connected_kw(3)
    total_kw = audit.total_connected_kw()

    if total_kw <= 0.0:
        t1_pct = t2_pct = t3_pct = 0.0
    else:
        t1_pct = (t1_kw / total_kw) * 100.0
        t2_pct = (t2_kw / total_kw) * 100.0
        t3_pct = (t3_kw / total_kw) * 100.0

    return TierAuditComparison(
        total_connected_kw=total_kw,
        tier1_connected_kw=t1_kw,
        tier2_connected_kw=t2_kw,
        tier3_connected_kw=t3_kw,
        tier1_connected_pct=t1_pct,
        tier2_connected_pct=t2_pct,
        tier3_connected_pct=t3_pct,
    )


@dataclass
class UnservedTracker:
    """State tracker accumulating per-tier unserved electrical energy and outage metrics (OUT-002.4).

    Tracks cumulative unserved energy and evaluates whether physical delivery shortfalls
    occurred during outages. If battery discharge falls short of policy served demand,
    the shortfall is allocated strictly in reverse-priority order (lowest-priority
    active tier first).

    Attributes:
        total_hours: Total simulation timesteps recorded.
        total_outage_hours: Number of hours where grid_available was False.
        outage_hours_tier1_served: Number of outage hours where Tier 1 was 100% served.
        outage_hours_tier2_served: Number of outage hours where Tier 2 was 100% served.
        outage_hours_tier3_served: Number of outage hours where Tier 3 was 100% served.
        unserved_tier1_kwh: Cumulative unserved energy for Tier 1 Critical load (kWh).
        unserved_tier2_kwh: Cumulative unserved energy for Tier 2 Essential load (kWh).
        unserved_tier3_kwh: Cumulative unserved energy for Tier 3 Sheddable load (kWh).
        served_tier1_kwh: Cumulative served energy for Tier 1 Critical load (kWh).
        served_tier2_kwh: Cumulative served energy for Tier 2 Essential load (kWh).
        served_tier3_kwh: Cumulative served energy for Tier 3 Sheddable load (kWh).
    """

    total_hours: int = 0
    total_outage_hours: int = 0
    outage_hours_tier1_served: int = 0
    outage_hours_tier2_served: int = 0
    outage_hours_tier3_served: int = 0
    unserved_tier1_kwh: float = 0.0
    unserved_tier2_kwh: float = 0.0
    unserved_tier3_kwh: float = 0.0
    served_tier1_kwh: float = 0.0
    served_tier2_kwh: float = 0.0
    served_tier3_kwh: float = 0.0

    @property
    def total_unserved_kwh(self) -> float:
        """Total cumulative unserved electrical energy across all tiers (kWh)."""
        return self.unserved_tier1_kwh + self.unserved_tier2_kwh + self.unserved_tier3_kwh

    @property
    def total_served_kwh(self) -> float:
        """Total cumulative served electrical energy across all tiers (kWh)."""
        return self.served_tier1_kwh + self.served_tier2_kwh + self.served_tier3_kwh

    @property
    def outage_hours_tier1_served_pct(self) -> float:
        """Percentage of outage hours where Tier 1 was fully served (0.0 to 100.0%)."""
        if self.total_outage_hours == 0:
            return 100.0
        return (self.outage_hours_tier1_served / self.total_outage_hours) * 100.0

    @property
    def outage_hours_tier2_served_pct(self) -> float:
        """Percentage of outage hours where Tier 2 was fully served (0.0 to 100.0%)."""
        if self.total_outage_hours == 0:
            return 100.0
        return (self.outage_hours_tier2_served / self.total_outage_hours) * 100.0

    @property
    def outage_hours_tier3_served_pct(self) -> float:
        """Percentage of outage hours where Tier 3 was fully served (0.0 to 100.0%)."""
        if self.total_outage_hours == 0:
            return 100.0
        return (self.outage_hours_tier3_served / self.total_outage_hours) * 100.0

    def record(
        self,
        result: ShedResult,
        *,
        grid_available: bool,
        battery_shortfall_kwh: float = 0.0,
    ) -> tuple[tuple[float, float, float], tuple[float, float, float]]:
        """Record hourly load shedding outcome and account for physical battery delivery shortfall.

        Shortfall Allocation Principle [D-34]:
        When the microgrid battery cannot physically deliver the full policy-served demand
        (`battery_shortfall_kwh > 0.0`), the shortfall cuts into served energy starting with
        the lowest-priority active tier:
        1. Tier 3 served is cut first until reduced to 0.0.
        2. Any remaining shortfall cuts into Tier 2 served until reduced to 0.0.
        3. Any remaining shortfall cuts into Tier 1 served.

        Energy conservation is strictly maintained:
        actual_served[i] + actual_unserved[i] == result.served_kwh[i] + result.unserved_kwh[i].

        Args:
            result: ShedResult returned by `shed_for_hour`.
            grid_available: Utility grid availability status.
            battery_shortfall_kwh: Unmet discharge energy in kWh where requested battery power
                could not be supplied due to power/energy exhaustion (non-negative).

        Returns:
            A tuple of (actual_served_kwh, actual_unserved_kwh) 3-tuples for the hour.

        Raises:
            ValueError: If battery_shortfall_kwh is negative or non-finite.
        """
        if not isinstance(battery_shortfall_kwh, (int, float)):
            raise TypeError(f"battery_shortfall_kwh must be numeric, got {type(battery_shortfall_kwh)}")
        if math.isnan(battery_shortfall_kwh) or math.isinf(battery_shortfall_kwh):
            raise ValueError(f"battery_shortfall_kwh must be finite, got {battery_shortfall_kwh}")
        if battery_shortfall_kwh < 0.0:
            raise ValueError(f"battery_shortfall_kwh must be non-negative, got {battery_shortfall_kwh}")

        s1, s2, s3 = result.served_kwh
        u1, u2, u3 = result.unserved_kwh

        # Reverse priority shortfall allocation: Tier 3 -> Tier 2 -> Tier 1
        rem = float(battery_shortfall_kwh)

        cut3 = min(s3, rem)
        rem -= cut3

        cut2 = min(s2, rem)
        rem -= cut2

        cut1 = min(s1, rem)
        rem -= cut1

        # Effective actual flows
        act_s1 = s1 - cut1
        act_s2 = s2 - cut2
        act_s3 = s3 - cut3

        act_u1 = u1 + cut1
        act_u2 = u2 + cut2
        act_u3 = u3 + cut3

        # Accumulate metrics
        self.unserved_tier1_kwh += act_u1
        self.unserved_tier2_kwh += act_u2
        self.unserved_tier3_kwh += act_u3

        self.served_tier1_kwh += act_s1
        self.served_tier2_kwh += act_s2
        self.served_tier3_kwh += act_s3

        self.total_hours += 1

        if not grid_available:
            self.total_outage_hours += 1

            # A tier is fully served in an outage hour if actual unserved energy is zero
            if act_u1 <= 1e-6:
                self.outage_hours_tier1_served += 1
            if act_u2 <= 1e-6:
                self.outage_hours_tier2_served += 1
            if act_u3 <= 1e-6:
                self.outage_hours_tier3_served += 1

        return (act_s1, act_s2, act_s3), (act_u1, act_u2, act_u3)

    def summary(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Produce a comprehensive dictionary of unserved load statistics and outage resilience."""
        return {
            "total_hours": self.total_hours,
            "total_outage_hours": self.total_outage_hours,
            "outage_hours_tier1_served": self.outage_hours_tier1_served,
            "outage_hours_tier2_served": self.outage_hours_tier2_served,
            "outage_hours_tier3_served": self.outage_hours_tier3_served,
            "outage_hours_tier1_served_pct": round(self.outage_hours_tier1_served_pct, 2),
            "outage_hours_tier2_served_pct": round(self.outage_hours_tier2_served_pct, 2),
            "outage_hours_tier3_served_pct": round(self.outage_hours_tier3_served_pct, 2),
            "unserved_tier1_kwh": round(self.unserved_tier1_kwh, 6),
            "unserved_tier2_kwh": round(self.unserved_tier2_kwh, 6),
            "unserved_tier3_kwh": round(self.unserved_tier3_kwh, 6),
            "total_unserved_kwh": round(self.total_unserved_kwh, 6),
            "served_tier1_kwh": round(self.served_tier1_kwh, 6),
            "served_tier2_kwh": round(self.served_tier2_kwh, 6),
            "served_tier3_kwh": round(self.served_tier3_kwh, 6),
            "total_served_kwh": round(self.total_served_kwh, 6),
        }

    def reset(self) -> None:
        """Reset all accumulator variables to zero."""
        self.total_hours = 0
        self.total_outage_hours = 0
        self.outage_hours_tier1_served = 0
        self.outage_hours_tier2_served = 0
        self.outage_hours_tier3_served = 0
        self.unserved_tier1_kwh = 0.0
        self.unserved_tier2_kwh = 0.0
        self.unserved_tier3_kwh = 0.0
        self.served_tier1_kwh = 0.0
        self.served_tier2_kwh = 0.0
        self.served_tier3_kwh = 0.0
```
