### `src/ccis_sim/solar/array.py`

*165 lines. Solar PV array capacity scaling, inverter clipping, and balance-of-system losses.*

```python
"""Solar PV array capacity scaling, inverter clipping, and balance-of-system losses.

Implements conversion of hourly DC generation per kWp (from SOL-001) into AC power
generation (kWh/h) for sized rooftop PV arrays across scenario capacities
(0, 50, 100, 200 kWp per [D-18]), modeling inverter efficiency (98%), DC/AC clipping,
and non-temperature balance-of-system (BOS) derating per decisions [D-08] and [D-30].

Conforms to Phase 2 interface contract (docs/context/phase2-interface-contract.md).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging

import numpy as np

from ccis_sim.core.models import SolarArray

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# [ASSUMPTION] Peak inverter efficiency per [D-08] and [D-30]
DEFAULT_INVERTER_EFFICIENCY: float = 0.980  # 98.0% peak inverter efficiency (SOL-002.2)

# [ASSUMPTION] Commercial rooftop inverter loading ratio (DC/AC ratio) per [D-30]
# Standard IEEE/NREL sizing convention optimizing inverter utilization under tropical thermal derates
DEFAULT_DC_AC_RATIO: float = 1.20

# [ASSUMPTION] Named balance-of-system (BOS) derate factors per [D-08] and [D-30]
# Decomposed so that Non-Thermal BOS * Inverter Efficiency * Assumed Mean Thermal ≈ 0.77 PR
DEFAULT_SOILING_FACTOR: float = 0.960       # 4.0% seasonal soiling/dust loss in Batac
DEFAULT_DC_WIRING_FACTOR: float = 0.985     # 1.5% DC ohmic cabling and connection loss
DEFAULT_AC_WIRING_FACTOR: float = 0.990     # 1.0% AC collection and transformer loss
DEFAULT_MISMATCH_FACTOR: float = 0.980      # 2.0% module mismatch and manufacturer nameplate tolerance
DEFAULT_AVAILABILITY_FACTOR: float = 0.990  # 1.0% operational availability and maintenance downtime

# [ASSUMPTION] Assumed annual mean thermal factor representing cell temperature losses
# Derived from physical NOCT thermal modeling in Batac tropical climate (~13.49% mean thermal loss) per [D-30]
ASSUMED_MEAN_THERMAL_FACTOR: float = 0.8650888630630106

# [ASSUMPTION] Implied annual energy-weighted cell thermal derating factor evaluated upstream
# by SOL-001 under the 2019 Batac weather fixture (SOL-002.4 / [D-35]).
# (Derated DC yield 1,695.05 kWh/kWp / Un-derated POA insolation 1,945.98 kWh/m2 ≈ 0.871056; ~12.89% thermal loss).
IMPLIED_ANNUAL_THERMAL_FACTOR_2019: float = 0.8710563456728557


def get_non_thermal_bos_factor(
    *,
    soiling: float = DEFAULT_SOILING_FACTOR,
    dc_wiring: float = DEFAULT_DC_WIRING_FACTOR,
    ac_wiring: float = DEFAULT_AC_WIRING_FACTOR,
    mismatch: float = DEFAULT_MISMATCH_FACTOR,
    availability: float = DEFAULT_AVAILABILITY_FACTOR,
) -> float:
    """Calculate the cumulative non-thermal Balance-of-System (BOS) derating factor.

    Product of named non-thermal loss factors:
    f_bos = soiling * dc_wiring * ac_wiring * mismatch * availability

    Returns:
        Cumulative dimensionless non-thermal BOS derate factor (~0.9082).
    """
    return soiling * dc_wiring * ac_wiring * mismatch * availability


def get_inverter_ac_rating(
    capacity_kwp: float,
    *,
    dc_ac_ratio: float = DEFAULT_DC_AC_RATIO,
) -> float:
    """Calculate the inverter rated continuous AC power capacity (kW AC).

    Formula per [D-30]:
    P_ac_rated = capacity_kwp / dc_ac_ratio

    Args:
        capacity_kwp: Nameplate DC capacity of the PV array (kWp).
        dc_ac_ratio: Inverter loading ratio (default: 1.20).

    Returns:
        Inverter continuous AC power rating in kW.
    """
    if capacity_kwp <= 0.0:
        return 0.0
    if dc_ac_ratio <= 0.0:
        raise ValueError(f"dc_ac_ratio must be strictly positive, got {dc_ac_ratio}")
    return capacity_kwp / dc_ac_ratio


def compute_ac_generation(
    dc_kw_per_kwp: np.ndarray,
    array: SolarArray,
    *,
    inverter_efficiency: float = DEFAULT_INVERTER_EFFICIENCY,
    dc_ac_ratio: float = DEFAULT_DC_AC_RATIO,
    soiling: float = DEFAULT_SOILING_FACTOR,
    dc_wiring: float = DEFAULT_DC_WIRING_FACTOR,
    ac_wiring: float = DEFAULT_AC_WIRING_FACTOR,
    mismatch: float = DEFAULT_MISMATCH_FACTOR,
    availability: float = DEFAULT_AVAILABILITY_FACTOR,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Compute hourly AC power generation for a sized solar PV array.

    Scales array capacity, models inverter peak efficiency and DC/AC clipping limit,
    and applies non-thermal balance-of-system (BOS) loss factors to produce hourly
    AC power output. Does not double-count temperature losses, as temperature derating
    is explicitly modeled upstream in dc_kw_per_kwp (SOL-001).

    Conforms to Phase 2 interface contract (docs/context/phase2-interface-contract.md).

    Args:
        dc_kw_per_kwp: 1D numpy array of length N (8,760) representing hourly DC yield
            per unit capacity (kW DC / kWp), computed by SOL-001.
        array: SolarArray instance specifying capacity_kwp and parameters.
        inverter_efficiency: Inverter peak conversion efficiency (default: 0.98 per [D-30]).
        dc_ac_ratio: Inverter DC/AC sizing ratio for clipping (default: 1.20 per [D-30]).
        soiling: Soiling derating factor (default: 0.96).
        dc_wiring: DC wiring derating factor (default: 0.985).
        ac_wiring: AC wiring derating factor (default: 0.99).
        mismatch: Module mismatch derating factor (default: 0.98).
        availability: System availability derating factor (default: 0.99).

    Returns:
        1D numpy float64 array of length N representing hourly AC energy output in kWh/h
        (numerically equivalent to kW AC at Δt = 1.0 h). Guaranteed strictly non-negative,
        bounded by the inverter AC rating, and 0.0 at night and for 0 kWp arrays.
    """
    dc_in = np.asarray(dc_kw_per_kwp, dtype=np.float64)

    if len(dc_in) == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(dc_in)):
        raise ValueError("Input 'dc_kw_per_kwp' contains non-finite values (NaN or inf).")
    if np.any(dc_in < 0.0):
        raise ValueError("Input 'dc_kw_per_kwp' contains negative values.")

    # Capacity scenario 0 kWp returns strict zero array
    if array.capacity_kwp == 0.0:
        return np.zeros_like(dc_in)

    # 1. Scale DC power to nameplate capacity
    # dc_raw in kW DC
    dc_raw = dc_in * array.capacity_kwp

    # 2. Apply DC-side balance-of-system losses (soiling, DC wiring, mismatch, availability)
    dc_bos_factor = soiling * dc_wiring * mismatch * availability
    dc_at_inverter = dc_raw * dc_bos_factor

    # 3. Inverter conversion and clipping
    # Gross AC power before clipping (kW)
    ac_gross = dc_at_inverter * inverter_efficiency

    # Inverter AC rating limit (kW AC)
    ac_rating = get_inverter_ac_rating(array.capacity_kwp, dc_ac_ratio=dc_ac_ratio)

    # Inverter clipping: AC output cannot exceed continuous rating
    ac_clipped = np.minimum(ac_gross, ac_rating)

    # 4. Apply AC-side collection and wiring loss
    ac_net = ac_clipped * ac_wiring

    # Clean numerical precision and guarantee strict non-negativity
    ac_net = np.maximum(0.0, ac_net)

    return ac_net
```

### `src/ccis_sim/solar/irradiance.py`

*429 lines. Solar irradiance geometry and NOCT cell temperature thermal derating model.*

```python
"""Solar irradiance geometry and NOCT cell temperature thermal derating model.

Calculates hourly plane-of-array (POA) irradiance for a tilted rooftop PV array
at Batac, Ilocos Norte, models cell operating temperature with rooftop mounting
thermal penalty, and applies temperature derating to compute DC power yield per kWp.

Conforms to Phase 2 interface contract (docs/context/phase2-interface-contract.md)
and decisions [D-08] and [D-29].
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from typing import NamedTuple

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# Standard constants
SOLAR_CONSTANT_W_M2 = 1367.0  # W/m^2 solar constant (G_sc)
STC_IRRADIANCE_W_M2 = 1000.0  # W/m^2 Standard Test Conditions (STC) irradiance
STC_CELL_TEMP_C = 25.0  # °C STC cell reference temperature

# Physical modeling parameters per [D-08] and [D-29]
DEFAULT_TILT_DEG = 15.0  # degrees South-facing optimal tilt for Batac (~18.06°N)
DEFAULT_AZIMUTH_DEG = 180.0  # degrees True South (North = 0°, South = 180°)
BATAC_LATITUDE_DEG = 18.06  # degrees North
BATAC_LONGITUDE_DEG = 120.56  # degrees East
STANDARD_MERIDIAN_DEG = 120.0  # degrees East (UTC+8 Philippine Standard Time)

# [ASSUMPTION] Ground diffuse albedo for academic campus surroundings (grass/concrete mix) per [D-29]
DEFAULT_ALBEDO = 0.20

# [ASSUMPTION] Nominal Operating Cell Temperature with +10°C rooftop thermal penalty per [D-08] and [D-29]
DEFAULT_NOCT_C = 45.0  # °C baseline manufacturer NOCT
ROOFTOP_PENALTY_C = 10.0  # °C close-roof mounting thermal penalty per [D-08]
EFFECTIVE_NOCT_C = DEFAULT_NOCT_C + ROOFTOP_PENALTY_C  # 55.0 °C effective NOCT

# Temperature coefficient of power per [D-08]
TEMP_COEFFICIENT_PER_C = -0.0040  # -0.40%/°C for monocrystalline silicon


class SolarPosition(NamedTuple):
    """Astronomical solar position and geometric angles."""

    solar_time_hours: np.ndarray
    hour_angle_rad: np.ndarray
    declination_rad: np.ndarray
    cos_zenith: np.ndarray
    zenith_rad: np.ndarray
    elevation_deg: np.ndarray
    cos_incidence: np.ndarray


def compute_solar_position(
    timestamps: pd.Series | pd.DatetimeIndex | np.ndarray,
    *,
    tilt_deg: float = DEFAULT_TILT_DEG,
    azimuth_deg: float = DEFAULT_AZIMUTH_DEG,
    latitude_deg: float = BATAC_LATITUDE_DEG,
    longitude_deg: float = BATAC_LONGITUDE_DEG,
    standard_meridian_deg: float = STANDARD_MERIDIAN_DEG,
) -> SolarPosition:
    """Compute astronomical solar position and surface incidence angle.

    Uses the Spencer (1971) Fourier series formulation for solar declination
    and Equation of Time (EoT), with longitude correction for local solar time.

    Args:
        timestamps: Series or array of datetimes (UTC+8 / PST).
        tilt_deg: Surface tilt angle from horizontal in degrees (default: 15.0°).
        azimuth_deg: Surface azimuth from True North in degrees (default: 180.0° / South).
        latitude_deg: Site latitude in degrees North (default: 18.06°).
        longitude_deg: Site longitude in degrees East (default: 120.56°).
        standard_meridian_deg: Timezone standard meridian in degrees East (default: 120.0°).

    Returns:
        SolarPosition named tuple containing solar time, hour angle, declination,
        cos(zenith), zenith, elevation, and cos(incidence).
    """
    dti = pd.DatetimeIndex(pd.to_datetime(timestamps))
    day_of_year = dti.dayofyear.values.astype(np.float64)
    local_hours = (
        dti.hour.values.astype(np.float64)
        + dti.minute.values.astype(np.float64) / 60.0
        + dti.second.values.astype(np.float64) / 3600.0
    )

    phi = np.radians(latitude_deg)
    beta = np.radians(tilt_deg)
    # Duffie & Beckman surface azimuth from South: gamma_DB = azimuth - 180°
    gamma_db = np.radians(azimuth_deg - 180.0)

    # Fractional day angle B in radians (Spencer 1971)
    b = 2.0 * np.pi * (day_of_year - 1.0) / 365.0

    # Spencer Fourier series for solar declination (radians)
    delta = (
        0.006918
        - 0.399912 * np.cos(b)
        + 0.070257 * np.sin(b)
        - 0.006758 * np.cos(2.0 * b)
        + 0.000907 * np.sin(2.0 * b)
        - 0.002697 * np.cos(3.0 * b)
        + 0.00148 * np.sin(3.0 * b)
    )

    # Spencer Equation of Time (minutes)
    eot_min = 229.18 * (
        0.000075
        + 0.001868 * np.cos(b)
        - 0.032077 * np.sin(b)
        - 0.014615 * np.cos(2.0 * b)
        - 0.040849 * np.sin(2.0 * b)
    )

    # Longitude time correction (minutes): 4 min per degree East of meridian
    lon_correction_min = 4.0 * (longitude_deg - standard_meridian_deg)

    # Local Solar Time (hours)
    solar_time = local_hours + (eot_min + lon_correction_min) / 60.0

    # Solar hour angle omega (radians, omega = 0 at solar noon)
    omega = np.radians(15.0 * (solar_time - 12.0))

    # Solar zenith angle: cos(theta_z)
    cos_theta_z = np.sin(phi) * np.sin(delta) + np.cos(phi) * np.cos(delta) * np.cos(omega)
    cos_theta_z = np.clip(cos_theta_z, -1.0, 1.0)
    theta_z = np.arccos(cos_theta_z)
    elevation_deg = 90.0 - np.degrees(theta_z)

    # Angle of incidence theta on tilted plane (Duffie & Beckman eq 1.6.2):
    # cos(theta) = sin(delta)*sin(phi)*cos(beta) - sin(delta)*cos(phi)*sin(beta)*cos(gamma_DB)
    #              + cos(delta)*cos(phi)*cos(beta)*cos(omega)
    #              + cos(delta)*sin(phi)*sin(beta)*cos(gamma_DB)*cos(omega)
    #              + cos(delta)*sin(beta)*sin(gamma_DB)*sin(omega)
    cos_incidence = (
        np.sin(delta) * np.sin(phi) * np.cos(beta)
        - np.sin(delta) * np.cos(phi) * np.sin(beta) * np.cos(gamma_db)
        + np.cos(delta) * np.cos(phi) * np.cos(beta) * np.cos(omega)
        + np.cos(delta) * np.sin(phi) * np.sin(beta) * np.cos(gamma_db) * np.cos(omega)
        + np.cos(delta) * np.sin(beta) * np.sin(gamma_db) * np.sin(omega)
    )
    cos_incidence = np.clip(cos_incidence, -1.0, 1.0)

    return SolarPosition(
        solar_time_hours=solar_time,
        hour_angle_rad=omega,
        declination_rad=delta,
        cos_zenith=cos_theta_z,
        zenith_rad=theta_z,
        elevation_deg=elevation_deg,
        cos_incidence=cos_incidence,
    )


def decompose_ghi_erbs(
    ghi_w_m2: np.ndarray,
    cos_zenith: np.ndarray,
    day_of_year: np.ndarray,
) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """Decompose Global Horizontal Irradiance into DHI and DNI via the Erbs model.

    Implements the Erbs, Klein, and Duffie (1982) empirical correlation relating
    the diffuse fraction kd = DHI / GHI to the hourly clearness index kt = GHI / G_0.

    Args:
        ghi_w_m2: 1D array of global horizontal irradiance (W/m^2).
        cos_zenith: 1D array of cos(theta_z) solar zenith cosines.
        day_of_year: 1D array of day numbers (1 to 365/366).

    Returns:
        Tuple of (dhi_w_m2, dni_w_m2) 1D numpy arrays.
    """
    ghi = np.asarray(ghi_w_m2, dtype=np.float64)
    cos_z = np.asarray(cos_zenith, dtype=np.float64)
    doy = np.asarray(day_of_year, dtype=np.float64)

    # Extraterrestrial normal and horizontal irradiance
    # G_on = G_sc * [1 + 0.033 * cos(2*pi*n/365)]
    g_on = SOLAR_CONSTANT_W_M2 * (1.0 + 0.033 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * doy / 365.0))
    g_0 = np.maximum(0.0, g_on * cos_z)

    # Daytime mask: sun above horizon and positive irradiance
    daytime = (ghi > 0.0) & (cos_z > 1e-3)

    # Hourly clearness index kt
    kt = np.zeros_like(ghi)
    safe_g0 = np.maximum(g_0[daytime], 1.0)
    kt[daytime] = np.clip(ghi[daytime] / safe_g0, 0.0, 1.2)

    # Erbs piecewise correlation for diffuse fraction kd
    kd = np.ones_like(ghi)  # Night or zero irradiance defaults to 1.0

    mask1 = daytime & (kt <= 0.22)
    kd[mask1] = 1.0 - 0.09 * kt[mask1]

    mask2 = daytime & (kt > 0.22) & (kt <= 0.80)
    kt2 = kt[mask2]
    kd[mask2] = 0.9511 - 0.1604 * kt2 + 4.388 * (kt2**2) - 16.638 * (kt2**3) + 12.336 * (kt2**4)

    mask3 = daytime & (kt > 0.80)
    kd[mask3] = 0.165

    # Compute DHI and DNI
    dhi = np.zeros_like(ghi)
    dhi[daytime] = np.clip(ghi[daytime] * kd[daytime], 0.0, ghi[daytime])

    dni = np.zeros_like(ghi)
    dni[daytime] = np.maximum(0.0, (ghi[daytime] - dhi[daytime]) / cos_z[daytime])
    # Cap DNI at extraterrestrial normal radiation
    dni[daytime] = np.minimum(dni[daytime], g_on[daytime])

    return dhi, dni


def transpose_irradiance_isotropic(
    ghi_w_m2: np.ndarray,
    dhi_w_m2: np.ndarray,
    dni_w_m2: np.ndarray,
    cos_zenith: np.ndarray,
    cos_incidence: np.ndarray,
    *,
    tilt_deg: float = DEFAULT_TILT_DEG,
    albedo: float = DEFAULT_ALBEDO,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Transpose horizontal solar components to plane-of-array (POA) irradiance.

    Uses the Liu & Jordan (1963) isotropic sky diffuse model:
    G_poa = G_beam + G_diffuse_sky + G_ground_reflected

    Args:
        ghi_w_m2: Global horizontal irradiance (W/m^2).
        dhi_w_m2: Diffuse horizontal irradiance (W/m^2).
        dni_w_m2: Direct normal irradiance (W/m^2).
        cos_zenith: Cosine of solar zenith angle.
        cos_incidence: Cosine of surface incidence angle.
        tilt_deg: Surface tilt from horizontal (degrees).
        albedo: Ground reflectance factor (default: 0.20 per [D-29]).

    Returns:
        1D numpy array of total POA irradiance (W/m^2).
    """
    ghi = np.asarray(ghi_w_m2, dtype=np.float64)
    dhi = np.asarray(dhi_w_m2, dtype=np.float64)
    dni = np.asarray(dni_w_m2, dtype=np.float64)
    cos_z = np.asarray(cos_zenith, dtype=np.float64)
    cos_inc = np.asarray(cos_incidence, dtype=np.float64)

    beta = np.radians(tilt_deg)
    daytime = (ghi > 0.0) & (cos_z > 1e-3)

    # 1. Beam on tilted plane: DNI * max(0, cos(theta))
    g_beam = np.zeros_like(ghi)
    g_beam[daytime] = dni[daytime] * np.maximum(0.0, cos_inc[daytime])

    # 2. Isotropic sky diffuse: DHI * (1 + cos(beta)) / 2
    g_sky = np.zeros_like(ghi)
    g_sky[daytime] = dhi[daytime] * (1.0 + np.cos(beta)) / 2.0

    # 3. Ground-reflected diffuse: GHI * rho_g * (1 - cos(beta)) / 2
    g_ground = np.zeros_like(ghi)
    g_ground[daytime] = ghi[daytime] * albedo * (1.0 - np.cos(beta)) / 2.0

    g_poa = np.zeros_like(ghi)
    g_poa[daytime] = g_beam[daytime] + g_sky[daytime] + g_ground[daytime]
    g_poa = np.maximum(0.0, g_poa)

    return g_poa


def compute_cell_temperature(
    t_amb_c: np.ndarray,
    g_poa_w_m2: np.ndarray,
    *,
    effective_noct_c: float = EFFECTIVE_NOCT_C,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Compute PV cell operating temperature including rooftop mounting penalty.

    Formula per [D-08] and [D-29]:
    T_cell = T_amb + ((effective_noct - 20.0) / 800.0) * G_poa
    where effective_noct = NOCT_base (45°C) + rooftop_penalty (+10°C) = 55°C.

    Args:
        t_amb_c: Ambient dry-bulb temperature (°C).
        g_poa_w_m2: Plane-of-array irradiance (W/m^2).
        effective_noct_c: Effective NOCT accounting for mounting thermal penalty (°C).

    Returns:
        1D numpy array of cell operating temperature (°C).
    """
    t_amb = np.asarray(t_amb_c, dtype=np.float64)
    g_poa = np.asarray(g_poa_w_m2, dtype=np.float64)

    # (55.0 - 20.0) / 800.0 = 35.0 / 800.0 = 0.04375 °C / (W/m^2)
    thermal_slope = (effective_noct_c - 20.0) / 800.0
    t_cell = t_amb + thermal_slope * np.maximum(0.0, g_poa)
    return t_cell


def compute_thermal_derating_factor(
    t_cell_c: np.ndarray,
    *,
    gamma_per_c: float = TEMP_COEFFICIENT_PER_C,
    t_ref_c: float = STC_CELL_TEMP_C,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Compute module temperature derating factor relative to STC (25°C).

    Formula per [D-08]:
    f_temp = max(0.0, 1.0 + gamma * (T_cell - 25.0))
    where gamma = -0.40%/°C (-0.0040/°C).

    Args:
        t_cell_c: Cell operating temperature (°C).
        gamma_per_c: Temperature coefficient of power (1/°C, negative).
        t_ref_c: Reference cell temperature (°C, default: 25.0).

    Returns:
        1D numpy array of dimensionless thermal derating factors.
    """
    t_cell = np.asarray(t_cell_c, dtype=np.float64)
    f_temp = 1.0 + gamma_per_c * (t_cell - t_ref_c)
    return np.maximum(0.0, f_temp)


def compute_dc_power_per_kwp(
    weather_df: pd.DataFrame,
    *,
    tilt_deg: float = DEFAULT_TILT_DEG,
    azimuth_deg: float = DEFAULT_AZIMUTH_DEG,
    latitude_deg: float = BATAC_LATITUDE_DEG,
    longitude_deg: float = BATAC_LONGITUDE_DEG,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Compute hourly DC power output per kWp for a rooftop PV array at Batac.

    Transforms hourly Global Horizontal Irradiance (GHI) and ambient temperature
    into plane-of-array irradiance, calculates cell operating temperature under
    rooftop thermal mounting penalties, and applies module thermal derating.

    Outputs DC power per kWp BEFORE balance-of-system and inverter losses
    (which are modeled in SOL-002 per docs/context/phase2-interface-contract.md).

    Args:
        weather_df: Verified weather DataFrame containing columns:
            ['hour', 'timestamp', 't_amb', 'rh', 'ghi'].
        tilt_deg: Array tilt angle from horizontal in degrees (default: 15.0°).
        azimuth_deg: Array azimuth angle from North in degrees (default: 180.0° / South).
        latitude_deg: Site latitude in degrees North (default: 18.06°).
        longitude_deg: Site longitude in degrees East (default: 120.56°).

    Returns:
        1D numpy float array of length N (8,760 for annual runs) representing
        hourly DC power generation in kW DC per kWp (equivalent to kWh/kWp at Δt = 1.0h).
    """
    if "ghi" not in weather_df.columns:
        raise ValueError("Weather DataFrame missing required column 'ghi'.")
    if "t_amb" not in weather_df.columns:
        raise ValueError("Weather DataFrame missing required column 't_amb'.")

    n_rows = len(weather_df)
    if n_rows == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    ghi = weather_df["ghi"].values.astype(np.float64)
    t_amb = weather_df["t_amb"].values.astype(np.float64)

    # Validate inputs
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(ghi)):
        raise ValueError("Column 'ghi' contains non-finite values (NaN or inf).")
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(t_amb)):
        raise ValueError("Column 't_amb' contains non-finite values (NaN or inf).")
    if np.any(ghi < 0.0):
        raise ValueError("Column 'ghi' contains negative irradiance values.")

    # Determine timestamps / days of year
    if "timestamp" in weather_df.columns:
        ts = pd.DatetimeIndex(pd.to_datetime(weather_df["timestamp"]))
        doy = ts.dayofyear.values.astype(np.float64)
    elif "hour" in weather_df.columns:
        # Fallback if only 'hour' is provided (1..8760 standard annual sequence)
        hours = weather_df["hour"].values.astype(int)
        # Hour 1 corresponds to 2019-01-01 00:00:00+08:00
        ref_start = pd.Timestamp("2019-01-01 00:00:00+08:00")
        ts = pd.date_range(start=ref_start, periods=len(hours), freq="1h")
        doy = ts.dayofyear.values.astype(np.float64)
    else:
        raise ValueError("Weather DataFrame must contain either 'timestamp' or 'hour' column.")

    # 1. Astronomical solar position
    pos = compute_solar_position(
        ts,
        tilt_deg=tilt_deg,
        azimuth_deg=azimuth_deg,
        latitude_deg=latitude_deg,
        longitude_deg=longitude_deg,
    )

    # 2. GHI -> DHI / DNI decomposition (Erbs correlation)
    dhi, dni = decompose_ghi_erbs(ghi, pos.cos_zenith, doy)

    # 3. Transposition to Plane of Array (Liu-Jordan Isotropic)
    g_poa = transpose_irradiance_isotropic(
        ghi,
        dhi,
        dni,
        pos.cos_zenith,
        pos.cos_incidence,
        tilt_deg=tilt_deg,
        albedo=DEFAULT_ALBEDO,
    )

    # 4. Cell temperature calculation (NOCT + rooftop thermal penalty)
    t_cell = compute_cell_temperature(t_amb, g_poa, effective_noct_c=EFFECTIVE_NOCT_C)

    # 5. Thermal derating factor relative to STC
    f_temp = compute_thermal_derating_factor(t_cell, gamma_per_c=TEMP_COEFFICIENT_PER_C)

    # 6. DC Power output per kWp: (G_poa / 1000 W/m^2) * f_temp
    # Strictly zero whenever GHI is zero or sun is below horizon
    daytime = (ghi > 0.0) & (pos.cos_zenith > 1e-3)
    dc_power_kw_per_kwp = np.zeros(n_rows, dtype=np.float64)
    dc_power_kw_per_kwp[daytime] = (g_poa[daytime] / STC_IRRADIANCE_W_M2) * f_temp[daytime]

    # Clean numerical precision
    dc_power_kw_per_kwp = np.maximum(0.0, dc_power_kw_per_kwp)

    return dc_power_kw_per_kwp
```

### `src/ccis_sim/validation/audit.py`

*196 lines. Verification and physical invariant audit harness for CCIS microgrid simulation (INT-003).*

```python
"""Verification and physical invariant audit harness for CCIS microgrid simulation (INT-003).

Executes comprehensive energy balance, battery boundary, finite value, and
tier demand conservation checks across multi-scenario simulation runs.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any, Sequence

import pandas as pd

from ccis_sim.core.models import HourlyRecord
from ccis_sim.integration.engine import SimulationConfig, SimulationResult, simulate


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AuditResult:
    """Outcome of an invariant audit execution on a simulation configuration."""

    config: SimulationConfig
    passed: bool
    max_hourly_residual: float
    cumulative_residual: float
    min_soc_observed: float
    max_soc_observed: float
    soc_within_bounds: bool
    non_finite_count: int
    tier_conservation_passed: bool
    details: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def summary(self) -> str:
        """Format a single-line summary of audit verification."""
        status = "PASSED" if self.passed else "FAILED"
        return (
            f"[{status}] solar={self.config.solar_kwp:.0f}kWp, bat={self.config.battery_kwh:.0f}kWh, "
            f"year={self.config.weather_year}, regime={self.config.outage_regime} | "
            f"max_res={self.max_hourly_residual:.2e} kWh, cum_res={self.cumulative_residual:.2e} kWh, "
            f"SoC=[{self.min_soc_observed:.1f}, {self.max_soc_observed:.1f}], "
            f"non_finite={self.non_finite_count}, tier_cons={self.tier_conservation_passed}"
        )


def audit_run(
    config: SimulationConfig,
    weather_df: pd.DataFrame | None = None,
    seed: int | None = None,
    tolerance: float = 1e-5,
) -> AuditResult:
    """Execute simulation with verification enabled and audit physical conservation invariants.

    Parameters
    ----------
    config : SimulationConfig
        Target simulation sizing and operational configuration.
    weather_df : pd.DataFrame, optional
        Pre-loaded weather DataFrame to reuse across runs.
    seed : int, optional
        Random seed override (defaults to config.seed).
    tolerance : float, optional
        Maximum allowable energy balance residual per hour in kWh (default: 1e-5).

    Returns
    -------
    AuditResult
        Structured verification record containing residual metrics, SoC bounds,
        finite counts, and tier-conservation status.
    """
    res: SimulationResult = simulate(
        config=config,
        weather_df=weather_df,
        seed=seed,
        verify=True,
    )

    records: Sequence[HourlyRecord] = res.records
    numeric_fields = (
        "demand_gross_kwh",
        "demand_served_kwh",
        "solar_generation_kwh",
        "solar_to_demand_kwh",
        "solar_to_battery_kwh",
        "solar_curtailed_kwh",
        "battery_soc_kwh",
        "battery_charge_kwh",
        "battery_discharge_kwh",
        "grid_import_kwh",
        "unserved_load_kwh",
    )

    non_finite_count = 0
    tier_conservation_passed = True
    tier_mismatch_max = 0.0

    soc_values = [r.battery_soc_kwh for r in records]
    min_soc_observed = min(soc_values) if soc_values else 0.0
    max_soc_observed = max(soc_values) if soc_values else 0.0

    for r in records:
        # Check finite values
        for f in numeric_fields:
            val = getattr(r, f, 0.0)
            if not math.isfinite(val):
                non_finite_count += 1

        # Check hourly tier demand conservation: served + unserved == gross demand
        tier_sum = r.demand_served_kwh + r.unserved_load_kwh
        diff = abs(tier_sum - r.demand_gross_kwh)
        if diff > tier_mismatch_max:
            tier_mismatch_max = diff
        if diff > 1e-7:
            tier_conservation_passed = False

    max_hourly_residual = max(r.energy_balance_residual_kwh for r in records)
    cumulative_residual = sum(r.signed_residual_kwh for r in records)

    # Validate SoC bounds against physical battery specifications
    if config.battery_kwh > 0.0:
        min_allowed_soc = 0.10 * config.battery_kwh  # 10% min_soc protection floor
        max_allowed_soc = config.battery_kwh
        soc_within_bounds = (
            (min_soc_observed >= min_allowed_soc - 1e-7)
            and (max_soc_observed <= max_allowed_soc + 1e-7)
        )
    else:
        soc_within_bounds = (abs(min_soc_observed) < 1e-7) and (abs(max_soc_observed) < 1e-7)

    # Verification threshold pass/fail determination
    # Note: Cumulative tolerance scales as O(sqrt(N) * epsilon) per [D-21]
    cum_tolerance = max(tolerance, math.sqrt(len(records)) * tolerance)
    passed = (
        (max_hourly_residual <= tolerance)
        and (abs(cumulative_residual) <= cum_tolerance)
        and soc_within_bounds
        and (non_finite_count == 0)
        and tier_conservation_passed
    )

    details = {
        "record_count": len(records),
        "cum_tolerance_kwh": cum_tolerance,
        "max_hourly_tolerance_kwh": tolerance,
        "tier_mismatch_max_kwh": tier_mismatch_max,
        "tracker_summary": res.tracker_summary,
    }

    return AuditResult(
        config=config,
        passed=passed,
        max_hourly_residual=max_hourly_residual,
        cumulative_residual=cumulative_residual,
        min_soc_observed=min_soc_observed,
        max_soc_observed=max_soc_observed,
        soc_within_bounds=soc_within_bounds,
        non_finite_count=non_finite_count,
        tier_conservation_passed=tier_conservation_passed,
        details=details,
    )


def run_standard_audit_matrix() -> list[AuditResult]:
    """Execute audit across the standard verification matrix specified in INT-003.

    Covers 8 primary configurations across sizing and weather/outage regimes:
    4 sizings:
      - grid-only (0 kWp PV, 0 kWh battery)
      - PV-only 100 (100 kWp PV, 0 kWh battery)
      - PV+battery 100/100 (100 kWp PV, 100 kWh battery)
      - PV+battery 200/200 (200 kWp PV, 200 kWh battery)
    across {2019 normal, 2019 typhoon}, plus 2016 normal benchmark configurations.
    """
    configs = [
        # 1-4: 2019 Normal
        SimulationConfig(solar_kwp=0.0, battery_kwh=0.0, weather_year=2019, outage_regime="normal", seed=0),
        SimulationConfig(solar_kwp=100.0, battery_kwh=0.0, weather_year=2019, outage_regime="normal", seed=0),
        SimulationConfig(solar_kwp=100.0, battery_kwh=100.0, weather_year=2019, outage_regime="normal", seed=0),
        SimulationConfig(solar_kwp=200.0, battery_kwh=200.0, weather_year=2019, outage_regime="normal", seed=0),
        # 5-8: 2019 Typhoon
        SimulationConfig(solar_kwp=0.0, battery_kwh=0.0, weather_year=2019, outage_regime="typhoon", seed=0),
        SimulationConfig(solar_kwp=100.0, battery_kwh=0.0, weather_year=2019, outage_regime="typhoon", seed=0),
        SimulationConfig(solar_kwp=100.0, battery_kwh=100.0, weather_year=2019, outage_regime="typhoon", seed=0),
        SimulationConfig(solar_kwp=200.0, battery_kwh=200.0, weather_year=2019, outage_regime="typhoon", seed=0),
        # 9-12: 2016 Normal (El Niño hot year)
        SimulationConfig(solar_kwp=0.0, battery_kwh=0.0, weather_year=2016, outage_regime="normal", seed=0),
        SimulationConfig(solar_kwp=100.0, battery_kwh=0.0, weather_year=2016, outage_regime="normal", seed=0),
        SimulationConfig(solar_kwp=100.0, battery_kwh=100.0, weather_year=2016, outage_regime="normal", seed=0),
        SimulationConfig(solar_kwp=200.0, battery_kwh=200.0, weather_year=2016, outage_regime="normal", seed=0),
    ]

    results = []
    for cfg in configs:
        res = audit_run(cfg)
        results.append(res)
    return results
```

### `src/ccis_sim/validation/hypothesis.py`

*343 lines. Paired and unpaired hypothesis tests over the INT-002 scenario grid (DOC-FIX-001 Part B).*

```python
"""Paired and unpaired hypothesis tests over the INT-002 scenario grid (DOC-FIX-001 Part B).

Seeds are common random numbers across sizings, so comparisons that share
(weather_year, outage_regime, demand_scale, temp_offset_c, seed) are paired by seed.

All p-values describe seed-to-seed simulation variability. They are not real-world
inference, and N is small (15 or 30 paired seeds depending on the tranche).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Iterable, Mapping, Sequence

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from scipy import stats

SCENARIO_GRID_PATH = Path("results/scenario_grid.csv")

PAIRING_KEYS = ["weather_year", "outage_regime", "demand_scale", "temp_offset_c", "seed"]


def add_combined_unserved(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Return a copy with ``unserved_t1_2_kwh`` = Tier 1 + Tier 2 unserved energy."""
    out = df.copy()
    out["unserved_t1_2_kwh"] = out["unserved_t1_kwh"] + out["unserved_t2_kwh"]
    return out


def load_scenario_grid(path: str | Path = SCENARIO_GRID_PATH) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Load the scenario grid CSV and add the combined Tier 1+2 unserved column."""
    return add_combined_unserved(pd.read_csv(path))


def _filter(
    df: pd.DataFrame,
    *,
    weather_year: int,
    outage_regime: str,
    demand_scale: float,
    temp_offset_c: float = 0.0,
    solar_kwp: float,
    battery_kwh: float,
    seed_max: int | None = None,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    if "unserved_t1_2_kwh" not in df.columns:
        df = add_combined_unserved(df)
    mask = (
        (df["weather_year"] == weather_year)
        & (df["outage_regime"] == outage_regime)
        & np.isclose(df["demand_scale"], demand_scale)
        & np.isclose(df["temp_offset_c"], temp_offset_c)
        & np.isclose(df["solar_kwp"], solar_kwp)
        & np.isclose(df["battery_kwh"], battery_kwh)
    )
    if seed_max is not None:
        mask &= df["seed"] <= seed_max
    return df.loc[mask, PAIRING_KEYS + ["unserved_t1_2_kwh", "grid_import_kwh"]].copy()


def holm_adjust(pvalues: Sequence[float]) -> np.ndarray:
    """Holm-Bonferroni step-down adjusted p-values, preserving input order."""
    p = np.asarray(pvalues, dtype=float)
    m = p.size
    order = np.argsort(p)
    adjusted = np.empty(m, dtype=float)
    running = 0.0
    for rank, idx in enumerate(order):
        running = max(running, (m - rank) * p[idx])
        adjusted[idx] = min(1.0, running)
    return adjusted


def paired_test(
    left: np.ndarray,
    right: np.ndarray,
    *,
    label: str = "",
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Wilcoxon signed-rank (primary) and paired t-test (secondary) on differences.

    The 95% CI is the paired t interval for the mean difference. Cohen's dz is
    ``mean(diff) / sd(diff)``.
    """
    a = np.asarray(left, dtype=float)
    b = np.asarray(right, dtype=float)
    if a.shape != b.shape:
        raise ValueError("paired samples must have equal length")
    diff = b - a
    n = int(diff.size)
    if n == 0:
        raise ValueError("paired samples must not be empty")

    mean_diff = float(np.mean(diff))
    median_diff = float(np.median(diff))
    sd_diff = float(np.std(diff, ddof=1)) if n > 1 else 0.0
    se = sd_diff / np.sqrt(n) if n > 0 else 0.0
    t_crit = float(stats.t.ppf(0.975, df=n - 1)) if n > 1 else float("nan")
    ci_half = t_crit * se
    dz = mean_diff / sd_diff if sd_diff > 0.0 else float("nan")

    if n < 2:
        wilcoxon_p = float("nan")
        ttest_p = float("nan")
    elif np.allclose(diff, 0.0):
        wilcoxon_p = 1.0
        ttest_p = 1.0
    else:
        try:
            wilcoxon_p = float(stats.wilcoxon(diff, zero_method="wilcox").pvalue)
        except ValueError:
            wilcoxon_p = float("nan")
        ttest_p = float(stats.ttest_rel(b, a).pvalue)

    return {
        "comparison": label,
        "n": n,
        "mean_diff": mean_diff,
        "median_diff": median_diff,
        "sd_diff": sd_diff,
        "ci95_half": float(ci_half),
        "cohens_dz": float(dz),
        "wilcoxon_p": wilcoxon_p,
        "paired_t_p": ttest_p,
    }


def unpaired_test(left: np.ndarray, right: np.ndarray, *, label: str = "") -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Mann-Whitney U (primary) and Welch t-test (secondary) on independent samples."""
    a = np.asarray(left, dtype=float)
    b = np.asarray(right, dtype=float)
    if a.size == 0 or b.size == 0:
        raise ValueError("unpaired samples must not be empty")

    mw = stats.mannwhitneyu(b, a, alternative="two-sided")
    welch = stats.ttest_ind(b, a, equal_var=False)
    mean_diff = float(np.mean(b) - np.mean(a))
    return {
        "comparison": label,
        "n_left": int(a.size),
        "n_right": int(b.size),
        "mean_left": float(np.mean(a)),
        "mean_right": float(np.mean(b)),
        "mean_diff": mean_diff,
        "mannwhitney_p": float(mw.pvalue),
        "welch_t_p": float(welch.pvalue),
    }


def _merge_paired(
    left_df: pd.DataFrame,
    right_df: pd.DataFrame,
    column: str,
    *,
    on: Sequence[str] | None = None,
) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    keys = list(on) if on is not None else PAIRING_KEYS
    merged = left_df.merge(right_df, on=keys, suffixes=("_left", "_right"))
    if merged.empty:
        raise ValueError("no paired seeds matched between the two selections")
    return merged[f"{column}_left"].to_numpy(), merged[f"{column}_right"].to_numpy()


def sizing_pair_tests(
    df: pd.DataFrame,
    *,
    demand_scale: float = 1.0,
    weather_year: int = 2019,
    outage_regime: str = "typhoon",
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Paired unserved T1+2 tests for the five sizing comparisons requested by the APC."""
    pairs = [
        ((100.0, 50.0), (100.0, 100.0)),
        ((100.0, 100.0), (200.0, 100.0)),
        ((200.0, 100.0), (200.0, 200.0)),
        ((0.0, 0.0), (100.0, 100.0)),
    ]
    rows = []
    for (sol_l, bat_l), (sol_r, bat_r) in pairs:
        left_df = _filter(
            df,
            weather_year=weather_year,
            outage_regime=outage_regime,
            demand_scale=demand_scale,
            solar_kwp=sol_l,
            battery_kwh=bat_l,
        )
        right_df = _filter(
            df,
            weather_year=weather_year,
            outage_regime=outage_regime,
            demand_scale=demand_scale,
            solar_kwp=sol_r,
            battery_kwh=bat_r,
        )
        left, right = _merge_paired(left_df, right_df, "unserved_t1_2_kwh")
        label = f"{sol_l:.0f}/{bat_l:.0f} -> {sol_r:.0f}/{bat_r:.0f}"
        rows.append(paired_test(left, right, label=label))

    result = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    result["holm_adjusted_p"] = holm_adjust(result["wilcoxon_p"].to_numpy())
    return result


def grid_import_tests(
    df: pd.DataFrame,
    *,
    demand_scale: float = 1.0,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Paired grid-import tests: 2016 vs 2019, and +2 °C vs base."""
    rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    for sol, bat in ((0.0, 0.0), (100.0, 100.0)):
        d2019 = _filter(
            df,
            weather_year=2019,
            outage_regime="normal",
            demand_scale=demand_scale,
            solar_kwp=sol,
            battery_kwh=bat,
        )
        d2016 = _filter(
            df,
            weather_year=2016,
            outage_regime="normal",
            demand_scale=demand_scale,
            solar_kwp=sol,
            battery_kwh=bat,
        )
        left, right = _merge_paired(
            d2016, d2019, "grid_import_kwh",
            on=["outage_regime", "demand_scale", "temp_offset_c", "seed"],
        )
        rows.append(paired_test(left, right, label=f"2019 minus 2016, normal, {sol:.0f}/{bat:.0f}"))

    base = _filter(
        df,
        weather_year=2019,
        outage_regime="normal",
        demand_scale=demand_scale,
        temp_offset_c=0.0,
        solar_kwp=0.0,
        battery_kwh=0.0,
        seed_max=14,
    )
    hot = _filter(
        df,
        weather_year=2019,
        outage_regime="normal",
        demand_scale=demand_scale,
        temp_offset_c=2.0,
        solar_kwp=0.0,
        battery_kwh=0.0,
    )
    left, right = _merge_paired(
        base, hot, "grid_import_kwh",
        on=["outage_regime", "demand_scale", "seed"],
    )
    rows.append(paired_test(left, right, label="+2 C minus base, 2019 normal, grid-only, 15 seeds"))

    result = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    result["holm_adjusted_p"] = holm_adjust(result["wilcoxon_p"].to_numpy())
    return result


def regime_test(
    df: pd.DataFrame,
    *,
    demand_scale: float = 1.0,
    weather_year: int = 2019,
    solar_kwp: float = 100.0,
    battery_kwh: float = 100.0,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Unpaired typhoon vs normal unserved T1+2 comparison for one sizing."""
    normal = _filter(
        df,
        weather_year=weather_year,
        outage_regime="normal",
        demand_scale=demand_scale,
        solar_kwp=solar_kwp,
        battery_kwh=battery_kwh,
    )
    typhoon = _filter(
        df,
        weather_year=weather_year,
        outage_regime="typhoon",
        demand_scale=demand_scale,
        solar_kwp=solar_kwp,
        battery_kwh=battery_kwh,
    )
    row = unpaired_test(
        normal["unserved_t1_2_kwh"].to_numpy(),
        typhoon["unserved_t1_2_kwh"].to_numpy(),
        label=f"typhoon vs normal, 2019, {solar_kwp:.0f}/{battery_kwh:.0f}",
    )
    return pd.DataFrame([row])


def run_hypothesis_suite(df: pd.DataFrame | None = None) -> dict[str, pd.DataFrame]:
    """Run all Part B test families and return them keyed by family name."""
    data = load_scenario_grid() if df is None else add_combined_unserved(df)
    return {
        "sizing_pairs": sizing_pair_tests(data),
        "grid_import": grid_import_tests(data),
        "regime": regime_test(data),
    }


def format_markdown(tables: Mapping[str, pd.DataFrame]) -> str:
    """Render the test tables as Markdown with the required interpretation caveat."""
    lines = [
        "# Hypothesis Tests (DOC-FIX-001 Part B)",
        "",
        "> **Interpretation:** p-values describe seed-to-seed simulation variability under common",
        "> random numbers, not real-world inference. N is small (15 or 30 paired seeds).",
        "> No claim of precision beyond these data is made.",
        ">",
        "> `mean_diff` is **right minus left** in each `A -> B` comparison. A negative value means",
        "> the right-hand configuration/case has lower unserved energy or grid import.",
        "",
    ]
    titles = {
        "sizing_pairs": "1. Paired sizing comparisons — unserved Tier 1+2, 2019 typhoon, bottom-up",
        "grid_import": "2. Paired grid import — weather year and +2 °C",
        "regime": "3. Unpaired typhoon vs normal — unserved Tier 1+2, 2019, 100/100",
    }
    for key, table in tables.items():
        lines.append(f"## {titles.get(key, key)}")
        lines.append("")
        lines.append(table.to_markdown(index=False))
        lines.append("")
    return "\n".join(lines)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tables = run_hypothesis_suite()
    output = Path("results/hypothesis_tests.md")
    output.write_text(format_markdown(tables), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"Wrote {output}")
    for name, table in tables.items():
        print(f"\n[{name}]")
        print(table.to_string(index=False))
```

### `src/ccis_sim/validation/plausibility.py`

*269 lines. Plausibility evaluation and benchmark reconciliation module (INT-003).*

```python
"""Plausibility evaluation and benchmark reconciliation module (INT-003).

Provides calculation and comparison routines for ASHRAE Guideline 14 metrics
(NMBE, CV(RMSE)), diurnal/seasonal demand profiles, and solar yield reconciliation.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from collections import defaultdict
from collections.abc import Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

import pandas as pd

from ccis_sim.core.models import HourlyRecord
from ccis_sim.demand.schedule import AcademicCalendar


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ASHRAEMetrics:
    """ASHRAE Guideline 14 statistical validation metrics for monthly calibration."""

    n_periods: int
    sim_total_kwh: float
    measured_total_kwh: float
    measured_mean_kwh: float
    nmbe_pct: float
    nmbe_raw_pct: float
    cv_rmse_pct: float
    ashrae_monthly_pass: bool
    notes: str = ""

    def summary(self) -> str:
        """Format human-readable summary of statistical fit."""
        pass_str = "PASS" if self.ashrae_monthly_pass else "FAIL"
        return (
            f"ASHRAE Guideline 14 Monthly Fit: [{pass_str}] | "
            f"NMBE={self.nmbe_pct:.2f}% (thresh ±5.0%), "
            f"CV(RMSE)={self.cv_rmse_pct:.2f}% (thresh 15.0%) | "
            f"Sim={self.sim_total_kwh:,.1f} kWh, Measured={self.measured_total_kwh:,.1f} kWh"
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PlausibilitySummary:
    """Consolidated headline demand and profile plausibility metrics."""

    annual_gross_kwh: float
    annual_served_kwh: float
    gross_eui_kwh_per_m2: float
    served_eui_kwh_per_m2: float
    peak_demand_kw: float
    peak_timestamp: str
    peak_hour: int
    load_factor: float
    monthly_profile_kwh: list[float]
    weekday_mean_kwh_per_day: float
    weekend_mean_kwh_per_day: float
    vacation_mean_kwh_per_day: float
    n_weekdays: int
    n_weekends: int
    n_vacation_days: int


def compute_ashrae_metrics(
    sim_monthly: Sequence[float],
    measured_monthly: Sequence[float],
) -> ASHRAEMetrics:
    """Calculate ASHRAE Guideline 14 NMBE and CV(RMSE) statistical metrics.

    Formulas per ASHRAE Guideline 14-2014 for monthly utility billing comparisons:
        NMBE = [sum(y_sim - y_meas) / ((n - 1) * y_meas_mean)] * 100%
        CV(RMSE) = [sqrt(sum((y_sim - y_meas)^2) / (n - 1)) / y_meas_mean] * 100%

    Parameters
    ----------
    sim_monthly : Sequence[float]
        Sequence of simulated monthly energy totals in kWh (length N, typically 12).
    measured_monthly : Sequence[float]
        Sequence of measured (or synthetic mock utility bill) monthly energy totals.

    Returns
    -------
    ASHRAEMetrics
        Calculated NMBE, CV(RMSE), and compliance indicator against ASHRAE Guideline 14
        monthly thresholds (|NMBE| <= 5%, CV(RMSE) <= 15%).
    """
    n = len(measured_monthly)
    if n == 0 or len(sim_monthly) != n:
        raise ValueError(f"Inputs must have equal positive lengths, got {len(sim_monthly)} and {n}")

    meas_total = float(sum(measured_monthly))
    sim_total = float(sum(sim_monthly))
    meas_mean = meas_total / n

    if meas_mean <= 0.0:
        raise ValueError(f"Measured mean must be positive, got {meas_mean}")

    diffs = [float(s) - float(m) for s, m in zip(sim_monthly, measured_monthly)]

    # Degrees of freedom: n - 1 (or n - p where p = 1 regression parameter)
    dof = max(1, n - 1)

    nmbe_pct = (sum(diffs) / (dof * meas_mean)) * 100.0
    nmbe_raw_pct = (sum(diffs) / (n * meas_mean)) * 100.0
    sum_sq_err = sum(d**2 for d in diffs)
    cv_rmse_pct = (math.sqrt(sum_sq_err / dof) / meas_mean) * 100.0

    # ASHRAE Guideline 14 monthly thresholds: |NMBE| <= 5%, CV(RMSE) <= 15%
    passes_threshold = (abs(nmbe_pct) <= 5.0) and (cv_rmse_pct <= 15.0)

    notes = (
        "Evaluation against ASHRAE Guideline 14 monthly thresholds (±5% NMBE, 15% CV(RMSE)). "
        "Caution: synthetic utility bills and bottom-up models are derived from the same audit "
        "and do not constitute independent empirical validation."
    )

    return ASHRAEMetrics(
        n_periods=n,
        sim_total_kwh=sim_total,
        measured_total_kwh=meas_total,
        measured_mean_kwh=meas_mean,
        nmbe_pct=nmbe_pct,
        nmbe_raw_pct=nmbe_raw_pct,
        cv_rmse_pct=cv_rmse_pct,
        ashrae_monthly_pass=passes_threshold,
        notes=notes,
    )


def compute_plausibility_summary(
    records: Sequence[HourlyRecord],
    building_area_m2: float = 2500.0,
    calendar: AcademicCalendar | None = None,
) -> PlausibilitySummary:
    """Compute annual headline KPIs, 12-month profile, and day-type mean consumptions.

    Parameters
    ----------
    records : Sequence[HourlyRecord]
        Complete annual sequence of 8,760 HourlyRecord objects.
    building_area_m2 : float, optional
        Facility gross floor area (default: 2,500 m²).
    calendar : AcademicCalendar, optional
        Academic schedule definition for day-type categorization.

    Returns
    -------
    PlausibilitySummary
        Structured object containing headline KPIs and day-type means.
    """
    if not records:
        raise ValueError("Cannot compute plausibility summary on empty records.")

    cal = calendar or AcademicCalendar()

    annual_gross_kwh = sum(r.demand_gross_kwh for r in records)
    annual_served_kwh = sum(r.demand_served_kwh for r in records)

    gross_eui = annual_gross_kwh / building_area_m2
    served_eui = annual_served_kwh / building_area_m2

    peak_rec = max(records, key=lambda r: r.demand_gross_kwh)
    peak_demand_kw = peak_rec.demand_gross_kwh
    peak_timestamp = peak_rec.timestamp
    peak_hour = peak_rec.hour

    load_factor = (
        annual_gross_kwh / (peak_demand_kw * len(records)) if peak_demand_kw > 0.0 else 0.0
    )

    # Monthly aggregation
    monthly_sums: dict[int, float] = defaultdict(float)
    daily_sums: dict[str, float] = defaultdict(float)

    for r in records:
        # ISO timestamp format: "YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SS+08:00"
        month_idx = int(r.timestamp[5:7])
        date_str = r.timestamp[:10]
        monthly_sums[month_idx] += r.demand_gross_kwh
        daily_sums[date_str] += r.demand_gross_kwh

    monthly_profile_kwh = [monthly_sums[m] for m in range(1, 13)]

    # Day-type grouping: weekday vs weekend vs vacation
    weekday_days: list[float] = []
    weekend_days: list[float] = []
    vacation_days: list[float] = []

    for date_str, daily_kwh in sorted(daily_sums.items()):
        dt = pd.to_datetime(date_str)
        d = dt.date()
        is_break_or_hol = cal.is_break(d) or cal.is_holiday(d)
        is_weekend = dt.weekday() >= 5

        if is_break_or_hol:
            vacation_days.append(daily_kwh)
        elif is_weekend:
            weekend_days.append(daily_kwh)
        else:
            weekday_days.append(daily_kwh)

    weekday_mean = sum(weekday_days) / len(weekday_days) if weekday_days else 0.0
    weekend_mean = sum(weekend_days) / len(weekend_days) if weekend_days else 0.0
    vacation_mean = sum(vacation_days) / len(vacation_days) if vacation_days else 0.0

    return PlausibilitySummary(
        annual_gross_kwh=annual_gross_kwh,
        annual_served_kwh=annual_served_kwh,
        gross_eui_kwh_per_m2=gross_eui,
        served_eui_kwh_per_m2=served_eui,
        peak_demand_kw=peak_demand_kw,
        peak_timestamp=peak_timestamp,
        peak_hour=peak_hour,
        load_factor=load_factor,
        monthly_profile_kwh=monthly_profile_kwh,
        weekday_mean_kwh_per_day=weekday_mean,
        weekend_mean_kwh_per_day=weekend_mean,
        vacation_mean_kwh_per_day=vacation_mean,
        n_weekdays=len(weekday_days),
        n_weekends=len(weekend_days),
        n_vacation_days=len(vacation_days),
    )


def reconcile_solar_yield(
    transposed_poa_kwh_m2: float = 1945.98,
    baseline_pr: float = 0.77,
    target_specific_yield: float = 1298.0,
    simulated_specific_yield: float = 1508.74,
) -> dict[str, float]:
    """Reconcile simulated rooftop solar specific yield against regional benchmarks.

    Parameters
    ----------
    transposed_poa_kwh_m2 : float, optional
        Annual Plane-of-Array insolation in Batac (default: 1,945.98 kWh/m²).
    baseline_pr : float, optional
        Nominal system performance ratio per [D-08] (default: 0.77).
    target_specific_yield : float, optional
        Multi-system Luzon benchmark target from literature (default: 1,298 kWh/kWp).
    simulated_specific_yield : float, optional
        Simulated end-to-end AC specific yield in Batac (default: 1,508.74 kWh/kWp).

    Returns
    -------
    dict[str, float]
        Reconciliation breakdown with theoretical product, percentage deltas,
        and insolation comparison.
    """
    theoretical_poa_pr = transposed_poa_kwh_m2 * baseline_pr
    delta_vs_target_pct = (
        (simulated_specific_yield - target_specific_yield) / target_specific_yield
    ) * 100.0
    delta_vs_poa_pr_pct = (
        (simulated_specific_yield - theoretical_poa_pr) / theoretical_poa_pr
    ) * 100.0

    return {
        "transposed_poa_kwh_m2": transposed_poa_kwh_m2,
        "baseline_pr": baseline_pr,
        "theoretical_poa_pr_kwh_per_kwp": theoretical_poa_pr,
        "target_specific_yield_kwh_per_kwp": target_specific_yield,
        "simulated_specific_yield_kwh_per_kwp": simulated_specific_yield,
        "delta_vs_target_pct": delta_vs_target_pct,
        "delta_vs_poa_pr_pct": delta_vs_poa_pr_pct,
    }
```

### `src/ccis_sim/validation/sensitivity.py`

*222 lines. One-at-a-time (OAT) parameter sensitivity analysis harness (INT-003).*

```python
"""One-at-a-time (OAT) parameter sensitivity analysis harness (INT-003).

Systematically evaluates microgrid sensitivity to key dispatch, thermal,
and physical storage parameters relative to the 100 kWp / 100 kWh baseline.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any, Sequence

from ccis_sim.core.models import Battery, Building
import ccis_sim.integration.engine as engine_module
from ccis_sim.integration.engine import SimulationConfig, simulate
from ccis_sim.solar import array as solar_array_module


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SensitivityPoint:
    """Individual sensitivity evaluation result for a perturbed parameter value."""

    parameter: str
    label: str
    value: Any
    grid_import_kwh: float
    unserved_tier1_2_kwh: float
    delta_grid_import_kwh: float
    delta_unserved_tier1_2_kwh: float
    is_exposed: bool
    notes: str = ""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SensitivitySweepResult:
    """Complete collection of OAT sensitivity points evaluated against a common baseline."""

    base_grid_import_kwh: float
    base_unserved_tier1_2_kwh: float
    points: list[SensitivityPoint] = field(default_factory=list)

    def to_dataframe_dict(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        """Export sensitivity results as list of dictionary records for tabular rendering."""
        rows = []
        for p in self.points:
            rows.append(
                {
                    "Parameter": p.parameter,
                    "Variation": p.label,
                    "Value": str(p.value),
                    "Grid Import (kWh)": round(p.grid_import_kwh, 2),
                    "Δ Grid (kWh)": round(p.delta_grid_import_kwh, 2),
                    "Unserved T1+2 (kWh)": round(p.unserved_tier1_2_kwh, 2),
                    "Δ Unserved T1+2 (kWh)": round(p.delta_unserved_tier1_2_kwh, 2),
                    "Exposed Parameter": "Yes" if p.is_exposed else "Monkeypatched",
                    "Notes": p.notes,
                }
            )
        return rows


def run_oat_sensitivity_analysis(
    weather_year: int = 2019,
    outage_regime: str = "normal",
    seed: int = 0,
) -> SensitivitySweepResult:
    """Run full One-at-a-Time (OAT) sensitivity matrix on 100 kWp / 100 kWh CCIS system.

    Parameters tested:
    1. Emergency Outage Reserve (0.10, 0.20, 0.50): exposed on SimulationConfig.
    2. Tier-2 Shed Threshold (0.10, 0.20, 0.30): exposed on SimulationConfig.
    3. Cooling Temperature Sensitivity Slope (3, 4, 5 %/°C): exposed on Building.
    4. PR-Driving Balance-of-System (BOS) Losses (±5%): monkeypatched in test harness.
    5. Battery Round-Trip Efficiency (0.88, 0.92, 0.96): monkeypatched in test harness.

    Returns
    -------
    SensitivitySweepResult
        Collection of all evaluation points with absolute and differential metrics.
    """
    base_cfg = SimulationConfig(
        solar_kwp=100.0,
        battery_kwh=100.0,
        weather_year=weather_year,
        outage_regime=outage_regime,
        seed=seed,
    )

    # 0. Baseline evaluation
    base_res = simulate(base_cfg)
    base_grid = base_res.metrics["annual_grid_import_kwh"]
    base_unserved_t1_2 = (
        base_res.tracker_summary["unserved_tier1_kwh"]
        + base_res.tracker_summary["unserved_tier2_kwh"]
    )

    points: list[SensitivityPoint] = []

    def add_point(
        param: str,
        label: str,
        val: Any,
        res: Any,
        is_exposed: bool,
        notes: str = "",
    ) -> None:
        grid = res.metrics["annual_grid_import_kwh"]
        unserved = (
            res.tracker_summary["unserved_tier1_kwh"]
            + res.tracker_summary["unserved_tier2_kwh"]
        )
        points.append(
            SensitivityPoint(
                parameter=param,
                label=label,
                value=val,
                grid_import_kwh=grid,
                unserved_tier1_2_kwh=unserved,
                delta_grid_import_kwh=grid - base_grid,
                delta_unserved_tier1_2_kwh=unserved - base_unserved_t1_2,
                is_exposed=is_exposed,
                notes=notes,
            )
        )

    # 1. Emergency Reserve (0.10, 0.20, 0.50)
    for er in (0.10, 0.20, 0.50):
        cfg = SimulationConfig(
            solar_kwp=100.0,
            battery_kwh=100.0,
            weather_year=weather_year,
            outage_regime=outage_regime,
            seed=seed,
            emergency_reserve_pct=er,
        )
        res = simulate(cfg)
        add_point(
            param="Emergency Reserve Fraction",
            label=f"Reserve = {er:.0%}",
            val=er,
            res=res,
            is_exposed=True,
            notes="Configurable on SimulationConfig.emergency_reserve_pct",
        )

    # 2. Tier-2 Shed Threshold (0.10, 0.20, 0.30)
    for t2 in (0.10, 0.20, 0.30):
        cfg = SimulationConfig(
            solar_kwp=100.0,
            battery_kwh=100.0,
            weather_year=weather_year,
            outage_regime=outage_regime,
            seed=seed,
            tier2_shed_soc_fraction=t2,
        )
        res = simulate(cfg)
        add_point(
            param="Tier-2 Shedding Threshold",
            label=f"Shed SoC = {t2:.0%}",
            val=t2,
            res=res,
            is_exposed=True,
            notes="Configurable on SimulationConfig.tier2_shed_soc_fraction",
        )

    # 3. Cooling Slope (3, 4, 5 %/°C)
    for cs in (0.03, 0.04, 0.05):
        bldg = Building(cooling_sensitivity_per_c=cs)
        res = simulate(base_cfg, building=bldg)
        add_point(
            param="Cooling Slope (%/°C)",
            label=f"Slope = {cs*100:.1f}%/°C",
            val=cs,
            res=res,
            is_exposed=True,
            notes="Exposed on Building archetype constructor",
        )

    # 4. PR-Driving BOS Factor (±5%)
    orig_compute_ac = engine_module.compute_ac_generation
    try:
        for mult, name in ((0.95, "-5% BOS (lower PR)"), (1.00, "Baseline BOS (PR 0.77)"), (1.05, "+5% BOS (higher PR)")):
            def _make_patched_compute(factor: float):
                def _patched(dc_kw_per_kwp, array, **kwargs):
                    kwargs["soiling"] = solar_array_module.DEFAULT_SOILING_FACTOR * factor
                    return orig_compute_ac(dc_kw_per_kwp, array, **kwargs)
                return _patched

            engine_module.compute_ac_generation = _make_patched_compute(mult)
            res = simulate(base_cfg)
            add_point(
                param="PR-Driving BOS Factor",
                label=name,
                val=f"{mult*100:.0f}% of base",
                res=res,
                is_exposed=False,
                notes="Monkeypatched in engine.compute_ac_generation (soiling factor)",
            )
    finally:
        engine_module.compute_ac_generation = orig_compute_ac

    # 5. Battery RTE (0.88, 0.92, 0.96)
    orig_defaults = Battery.__init__.__defaults__
    try:
        for rte in (0.88, 0.92, 0.96):
            Battery.__init__.__defaults__ = (rte, *orig_defaults[1:])
            res = simulate(base_cfg)
            add_point(
                param="Battery Round-Trip Efficiency (RTE)",
                label=f"RTE = {rte:.2f}",
                val=rte,
                res=res,
                is_exposed=False,
                notes="Monkeypatched in Battery.__init__.__defaults__ (not on SimulationConfig)",
            )
    finally:
        Battery.__init__.__defaults__ = orig_defaults

    return SensitivitySweepResult(
        base_grid_import_kwh=base_grid,
        base_unserved_tier1_2_kwh=base_unserved_t1_2,
        points=points,
    )
```
