### `src/ccis_sim/weather/client.py`

*197 lines. NASA POWER REST API client for fetching meteorological time series.*

```python
"""NASA POWER REST API client for fetching meteorological time series.

Target coordinates: Batac, Ilocos Norte (18.06°N, 120.56°E).
Provides methods for querying NASA POWER hourly (2001–present) and daily (1981–present)
point APIs, caching raw JSON responses, and offline fallback loading.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
import urllib.parse
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Sequence

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

BATAC_LATITUDE: float = 18.06
BATAC_LONGITUDE: float = 120.56

NASA_POWER_BASE_URL: str = "https://power.larc.nasa.gov/api/temporal"
DEFAULT_DAILY_ENDPOINT: str = f"{NASA_POWER_BASE_URL}/daily/point"
DEFAULT_HOURLY_ENDPOINT: str = f"{NASA_POWER_BASE_URL}/hourly/point"

DAILY_PARAMETERS: tuple[str, ...] = (
    "T2M",
    "T2M_MAX",
    "T2M_MIN",
    "RH2M",
    "ALLSKY_SFC_SW_DWN",
)

HOURLY_PARAMETERS: tuple[str, ...] = (
    "T2M",
    "RH2M",
    "ALLSKY_SFC_SW_DWN",
)


class NasaPowerClient:
    """REST API client for NASA POWER agroclimatology & renewable energy data."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        daily_endpoint: str = DEFAULT_DAILY_ENDPOINT,
        hourly_endpoint: str = DEFAULT_HOURLY_ENDPOINT,
        timeout: float = 60.0,
    ) -> None:
        self.daily_endpoint = daily_endpoint
        self.hourly_endpoint = hourly_endpoint
        self.timeout = timeout

    def fetch_daily(
        self,
        latitude: float = BATAC_LATITUDE,
        longitude: float = BATAC_LONGITUDE,
        start_date: str = "20190101",
        end_date: str = "20191231",
        parameters: Sequence[str] = DAILY_PARAMETERS,
        community: str = "RE",
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Fetch daily time-series from NASA POWER API.

        Args:
            latitude: Target latitude in decimal degrees.
            longitude: Target longitude in decimal degrees.
            start_date: Start date string formatted as 'YYYYMMDD'.
            end_date: End date string formatted as 'YYYYMMDD'.
            parameters: Meteorological parameters to query.
            community: Community code ('RE' for Renewable Energy).

        Returns:
            Decoded JSON dictionary from NASA POWER response.
        """
        params = {
            "parameters": ",".join(parameters),
            "community": community,
            "longitude": f"{longitude:.4f}",
            "latitude": f"{latitude:.4f}",
            "start": start_date,
            "end": end_date,
            "format": "JSON",
        }
        url = f"{self.daily_endpoint}?{urllib.parse.urlencode(params)}"
        logger.info("Requesting NASA POWER daily data from: %s", url)
        return self._send_request(url)

    def fetch_hourly(
        self,
        latitude: float = BATAC_LATITUDE,
        longitude: float = BATAC_LONGITUDE,
        start_date: str = "20190101",
        end_date: str = "20191231",
        parameters: Sequence[str] = HOURLY_PARAMETERS,
        community: str = "RE",
        time_standard: str = "LST",
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Fetch hourly time-series from NASA POWER API.

        Args:
            latitude: Target latitude in decimal degrees.
            longitude: Target longitude in decimal degrees.
            start_date: Start date string formatted as 'YYYYMMDD'.
            end_date: End date string formatted as 'YYYYMMDD'.
            parameters: Meteorological parameters to query.
            community: Community code ('RE' for Renewable Energy).
            time_standard: Time standard ('LST' or 'UTC').

        Returns:
            Decoded JSON dictionary from NASA POWER response.
        """
        params = {
            "parameters": ",".join(parameters),
            "community": community,
            "longitude": f"{longitude:.4f}",
            "latitude": f"{latitude:.4f}",
            "start": start_date,
            "end": end_date,
            "time-standard": time_standard,
            "format": "JSON",
        }
        url = f"{self.hourly_endpoint}?{urllib.parse.urlencode(params)}"
        logger.info("Requesting NASA POWER hourly data from: %s", url)
        return self._send_request(url)

    def fetch_annual_hourly(
        self,
        year: int,
        latitude: float = BATAC_LATITUDE,
        longitude: float = BATAC_LONGITUDE,
        parameters: Sequence[str] = HOURLY_PARAMETERS,
        community: str = "RE",
        time_standard: str = "LST",
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Fetch hourly series spanning a full annual calendar cycle for Batac.

        When time_standard is 'LST' (the NASA POWER default), hours are indexed in
        Local Solar Time. For Batac (120.56°E, ~0.56° from the 120°E PST central meridian),
        LST is within 2.2 minutes of Philippine Standard Time (PST = UTC+8). The query
        runs from YYYY0101 to YYYY1231.

        When time_standard is 'UTC', Jan 1 00:00 PST corresponds to Dec 31 16:00 UTC,
        so the query spans from (YYYY-1)1231 to YYYY1231.
        """
        if time_standard.upper() == "UTC":
            start_date = f"{year - 1}1231"
            end_date = f"{year}1231"
        else:
            start_date = f"{year}0101"
            end_date = f"{year}1231"

        return self.fetch_hourly(
            latitude=latitude,
            longitude=longitude,
            start_date=start_date,
            end_date=end_date,
            parameters=parameters,
            community=community,
            time_standard=time_standard,
        )

    def _send_request(self, url: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Execute HTTP request with custom User-Agent and JSON decoding."""
        req = urllib.request.Request(
            url,
            headers={
                "User-Agent": "ccis-heat-load-backup-sim/0.1.0 (Batac CCIS M&S Project)",
                "Accept": "application/json",
            },
        )
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=self.timeout) as resp:
                content = resp.read().decode("utf-8")
                return json.loads(content)
        except urllib.error.URLError as exc:
            logger.error("Failed to fetch data from NASA POWER: %s", exc)
            raise ConnectionError(f"NASA POWER API request failed: {exc}") from exc

    @staticmethod
    def save_raw(data: dict[str, Any], filepath: str | Path) -> Path:
        """Persist raw API JSON response to disk."""
        path = Path(filepath)
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(data, f, indent=2)
        logger.info("Saved raw NASA POWER payload to %s", path)
        return path

    @staticmethod
    def load_raw(filepath: str | Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Load raw API JSON response from disk (offline fallback)."""
        path = Path(filepath)
        if not path.exists():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Raw weather file not found at: {path}")
        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            return json.load(f)
```

### `src/ccis_sim/weather/disaggregator.py`

*209 lines. Daily-to-hourly weather disaggregation engine.*

```python
"""Daily-to-hourly weather disaggregation engine.

Implements physically-grounded disaggregation of daily NASA POWER meteorological
aggregates (T2M, T2M_MAX, T2M_MIN, RH2M, ALLSKY_SFC_SW_DWN) to hourly time series:
1. Solar Irradiance: Astronomical solar geometry model (declination, sunset hour angle)
   with half-sine daytime distribution, normalized for strict daily energy conservation.
2. Ambient Temperature: Diurnal sinusoidal profile anchored to T2M_MIN at sunrise
   and T2M_MAX at peak afternoon insolation (~14:00 PST).
3. Relative Humidity: Tetens saturation vapor pressure model assuming constant
   daily vapor pressure, yielding inverse diurnal coupling with temperature.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from typing import Any

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

BATAC_LATITUDE = 18.06   # degrees North
BATAC_LONGITUDE = 120.56 # degrees East
PST_OFFSET = timezone(timedelta(hours=8))


class DailyDisaggregator:
    """Disaggregates daily weather summaries into 24-hour diurnal profiles."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        latitude: float = BATAC_LATITUDE,
        longitude: float = BATAC_LONGITUDE,
        peak_temp_hour: float = 14.0,
    ) -> None:
        self.latitude = latitude
        self.longitude = longitude
        self.peak_temp_hour = peak_temp_hour

    @staticmethod
    def _saturation_vapor_pressure(temp_c: float | np.ndarray) -> float | np.ndarray:
        """Tetens equation for saturation vapor pressure (kPa) over liquid water."""
        return 0.61078 * np.exp((17.27 * temp_c) / (temp_c + 237.3))

    def _solar_geometry(self, day_of_year: int) -> tuple[float, float]:
        """Compute approximate sunrise and sunset solar times (hours from midnight).

        Args:
            day_of_year: Day of year (1 to 365/366).

        Returns:
            Tuple of (sunrise_hour, sunset_hour) in local solar/standard time.
        """
        phi = np.radians(self.latitude)
        # Cooper's equation for solar declination
        delta = np.radians(23.45 * np.sin(np.radians(360.0 / 365.0 * (284 + day_of_year))))

        # Sunset hour angle
        cos_omega_s = -np.tan(phi) * np.tan(delta)
        cos_omega_s = np.clip(cos_omega_s, -1.0, 1.0)
        omega_s_deg = np.degrees(np.arccos(cos_omega_s))

        half_day_hours = omega_s_deg / 15.0
        sunrise = 12.0 - half_day_hours
        sunset = 12.0 + half_day_hours
        return float(sunrise), float(sunset)

    def disaggregate_day(
        self,
        date: datetime,
        t_mean: float,
        t_max: float,
        t_min: float,
        rh_mean: float,
        ghi_daily_kwh: float,
    ) -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:
        """Disaggregate a single day's parameters into 24 hourly values.

        Args:
            date: Calendar date of the observation.
            t_mean: Daily mean temperature (°C).
            t_max: Daily maximum temperature (°C).
            t_min: Daily minimum temperature (°C).
            rh_mean: Daily mean relative humidity (%).
            ghi_daily_kwh: Daily global horizontal insolation (kWh/m^2/day).

        Returns:
            Dictionary with 24-element arrays:
            {'t_amb': ndarray, 'rh': ndarray, 'ghi': ndarray}
        """
        day_of_year = date.timetuple().tm_yday
        h_sr, h_ss = self._solar_geometry(day_of_year)

        # Mid-hour times: 0.5, 1.5, ..., 23.5
        hours = np.arange(24, dtype=float) + 0.5

        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        # 1. Solar Irradiance (GHI) Disaggregation
        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        # Half-sine distribution between sunrise and sunset
        weights = np.zeros(24, dtype=float)
        for i, h in enumerate(hours):
            if h_sr < h < h_ss:
                weights[i] = np.sin(np.pi * (h - h_sr) / (h_ss - h_sr))

        w_sum = np.sum(weights)
        if w_sum > 0.0 and ghi_daily_kwh > 0.0:
            ghi_hourly = (ghi_daily_kwh * 1000.0 / w_sum) * weights
        else:
            ghi_hourly = np.zeros(24, dtype=float)

        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        # 2. Ambient Temperature (t_amb) Disaggregation
        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        t_amb = np.zeros(24, dtype=float)
        h_max = self.peak_temp_hour

        if t_max < t_min:
            t_max, t_min = t_min, t_max

        delta_t = t_max - t_min

        for i, h in enumerate(hours):
            if h <= h_sr:
                t_effective = h + 24.0
                decay_duration = 24.0 - (h_max - h_sr)
                fraction = (t_effective - h_max) / decay_duration
                t_amb[i] = t_min + delta_t * 0.5 * (1.0 + np.cos(np.pi * fraction))
            elif h <= h_max:
                fraction = (h - h_sr) / (h_max - h_sr)
                t_amb[i] = t_min + delta_t * np.sin(np.pi * 0.5 * fraction)
            else:
                decay_duration = 24.0 - (h_max - h_sr)
                fraction = (h - h_max) / decay_duration
                t_amb[i] = t_min + delta_t * 0.5 * (1.0 + np.cos(np.pi * fraction))

        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        # 3. Relative Humidity (rh) Disaggregation
        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        e_s_mean = self._saturation_vapor_pressure(t_mean)
        actual_vapor_pressure = (np.clip(rh_mean, 0.0, 100.0) / 100.0) * e_s_mean
        e_s_hourly = self._saturation_vapor_pressure(t_amb)
        rh_hourly = 100.0 * (actual_vapor_pressure / e_s_hourly)
        rh_hourly = np.clip(rh_hourly, 5.0, 100.0)

        return {
            "t_amb": np.round(t_amb, 2),
            "rh": np.round(rh_hourly, 2),
            "ghi": np.round(ghi_hourly, 2),
        }

    def disaggregate_dataframe(self, daily_df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Disaggregate a daily weather DataFrame to a full hourly DataFrame.

        Expected daily_df columns:
            ['t_mean', 't_max', 't_min', 'rh_mean', 'ghi_daily_kwh']
        Index: DatetimeIndex of daily dates.

        Returns:
            pd.DataFrame with PST hourly index and columns:
            ['hour', 'timestamp', 't_amb', 'rh', 'ghi'].
        """
        required_cols = {"t_mean", "t_max", "t_min", "rh_mean", "ghi_daily_kwh"}
        missing = required_cols - set(daily_df.columns)
        if missing:
            raise ValueError(f"Daily DataFrame missing required columns: {missing}")

        all_records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        hour_counter = 1

        for date_idx, row in daily_df.iterrows():
            if isinstance(date_idx, pd.Timestamp):
                current_date = date_idx.to_pydatetime()
            else:
                current_date = pd.to_datetime(date_idx).to_pydatetime()

            hourly_data = self.disaggregate_day(
                date=current_date,
                t_mean=float(row["t_mean"]),
                t_max=float(row["t_max"]),
                t_min=float(row["t_min"]),
                rh_mean=float(row["rh_mean"]),
                ghi_daily_kwh=float(row["ghi_daily_kwh"]),
            )

            for h in range(24):
                hour_dt = datetime(
                    current_date.year,
                    current_date.month,
                    current_date.day,
                    h,
                    0,
                    0,
                    tzinfo=PST_OFFSET,
                )
                all_records.append(
                    {
                        "hour": hour_counter,
                        "timestamp": hour_dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S+08:00"),
                        "t_amb": hourly_data["t_amb"][h],
                        "rh": hourly_data["rh"][h],
                        "ghi": hourly_data["ghi"][h],
                    }
                )
                hour_counter += 1

        return pd.DataFrame(all_records)
```

### `src/ccis_sim/weather/loader.py`

*78 lines. Loader utility for cached offline weather CSV fixtures.*

```python
"""Loader utility for cached offline weather CSV fixtures."""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from pathlib import Path

import pandas as pd

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

REQUIRED_COLUMNS = ("hour", "timestamp", "t_amb", "rh", "ghi")


def _find_fixture_path(year_or_path: int | str | Path) -> Path:
    """Resolve year integer or filename path to an absolute Path."""
    if isinstance(year_or_path, int) or (isinstance(year_or_path, str) and year_or_path.isdigit()):
        year = int(year_or_path)
        filename = f"batac-weather-hourly-{year}.csv"
        # Check standard relative paths
        candidates = [
            Path("data/fixtures") / filename,
            Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3] / "data" / "fixtures" / filename,
        ]
        for c in candidates:
            if c.exists():
                return c.resolve()
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Fixture for year {year} not found. Searched: {candidates}")

    path = Path(year_or_path)
    if not path.exists():
        repo_candidate = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3] / path
        if repo_candidate.exists():
            return repo_candidate.resolve()
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Weather fixture file not found: {path}")
    return path.resolve()


def load_weather_fixture(
    year_or_path: int | str | Path,
    expected_rows: int = 8760,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Load and validate an offline hourly weather fixture CSV.

    Args:
        year_or_path: Calendar year (e.g., 2016, 2019) or path to CSV file.
        expected_rows: Expected row count (default 8,760 for annual simulation).

    Returns:
        pd.DataFrame containing verified weather columns:
        ['hour', 'timestamp', 't_amb', 'rh', 'ghi'].
    """
    path = _find_fixture_path(year_or_path)
    logger.info("Loading weather fixture from: %s", path)

    df = pd.read_csv(path)

    missing_cols = set(REQUIRED_COLUMNS) - set(df.columns)
    if missing_cols:
        raise ValueError(f"Weather fixture {path.name} missing required columns: {missing_cols}")

    if expected_rows is not None and len(df) != expected_rows:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Weather fixture {path.name} contains {len(df)} rows, expected {expected_rows}."
        )

    for col in ("t_amb", "rh", "ghi"):
        if df[col].isna().any():
            raise ValueError(f"Weather fixture {path.name} contains NaN in column '{col}'.")
        if (df[col] == -999.0).any():
            raise ValueError(f"Weather fixture {path.name} contains un-sanitized sentinel -999 in '{col}'.")

    df["hour"] = df["hour"].astype(int)
    df["t_amb"] = df["t_amb"].astype(float)
    df["rh"] = df["rh"].astype(float)
    df["ghi"] = df["ghi"].astype(float)

    return df
```

### `src/ccis_sim/weather/parser.py`

*126 lines. Parser for NASA POWER meteorological JSON and CSV payloads.*

```python
"""Parser for NASA POWER meteorological JSON and CSV payloads.

Transforms raw NASA POWER responses into pandas DataFrames with
validated types, canonical parameter mappings, and timezone awareness.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
from io import StringIO
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import pandas as pd

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# Philippine Standard Time is UTC+8
PST_OFFSET = timezone(timedelta(hours=8))

# Canonical mapping for hourly parameters
HOURLY_RENAME_MAP: dict[str, str] = {
    "T2M": "t_amb",
    "RH2M": "rh",
    "ALLSKY_SFC_SW_DWN": "ghi",
}

# Canonical mapping for daily parameters
DAILY_RENAME_MAP: dict[str, str] = {
    "T2M": "t_mean",
    "T2M_MAX": "t_max",
    "T2M_MIN": "t_min",
    "RH2M": "rh_mean",
    "ALLSKY_SFC_SW_DWN": "ghi_daily_kwh",
}


class WeatherParser:
    """Parses raw NASA POWER responses into structured DataFrames."""

    @staticmethod
    def parse_hourly_json(data: dict[str, Any]) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Parse raw NASA POWER hourly JSON response.

        NASA POWER hourly JSON has format:
            data["properties"]["parameter"][param_name]["YYYYMMDDHH"] = float_val
        Header defines the time standard:
            data["header"]["time_standard"] -> 'LST' (default) or 'UTC'

        Returns:
            pd.DataFrame with timezone-aware DatetimeIndex (PST if LST, UTC if UTC)
            and columns: ['t_amb', 'rh', 'ghi'].
        """
        if "properties" not in data or "parameter" not in data["properties"]:
            raise ValueError("Malformed NASA POWER JSON: missing 'properties.parameter' key.")

        header = data.get("header", {})
        time_standard = header.get("time_standard", "LST").upper()

        param_dict = data["properties"]["parameter"]
        df_dict: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}

        for param_key, series_data in param_dict.items():
            canonical_name = HOURLY_RENAME_MAP.get(param_key, param_key.lower())
            df_dict[canonical_name] = series_data

        df = pd.DataFrame(df_dict)

        # Parse timestamp index formatted as YYYYMMDDHH
        # If time_standard is LST, Batac (120.56°E) is local standard time (PST = UTC+8)
        raw_dt = pd.to_datetime(df.index, format="%Y%m%d%H")
        if time_standard == "UTC":
            df.index = raw_dt.tz_localize("UTC")
            df.index.name = "timestamp_utc"
        else:
            # LST for Batac (120.56°E, ~0.56° from 120°E central meridian) is localized to PST
            df.index = raw_dt.tz_localize(PST_OFFSET)
            df.index.name = "timestamp_pst"

        df = df.sort_index()

        # Enforce numeric float dtype
        for col in df.columns:
            df[col] = pd.to_numeric(df[col], errors="coerce")

        return df

    @staticmethod
    def parse_daily_json(data: dict[str, Any]) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Parse raw NASA POWER daily JSON response.

        NASA POWER daily JSON has format:
            data["properties"]["parameter"][param_name]["YYYYMMDD"] = float_val

        Returns:
            pd.DataFrame with DatetimeIndex and daily columns:
            ['t_mean', 't_max', 't_min', 'rh_mean', 'ghi_daily_kwh'].
        """
        if "properties" not in data or "parameter" not in data["properties"]:
            raise ValueError("Malformed NASA POWER JSON: missing 'properties.parameter' key.")

        param_dict = data["properties"]["parameter"]
        df_dict: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}

        for param_key, series_data in param_dict.items():
            canonical_name = DAILY_RENAME_MAP.get(param_key, param_key.lower())
            df_dict[canonical_name] = series_data

        df = pd.DataFrame(df_dict)
        df.index = pd.to_datetime(df.index, format="%Y%m%d")
        df.index.name = "date"
        df = df.sort_index()

        for col in df.columns:
            df[col] = pd.to_numeric(df[col], errors="coerce")

        return df

    @staticmethod
    def parse_csv(filepath_or_buffer: str | Path | StringIO) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Parse CSV weather fixture file into a pandas DataFrame."""
        df = pd.read_csv(filepath_or_buffer)
        if "timestamp" in df.columns:
            df["timestamp"] = pd.to_datetime(df["timestamp"])
        return df
```

### `src/ccis_sim/weather/sanitizer.py`

*190 lines. Weather data sanitizer for missing-value filtering, timezone alignment, and validation.*

```python
"""Weather data sanitizer for missing-value filtering, timezone alignment, and validation.

Handles:
- NASA POWER missing value sentinel (-999.0) replacement and interpolation.
- Timezone alignment to Philippine Standard Time (PST = UTC+8).
- Psychrometric boundary sanity checks (temperatures, humidity, solar irradiance).
- Annual 8,760-hour standardization (dropping Feb 29 in leap years per TMY/ASHRAE convention).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from datetime import timezone, timedelta

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# Philippine Standard Time is UTC+8
PST_OFFSET = timezone(timedelta(hours=8))
PST_TIMEZONE_STR = "+08:00"

SENTINEL_FILL_VALUE = -999.0

# Physical plausibility boundaries for Batac, Ilocos Norte
BOUNDS_T_AMB = (-10.0, 55.0)  # °C
BOUNDS_RH = (0.0, 100.0)      # %
BOUNDS_GHI = (0.0, 1500.0)    # W/m^2


class PsychrometricValidationError(ValueError):
    """Raised when weather parameters violate physical plausibility bounds."""
    pass


class WeatherSanitizer:
    """Sanitizes raw weather time-series for simulation consumption."""

    @staticmethod
    def filter_sentinels(df: pd.DataFrame, sentinel: float = SENTINEL_FILL_VALUE) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Replace NASA POWER missing-value sentinels (-999.0) with np.nan."""
        clean_df = df.copy()
        for col in clean_df.columns:
            if pd.api.types.is_numeric_dtype(clean_df[col]):
                is_sentinel = np.isclose(clean_df[col], sentinel, atol=1e-3)
                clean_df.loc[is_sentinel, col] = np.nan
        return clean_df

    @staticmethod
    def interpolate_missing(
        df: pd.DataFrame,
        limit: int = 24,
        method: str = "time",
    ) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Interpolate missing NaN values linearly with forward/backward boundary fill.

        Args:
            df: DataFrame with DatetimeIndex and numeric columns.
            limit: Maximum consecutive hours to interpolate linearly.
            method: Interpolation method (default 'time').

        Returns:
            Fully populated DataFrame with zero NaNs.
        """
        clean_df = df.copy()
        if isinstance(clean_df.index, pd.DatetimeIndex):
            clean_df = clean_df.interpolate(method="time", limit=limit)
        else:
            clean_df = clean_df.interpolate(method="linear", limit=limit)

        clean_df = clean_df.bfill().ffill()

        nan_count = clean_df.isna().sum().sum()
        if nan_count > 0:
            raise ValueError(f"Interpolation incomplete: {nan_count} NaN values remain in dataset.")

        return clean_df

    @staticmethod
    def align_to_pst(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Align DatetimeIndex to Philippine Standard Time (PST = UTC+8).

        If the index is in UTC, it is converted to PST (+8 hours).
        If the index is naive or in LST (already local time for Batac 120.56°E),
        it is localized to PST (+08:00).
        Adds canonical ISO-8601 string column 'timestamp' (YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS+08:00).
        """
        clean_df = df.copy()

        if isinstance(clean_df.index, pd.DatetimeIndex):
            if clean_df.index.tz is None:
                # Naive index: if named timestamp_utc, localize UTC then convert
                if clean_df.index.name == "timestamp_utc":
                    utc_idx = clean_df.index.tz_localize("UTC")
                    clean_df.index = utc_idx.tz_convert(PST_OFFSET)
                else:
                    # Default local time (LST) for Batac
                    clean_df.index = clean_df.index.tz_localize(PST_OFFSET)
            else:
                # Timezone-aware: convert directly to PST
                clean_df.index = clean_df.index.tz_convert(PST_OFFSET)
            clean_df.index.name = "timestamp_pst"
        elif "timestamp_utc" in clean_df.columns:
            utc_series = pd.to_datetime(clean_df["timestamp_utc"], utc=True)
            clean_df["timestamp_pst"] = utc_series.dt.tz_convert(PST_OFFSET)
            clean_df = clean_df.set_index("timestamp_pst")
        else:
            raise ValueError("DataFrame has neither a DatetimeIndex nor a 'timestamp_utc' column.")

        clean_df["timestamp"] = clean_df.index.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S+08:00")
        return clean_df

    @staticmethod
    def validate_psychrometrics(df: pd.DataFrame) -> None:
        """Check that meteorological values fall within physically plausible bounds."""
        if "t_amb" in df.columns:
            t_min, t_max = df["t_amb"].min(), df["t_amb"].max()
            if t_min < BOUNDS_T_AMB[0] or t_max > BOUNDS_T_AMB[1]:
                raise PsychrometricValidationError(
                    f"Ambient temperature out of bounds [{BOUNDS_T_AMB[0]}, {BOUNDS_T_AMB[1]}]: "
                    f"min={t_min:.2f}°C, max={t_max:.2f}°C"
                )

        if "rh" in df.columns:
            rh_min, rh_max = df["rh"].min(), df["rh"].max()
            if rh_min < BOUNDS_RH[0] - 1e-3 or rh_max > BOUNDS_RH[1] + 1e-3:
                raise PsychrometricValidationError(
                    f"Relative humidity out of bounds [{BOUNDS_RH[0]}, {BOUNDS_RH[1]}]: "
                    f"min={rh_min:.2f}%, max={rh_max:.2f}%"
                )

        if "ghi" in df.columns:
            ghi_min, ghi_max = df["ghi"].min(), df["ghi"].max()
            if ghi_min < BOUNDS_GHI[0] - 1e-3 or ghi_max > BOUNDS_GHI[1]:
                raise PsychrometricValidationError(
                    f"Solar irradiance GHI out of bounds [{BOUNDS_GHI[0]}, {BOUNDS_GHI[1]}]: "
                    f"min={ghi_min:.2f} W/m^2, max={ghi_max:.2f} W/m^2"
                )

    @classmethod
    def standardize_annual_hourly(
        cls,
        df: pd.DataFrame,
        target_year: int,
        drop_leap_day: bool = True,
    ) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Filter to target calendar year in PST, sanitize, and format as 8,760 hours.

        Args:
            df: Hourly DataFrame with PST DatetimeIndex.
            target_year: Calendar year (e.g., 2016, 2019).
            drop_leap_day: If True, omit Feb 29 rows in leap years to maintain
                the canonical 8,760-hour contract per [D-05].

        Returns:
            Canonical DataFrame with columns ['hour', 'timestamp', 't_amb', 'rh', 'ghi']
            containing exactly 8,760 rows indexed 1..8,760.
        """
        if not isinstance(df.index, pd.DatetimeIndex) or df.index.tz is None:
            raise ValueError("Input DataFrame must possess a timezone-aware PST DatetimeIndex.")

        year_mask = df.index.year == target_year
        year_df = df.loc[year_mask].copy()

        # Handle leap year
        is_leap = (target_year % 4 == 0 and (target_year % 100 != 0 or target_year % 400 == 0))
        if is_leap and drop_leap_day:
            leap_mask = ~((year_df.index.month == 2) & (year_df.index.day == 29))
            year_df = year_df.loc[leap_mask].copy()

        if len(year_df) != 8760:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Expected exactly 8,760 hours for standardized year {target_year}, "
                f"but found {len(year_df)} hours."
            )

        cls.validate_psychrometrics(year_df)

        result = pd.DataFrame(
            {
                "hour": np.arange(1, 8761, dtype=int),
                "timestamp": year_df.index.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S+08:00"),
                "t_amb": np.round(year_df["t_amb"].values, 2),
                "rh": np.round(np.clip(year_df["rh"].values, 0.0, 100.0), 2),
                "ghi": np.round(np.clip(year_df["ghi"].values, 0.0, None), 2),
            }
        )

        return result
```
